### [1] [Automating Formal Verification with Agent-Guided Tree Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27485)
*Leo Yao*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文评估了大语言模型在Lean中生成可验证代码（vericoding）的能力，发现非推理模型性能稳定，开源模型略有提升。通过引入mathlib搜索的智能体循环，GPT-5.4在423个规格上达到95.0%的准确率。进一步设计了两种树搜索方法（基于状态和基于上下文），其中上下文方法在中等难度规格上以更低token成本表现更优，而智能体基线在最高难度规格上仍具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证可以保证软件正确性，但成本高昂导致实际应用稀少。大语言模型有望加速这一过程，但当前对LLM驱动验证代码生成能力的评估尚不充分，需要系统性的评估和更高效的验证方法。

Method: 在现有vericoding-benchmark基准上评估多厂商模型（闭源和开源），引入含mathlib搜索的智能体循环，并设计两种树搜索策略：基于状态的编排器（在部分证明状态上分支）和基于上下文的编排器（在完整子智能体上下文上分支）。

Result: 非推理闭源模型性能稳定，开源模型略有提升；智能体循环显著提升性能，GPT-5.4在423个规格上达95.0%（K=50）；基于上下文的设计在中等难度规格上以更低token成本解决更多问题，智能体基线在最高难度规格上保持优势。

Conclusion: 搜索结构相比强智能体基线具有选择性优势，需要从现代代码中提取更具挑战性的基准来推动自动化形式化验证的进一步发展。

Abstract: Formal verification offers a path to provably correct software, but writing verified code remains expensive enough that the technique is rarely used in production. Recent large language models can accelerate this work, and recent benchmarks measure their ability to translate specifications into code and machine-checked proofs of correctness. This thesis evaluates the state of such LLM-driven verified-code generation ("vericoding") in Lean and develops search-based methods for improving verification performance. We first reproduce a subset of the vericoding-benchmark Lean leaderboard on a current cross-vendor model pool, finding that non-reasoning performance remains roughly steady on US closed-source models while open-weight models have slightly improved. We update the iterative methodology of vericoding-benchmark with an agentic loop equipped with mathlib search, finding that model performance greatly improves and scales with agent budget. GPT-5.4 nearly saturates the benchmark at 95.0% on 423 specs with $K=50$ LLM calls. We then design two agent-directed tree-search formulations: a state-based orchestrator that branches on partial-proof states, and a context-based orchestrator that branches on full subagent contexts. Compared against the agent baseline, the context-based design solves a wider range of intermediate-difficulty specs at lower token cost, while the agent baseline retains an advantage on the hardest specs, where uninterrupted iteration matters most. We conclude that search structure has selective advantages over a strong agent baseline, and that more challenging benchmarks drawn from modern code are important to measure and drive further progress in automated formal verification.
  Code available upon request by contacting the author at leoy@mit.edu.

</details>

### [2] [DeltaMCP: Incremental Regeneration via Spec-Aware Transformation for MCP servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28148)
*Aditya Pujara,Xiaogang Zhu,Hsiang-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出DeltaMCP工具，实现企业级MCP服务器的增量再生，仅更新受OpenAPI规范变更影响的工具部分，在保持版本一致性的同时降低开发者维护开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统如AutoMCP虽能自动化生成MCP服务器，但无法解决企业级API演进时MCP工具集需频繁重新生成的同步维护难题，开发者负担沉重。

Method: 设计DeltaMCP——一种规范感知的增量再生工具，通过检测OpenAPI规范变更，仅对受影响的MCP服务器工具进行选择性更新，而非全量重新生成。

Result: 以Azure REST API规范为数据集进行基准测试，相比全量生成方法，DeltaMCP在生成质量和系统性能上均表现更优，显著降低了开发者开销并提升了可维护性与版本一致性。

Conclusion: DeltaMCP为企业提供了一种可扩展的方案，用于维护与LLM系统交互的高保真、实时更新的MCP服务器基础设施。

Abstract: The rapid development of LLMs coupled with the introduction of Model Context Protocol (MCP) has revolutionized how intelligent agents interact with APIs through deterministic and structured methods \cite{ModelContextProtocolIntro2025}. While some existing systems like AutoMCP attempt to automate a previously completely manual process of generating MCP servers, they fail to address the recurring challenge of maintaining synchronization between evolving enterprise-level APIs and their corresponding MCP toolset implementation \cite{mastouri2025makingrestapisagentready}. This paper introduces DeltaMCP, a specification-aware, incremental regeneration tool for enterprise-grade MCP servers. DeltaMCP enables developers to only update the affected tooling of MCP servers, given a new release of it's corresponding service's OpenAPI specification. Using Azure REST API specifications as the evaluation dataset, DeltaMCP is benchmarked against baseline full generation methods on generation quality and system performance. The results demonstrate the reduction in developer overhead through DeltaMCP whilst improving maintainability and version consistency. This research offers a scalable approach for enterprises seeking to maintain high-fidelity, up-to-date MCP server infrastructures for LLM-based systems.

</details>


### [3] [GUI Agents for Continual Game Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28258)
*Yixu Huang,Bo Li,Na Li,Zhe Wang,Kaijie Chen,Haonan Ge,Qingyi Si,Yuanzhe Shen,Ruihan Yang,Guangjing Wang,Hongcheng Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出游戏生成需要“玩家”来评估和改进，通过两个GUI智能体角色——PlaytestArena（客观评估）和Play2Code（主观测试）——实现可游玩游戏的生成，Play2Code在八类游戏生成任务中达到66.8%的rubric通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成方法将游戏生成为一次性翻译任务（从提示到产物），无法检测交互层面的故障，导致生成的游戏不可游玩。作者认为评估和改进游戏生成需要引入“玩家”角色。

Method: 提出两个GUI智能体角色：(1) 客观评估器PlaytestArena——包含200个浏览器游戏生成任务（覆盖8种类型）及预期游戏行为rubric，由GUI智能体加载游戏并进行游玩评估；(2) 主观测试者Play2Code——游戏智能体与GUI智能体在共享记忆的持续循环中协作，将游戏生成转变为编码与游玩的对话。

Result: 前沿模型直接生成可游玩游戏困难；Play2Code达到66.8%的rubric通过率，比单次生成基线和agentic编码基线分别提高37.1和14.6个百分点。GUI测试者反馈比人类报告更可追溯，但具有类似人类测试者的特质性。

Conclusion: 游戏生成需要交互级评估，GUI智能体可作为有效的评估者和测试者；游戏测试可作为交互式代码生成的关键测试平台。

Abstract: Generating a game is not the same as making one that can be played. Despite advances in code generation, existing approaches treat game generation as one-shot translation from prompt to artifact, leaving interaction-level failures undetected. We argue that evaluating and improving game generation requires a player, and study two roles for graphical user interface (GUI) agents in this process: (1) as an objective evaluator, for which we introduce PlaytestArena, a new evaluation environment that pairs 200 browser-based game generation tasks across eight genres with rubrics of expected in-play behaviors, adjudicated by a GUI agent that loads each build in a browser and plays it; and (2) as a subjective playtester, for which we propose Play2Code, where a game agent and a GUI agent operate in a sustained loop with shared memory, turning game generation into a dialogue between coding and playing. Our experiments show that even frontier models struggle to generate playable games directly, while Play2Code achieves a 66.8\% rubric pass-rate, improving over single-pass and agentic-coding baselines by 37.1 and 14.6 points respectively. Further analysis shows that GUI playtester feedback is more traceable than a human report, yet idiosyncratic in ways reminiscent of human testers, establishing game playtesting as a critical testbed for interactive code generation. Our project website is available at https://continual-game-generation.vercel.app/.

</details>

### [4] [Personalized Observation Normalization for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Simulation Environments with Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27385)
*Yiran Pang,Zhen Ni,Xiangnan Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对联邦强化学习在异构环境中状态转移动态差异导致的输入分布不一致和参数更新不平衡问题，本文提出个性化观测归一化方法（PON），使每个智能体利用持续更新的运行均值和方差对原始状态输入进行局部归一化，并证明共享归一化参数无效，在异构MuJoCo任务中加速训练并取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FedRL在异构环境中因各智能体状态转移动态不同导致输入分布不一致和聚合时参数更新不平衡，影响全局策略训练效果。

Method: 提出个性化观测归一化（PON）方法，每个智能体使用持续更新的本地运行均值和方差对原始状态输入进行归一化，确保局部特征的尺度一致性且不干扰聚合过程，且不共享归一化参数。

Result: 在异构MuJoCo任务上，PON方法加速了训练收敛，并取得了优于基线方法的性能表现。

Conclusion: 个性化归一化策略有效解决了FedRL在异构环境中的输入分布不一致问题，共享归一化参数的方法不可行，PON为异构联邦强化学习提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning (FedRL) enables multiple agents to collaboratively train a global policy without sharing raw data, making it ideal for privacy-sensitive applications. However, FedRL faces challenges in heterogeneous environments where differing state-transition dynamics lead to non-identical input distributions and imbalanced parameter updates during aggregation. Therefore, this paper develops a personalized observation normalization (PON) method, allowing each agent to locally normalize raw state inputs using a continuously updated running mean and variance. This design ensures consistent scaling of local feature without overshadowing across agents during aggregation. Furthermore, we demonstrate that sharing normalization parameters across agents is ineffective due to the diverse local input distributions, which highlights the necessity of personalized statistics. Experiments on heterogeneous MuJoCo tasks show that our developed PON accelerates training and achieves superior performance compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [5] [IGADA-IoT: IoT Sensor Energy Optimization in Wireless Sensor Networks Driven by Automatic Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27397)
*Mingchun Sun,Rongqiang Zhao,Muhammad Abdul Munnaf,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出信息间隙引导的物联网传感器自动数据增强框架IGADA-IoT，通过分层多生成器协作与调度、信息间隙-模型性能联合评估闭环方法，解决现有单生成器、经验性增强决策的不足，在多个下游模型上平均准确率提升7.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据增强方法依赖单一生成器和经验确定的数据量，未能建立动态信息间隙与多生成器间的映射，忽略生成样本的异质性；同时缺乏联合考虑信息间隙与模型性能的评估与闭环方法。

Method: 提出IGADA-IoT框架，包含：(1) 分层多生成器协作与调度策略(HMGCS)，联合利用不同生成器能力以减小信息间隙；(2) 信息间隙-模型性能联合评估与闭环方法(IGMP-EC)，增强增强决策准确性并缓解欠增强与过增强风险。

Result: 多个下游模型平均准确率提升7.27%；相比先进数据增强方法提升8.67%；相比单一生成器提升7.24%；在UCR Archive与真实部署数据集上验证了准确性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 所提出的IGADA-IoT框架通过多生成器协同与闭环评估机制，有效提升了WSN中数据增强的针对性和决策准确性，实现了物联网传感器的能量优化。

Abstract: In wireless sensor networks (WSNs), data augmentation is a novel method to improve sampling-frequency decision performance, thereby enabling energy optimization for IoT (Internet of Things) sensors. However, existing methods rely on a single generator and empirically determined quantities, failing to establish a mapping between dynamic information gaps and multiple generators, and overlooking the heterogeneity of generated samples. Moreover, an evaluation and a closed-loop method that jointly considers the information gap and the model performance are lacking. To address these issues, we propose an information gap-guided IoT sensor automatic data augmentation framework (IGADA-IoT) with hierarchical multi-generator collaboration and scheduling over multiple rounds. Capabilities of different generators are jointly utilized to reduce the information gaps. In the IGADA-IoT, a hierarchical multi-generator collaboration and scheduling strategy (HMGCS) is proposed to enhance the targetedness and rationality of generated sample allocation. An information gap-model performance joint evaluation and closed-loop method (IGMP-EC) is proposed to enhance the accuracy of augmentation decisions, and to mitigate the risks of under-augmentation and over-augmentation. Experimental results show that the IGADA-IoT improves the average accuracy of multiple downstream models by 7.27%. Compared with advanced data augmentation methods, the average accuracy is improved by 8.67%. Compared with the individual generators, the average accuracy is improved by 7.24%. Furthermore, public IoT sensor datasets from the UCR Archive and real-world deployments demonstrate the accuracy and generalizability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [6] [Tackling Multimodal Learning Challenges with Mixture-of-Expert: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27431)
*Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Wei Emma Zhang,Olaf Maennel,Lin Yue,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇综述系统回答了“混合专家模型（MoE）如何有效解决多模态学习中的挑战”这一核心问题，从高效引擎、表示学习器和自适应器三个视角全面回顾MoE在多模态学习中的应用，并指出了可解释路由、专家通信、模态集成和终身多模态学习等关键研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述独立评价多模态学习或MoE的方法分类，忽视了二者之间的独特交互作用。本文旨在填补这一空白，回答MoE如何有效解决多模态挑战的核心问题。

Method: 从三个关键视角进行分析：（1）MoE作为高效多模态引擎——通过解耦计算成本与参数增长、选择性专家激活减少模态冗余；（2）MoE作为多模态表示学习器——整合互补的多专家知识以丰富对齐与交互表示；（3）MoE作为多模态自适应器——提供模块化灵活机制建模模态不平衡和缺失模态等不完美数据场景。

Result: 通过广泛文献回顾，识别出可解释路由、专家通信、模态集成和终身多模态学习等关键研究空白。

Conclusion: 本文定位为未来研究的基础，推动构建可解释且可持续的多模态Mixture-of-Experts系统。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) presents a naturally compatible and scalable framework for multimodal learning, demonstrating strong adaptability across diverse modalities and tasks. Despite its growing success, a comprehensive and systematic review on the MoE metho addressing multimodal challenges remains lacking. Existing surveys tend to evaluate either multimodal learning or MoE independently from method taxonomy, overlooking the unique interplay between them. This survey fills that gap by answering a central question: \textit{How does MoE effectively resolve multimodal challenges?} We approach this from three key perspectives: (1) \textbf{MoE as an Efficient Multimodal Engine:} enabling scalable multimodal modeling by decoupling computational cost from parameter growth and mitigating modality redundancy through selective expert activation; (2) \textbf{MoE as a Multimodal Representation Learner:} integrating complementary multi-opinion expert knowledge to enrich alignment and interaction representations; and (3) \textbf{MoE as a Multimodal Adapter:} providing a modular and flexible mechanism to model imperfect data scenarios such as modality imbalance and missing modality. Through our extensive literature review, we identify critical research gaps, including interpretable routing, expert communication, modality integration, and lifelong multimodal learning. We position this survey as a foundation for future research toward interpretable and sustainable multimodal Mixture-of-Experts system.

</details>


### [7] [Detect by Yourself: Self-Designing Agentic Workflows for Few-Shot Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27470)
*Tairan Huang,Qiang Chen,Yili Wang,Yueyue Ma,Changlong He,Xiu Su,Yi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SignGAD框架，将图异常检测从固定检测器训练重构为任务条件化的检测工作流自设计，通过选择合适图编码和检测器设计来利用任务特定异常证据，并引入校准重拟合的守卫策略增强有限监督下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法面临两个关键挑战：1) 固定管线限制了在有限监督下跨不同图任务的适应性；2) 弱证据使其无法将上下文和结构异常信号显式融入检测过程。

Method: 1) 提出新范式，将图异常检测从训练固定检测器重构为设计任务条件化检测工作流；2) 通过构建检测工作流，选取合适的图编码和检测器设计以利用任务特定异常证据；3) 引入守卫式最终重拟合策略，通过校准重拟合接受度增强有限监督下的可靠性。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，SignGAD相比最先进方法取得了强劲性能，验证了在图异常检测任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: SignGAD通过自设计工作流范式有效解决了固定管线和弱证据两大挑战，在少样本图异常检测中展现了优越性能。

Abstract: Graph anomaly detection aims to identify anomaly nodes in attributed graphs and plays an important role in real-world applications. However, existing graph anomaly detection methods still face two key challenges: 1) fixed pipelines, which restrict their adaptability across different graph tasks under limited supervision; 2) weak evidence, which prevents them from explicitly incorporating contextual and structural anomaly signals into the detection process. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, self-designing agentic workflows for few-shot graph anomaly detection (SignGAD). Specifically, we propose a novel paradigm that reformulates graph anomaly detection task from training a fixed anomaly detector to designing task-conditioned detection workflows. By constructing detection workflows, SignGAD selects suitable graph encodings and detector designs to exploit task-specific anomaly evidence. Meanwhile, we introduce a guarded final refit strategy to refine the selected workflow by calibrating refit acceptance, enhancing reliability under limited supervision. Extensive experiments conducted on several real-world datasets demonstrate that SignGAD achieves strong performance against state-of-the-art methods, highlighting its effectiveness on graph anomaly detection tasks.

</details>


### [8] [Balancing Fidelity and Diversity in Diffusion Models via Symmetric Attention Decomposition: Hopfield Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27476)
*Hyunmin Cho,Woo Kyoung Han,Kyong Hwan Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对Transformer中pre-softmax注意力矩阵进行对称-反对称分解，将对称部分解释为控制能量景观结构，反对称部分驱动能量景观上的循环，并基于此推导Hopfield稳定性度量，发现其与生成中的保真度-多样性权衡相关，最终通过修改循环动力学实现了对该权衡的可控调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer注意力机制的内在运作原理，特别是注意力矩阵如何编码特征间的关联，以及如何利用这种理解来控制和调节生成模型中的保真度-多样性权衡。

Method: 将pre-softmax注意力矩阵QK^T分解为对称部分和反对称部分；对称部分被解释为能量景观的结构，反对称部分被解释为驱动循环的动力；基于能量公式推导Hopfield风格稳定性度量。

Result: 发现Hopfield风格稳定性度量与生成中的保真度-多样性权衡之间存在有意义的关联；提出了一种通过修改底层动力学循环来调节该权衡的可控方法。

Conclusion: 注意力矩阵的对称-反对称分解为理解Transformer的联想记忆行为提供了新的视角，并且能够通过调控循环动力学来实际控制生成质量与多样性之间的平衡。

Abstract: We characterize the pre-softmax attention matrix $\mathbf{QK^\top}$ in transformers as an associative memory matrix encoding pairwise associations between input features. By decomposing this matrix into its symmetric and skew-symmetric parts, we interpret the symmetric component as governing the structure of the energy landscape, and the skew-symmetric component as driving circulation on that landscape. Leveraging the energy formulation induced by the symmetric component, we derive Hopfield-style stability measures that quantify the stability of retrieved features. We observe meaningful correlations between Hopfield-style stability measures and the fidelity-diversity trade-offs in generation. Finally, we propose a controllable knob to modulate this trade-off by modifying the circulation of the underlying dynamics. Code is available at our GitHub (https://github.com/hyeon-cho/Attention-Symmetric-Decomposition).

</details>


### [9] [Energy-Structured Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27482)
*Longhua Li,Lei Qi,Qi Tian,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: E²-LoRA通过能量集中与排序的低秩适应方法，解决持续学习中正交子空间方法的知识扩散和容量耗尽问题，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交子空间方法在持续学习中虽能缓解任务干扰，但存在能量在基向量间扩散的问题，导致知识压缩困难且快速耗尽未来任务的容量。

Method: 理论证明参数更新引起的输出特征漂移本质上是低秩的，沿漂移主方向保留参数可最小化输出重建误差。据此提出E²-LoRA：1）显式排序并集中知识到主导秩；2）设计动态秩分配策略，联合优化能量保留与模型可塑性以平衡稳定性和可塑性。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的大量实验表明，E²-LoRA达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过能量集中与排序的低秩适应，E²-LoRA有效释放了后续任务的容量，平衡了稳定性和可塑性，在持续学习任务中表现优异。

Abstract: While orthogonal subspace methods try to mitigate task interference in Continual Learning (CL), they often suffer from energy diffusion across the basis, hindering knowledge compaction and exhausting capacity for future tasks. We observe that output feature drift induced by parameter updates is inherently low-rank, and theoretically prove that preserving parameters along the principal directions of this drift minimizes the output reconstruction error. Motivated by this, we propose \textbf{E}nergy-Concentrated and \textbf{E}nergy-Ordered \textbf{Lo}w-\textbf{R}ank \textbf{A}daptation (E$^2$-LoRA). By explicitly ordering and concentrating knowledge into leading ranks, E$^2$-LoRA frees capacity for subsequent tasks. Furthermore, we design a dynamic rank allocation strategy to balance stability and plasticity by jointly optimizing energy retention and model plasticity. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that E$^2$-LoRA achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [10] [Information-theoretic Multimodal Representation Learning for Electrocardiogram Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27583)
*Phu X. Nguyen,Konstantinos Kontras,Wei Dai,Huy Phan,Christos Chatzichristos,Paul Pu Liang,Bert Vandenberk,Maarten De Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MERIT是一个基于信息论的双分支预训练框架，通过联合掩码心电图建模与心电图-文本对比对齐，学习同时保留信号结构与临床语义的心电图表示，在多项基准测试上优于现有方法，尤其对细粒度临床应用效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态方法将心电图信号与临床报告对齐以融入诊断语义，但临床报告难以保留心电图波形丰富的生理结构信息，尤其是从粗粒度诊断类别到细粒度形态学的多层级抽象结构。作者从信息论视角出发，旨在学习同时保留信号结构并整合临床语义的心电图表示。

Method: 基于信息论推导出一个可处理的目标函数，提出MERIT双分支预训练框架，包含两个核心组件：掩码心电图建模（保留信号结构）和心电图-文本对比对齐（整合临床语义）。

Result: 在PTB-XL上All分类F1提升超3%、SubClass分类F1提升超5%；零样本评估中PTB-XL SubClass的AUC提升+2.66%、F1提升+2.11%；在多种分布偏移设置下表现鲁棒；用于大语言模型的心电图条件临床文本生成时，ROUGE和METEOR等指标均有提升。

Conclusion: MERIT学习了更具信息量且更具临床意义的心电图表示，尤其适用于细粒度临床应用场景。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) are widely used non-invasive measurements of cardiac activity and play a central role in clinical diagnosis. Recent multimodal approaches align ECG signals with clinical reports to incorporate diagnostic semantics, but clinical reports often fail to preserve the rich physiological structure of ECG waveforms, particularly across multiple levels of abstraction ranging from coarse diagnostic categories to fine-grained morphology. To address this limitation, we formulate ECG representation learning from an information-theoretic perspective and derive a tractable objective that jointly preserves signal structure and integrates clinical semantics. Based on this principle, we propose \textbf{MERIT} (Multimodal ECG Representation via Information Theory), a dual-branch pretraining framework combining masked ECG modeling with ECG--text contrastive alignment. Extensive experiments on PTB-XL and additional benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over prior methods, including gains exceeding $3%$ F1 on PTB-XL All and $5%$ F1 on SubClass classification. In zero-shot evaluation, MERIT further improves performance by up to $ +2.66\%$ AUC and $ +2.11\%$ F1 on PTB-XL SubClass, while also demonstrating robustness under multiple distribution-shift settings. Moreover, leveraging the learned ECG representations for ECG-conditioned clinical text generation with large language models improves text quality across several metrics, including ROUGE and METEOR. Together, these results demonstrate that MERIT learns more informative and clinically meaningful ECG representations, particularly for fine-grained clinical applications.

</details>


### [11] [Gradient Transformer: Learning to Generate Updates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27591)
*Binh-Nguyen Nguyen,Khang Tran,NhatHai Phan,Issa Khalil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于梯度变换的无数据知识蒸馏框架Grad-Transformer，通过将小模型在私有数据上微调的更新向量转换为大模型的更新向量，使资源受限的组织无需共享私有数据即可协作更新大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组织机构缺乏计算资源在私有（不可共享）数据上对大语言模型（LLMs）进行微调以获得更好的效用，而单独微调小语言模型（TinyLMs）效果不佳。

Method: 提出Grad-Transformer，通过学习TinyLM与LLM更新向量之间的相关性（从影子数据集推导），将TinyLM在私有数据上微调产生的更新向量转换为LLM的更新向量，支持多组织协作。

Result: 在语言建模和推理任务上，Grad-Transformer显著优于最先进的知识蒸馏基线方法，即使在严格差分隐私保护下也是如此。

Conclusion: 该框架使资源受限的组织无需访问或共享私有数据即可协作更新LLM，兼具性能提升与成本效益。

Abstract: Many organizations lack computational resources to fine-tune large language models (LLMs) on private (unshareable) data for better utility, while fine-tuning tiny language models (TinyLMs) alone performs poorly. To address this bottleneck, we propose a data-free knowledge distillation framework that generates LLM update vectors based on TinyLMs fine-tuned on private data. An update vector is a vector of parameter changes from an initial model to its fine-tuned version on a dataset, capturing the effect of cumulative gradient steps during fine-tuning. The key idea of our framework is a novel Gradient Transformer that transforms TinyLM's update vectors into LLM's update vectors. As derived from shadow datasets, Grad-Transformer captures the correlation between TinyLM and LLM update vectors, enabling third-party providers to generate LLM update vectors given the organization's TinyLM update vectors without accessing the organization's private data. The framework supports multi-organization collaboration to jointly update LLMs, improving performance and cost-efficiency. Extensive experiments across language modeling and reasoning tasks show that Grad-Transformer remarkably outperforms state-of-the-art knowledge distillation baselines, even under strict differential privacy protection.

</details>


### [12] [The Energy Blind Spot: NVIDIA's Flagship Edge AI Hardware Cannot Support Process-Level Energy Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27599)
*Deepak Panigrahy,Aakash Tyagi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对ASUS Ascent GX10（GB10 SoC）平台的能源可观测性审计发现，该平台仅暴露GPU瞬时功耗，CPU能耗完全不可观测，且英伟达明确表示无计划公开CPU轨信息，导致基于RAPL的每进程能耗归因无法复现。作者给出了硬件需求规范、校准桥梁方案及标准路径建议，呼吁低碳计算社区将能源可观测性作为首要硬件要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI工作负载在边缘部署场景下，编排结构主导能耗成本（比线性基线高4.33倍，多步推理任务高7.63倍），且CPU侧处理占总延迟90.6%、动态能耗44%。然而当前边缘AI硬件平台缺乏对CPU能耗的可观测性支持，无法准确归因、优化和控制碳足迹，存在严重的能效管理盲区。

Method: 对ASUS Ascent GX10（基于GB10 SoC）进行系统性能源可观测性审计，检查支持的软件接口（CPU计数器、INA电源轨监控、IPMI/BMC、SCMI powercap协议、NVML等）的暴露情况；发现MediaTek固件已通过未文档化的ACPI接口（SPBM）内部计算每轨能耗，但英伟达拒绝公开。随后提出硬件需求规范、基于外部直流计量与GPU扣减的标定桥接方案，以及通过SCMI powercap的标准化路径。

Result: 该平台仅暴露GPU瞬时功耗（通过NVML），CPU能耗完全不可观测，无CPU计数器、INA电源轨、IPMI/BMC或SCMI powercap接口。虽MediaTek固件内部有每轨能耗计算能力，但英伟达明确"无计划公开CPU轨信息"。基于RAPL的每进程能耗归因在x86上可行，但在此ARM架构平台上无法通过支持接口复现。

Conclusion: Agentic AI的边缘部署亟需CPU能耗可观测性，当前GB10平台存在严重的不透明性。应推动能源可观测性成为硬件一级需求，通过外部计量桥接过渡，最终通过SCMI powercap等标准化方案实现。

Abstract: Agentic AI workloads - where a single user goal triggers multi-step orchestration, tool calls, retries, and failure recovery - are being targeted for edge deployment, with NVIDIA, Dell, HP, ASUS, MSI, Acer, and Gigabyte all shipping GB10-based desktop AI systems in 2026. We recently demonstrated that orchestration structure dominates agentic energy cost, with workflows consuming 4.33x more energy per successful goal than linear baselines and OOI reaching 7.63x for multi-step reasoning tasks. Separately, Rajat et al. show that CPU-side processing accounts for up to 90.6% of total latency and 44% of total dynamic energy in agentic workloads. We report a systematic energy-observability audit of the ASUS Ascent GX10 (GB10 SoC) and find that the platform exposes no CPU energy counter, no INA power-rail monitor, no IPMI/BMC, and no SCMI powercap protocol through any supported software interface. The only on-device energy telemetry is instantaneous GPU power via NVML. We further discover that the MediaTek firmware already computes per-rail energy internally via an undocumented ACPI interface (SPBM), but NVIDIA states there are "no plans to expose CPU rail information." On-device per-process energy attribution - as performed on x86 via RAPL - is therefore not reproducible on this platform through supported interfaces. We formalize a hardware requirements specification for energy-attributed AI, propose an interim calibration bridge using external DC metering combined with GPU subtraction, and identify a standards-track path via SCMI powercap. Our findings motivate the low-carbon computing community to demand energy observability as a first-class hardware requirement.

</details>


### [13] [Evaluating Local Explainability Metrics for Machine Learning Models on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27618)
*Tomás Pereira,João Vitorino,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了局部可解释性技术（LIME、Kernel SHAP、特征消融）在复杂表格分类任务中的可信度，发现解释质量与模型预测性能并不总是相关，数据集复杂性和特征分布才是影响解释可靠性的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性技术广泛应用于理解AI行为，但其生成的解释可能不可靠，尤其在复杂表格数据中，解释看似合理却无法反映模型真实推理过程，因此需要系统评估局部可解释性技术的可信度。

Method: 在32个数据集上针对LIME、Kernel SHAP和特征消融三种局部可解释性技术进行基准测试，评估其忠实度、鲁棒性和复杂度三个属性。根据模型性能将样本分为共识正确组（所有模型预测正确）和共识错误组（所有模型预测错误），分析解释质量与模型性能的关系。

Result: 解释质量与模型预测性能并不总是相关；数据集复杂性和特征分布是影响解释质量和可靠性的主要因素。

Conclusion: 局部可解释性技术的可信度受数据集固有特性影响更大，而非模型本身的预测性能，提示在实际应用中需谨慎依赖解释结果。

Abstract: Despite the wide use of explainability techniques to attempt to understand the behavior of Artificial Intelligence (AI), the generated explanations may not always be reliable. An explanation can appear plausible to humans but fail to capture the internal reasoning of a model, particularly when dealing with complex tabular data. This paper studies the trustworthiness of local explainability techniques when applied to complex tabular classification tasks, considering evaluated metrics for three main properties: faithfulness to the model's predictions, robustness to input data variations, and complexity of the explanation itself. A benchmark was performed for Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME), Kernel SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), and Feature Ablation techniques, across 32 datasets and different types of machine learning models. Model performance ranges were analyzed to identify two groups: consensus-correct, which are samples that all models predicted correctly, and consensus-wrong, samples that all models predicted incorrectly. The obtained results demonstrate that that the explanations are not always correlated with a model's predictive performance. Instead, dataset complexity and feature distributions seem to be the main factors affecting explanation quality and reliability.

</details>


### [14] [Supervised Distributional Reduction via Optimal Transport and Dependence Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27619)
*Sai-Aakash Ramesh,Archit Sood,Andrew Corbett,Tim Dodwell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种监督式分布约简（SDR）算法，通过融合最优传输与显式依赖最大化，学习既保留数据几何结构又捕获目标任务相关信号的紧凑表示，并利用其诱导的非平稳几何构造自适应高斯过程核函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据约简中，平衡压缩与预测保真度是一个基本挑战。现有的分布约简方法（如联合聚类和降维）虽有理论原则，但其有监督变体尚未充分探索，而保留任务相关信号对于下游预测和决策至关重要。

Method: SDR算法基于Fused Gromov-Wasserstein（FGW）目标函数对齐输入分布的 relational structure 与代表性点集，同时引入显式依赖项最大化嵌入与目标信号之间的依赖性，从而学习目标感知的紧凑表示。

Result: SDR能够生成既反映几何结构又蕴含监督信息的紧凑表示，并自然地诱导出数据依赖的非平稳几何结构，可用于构建适应局部数据几何和监督变化的自适应核函数。

Conclusion: SDR为目标感知表示学习提供了一种基于最优传输的新视角，并在非平稳核设计方面展示了潜力，为高斯过程建模等领域提供了新工具。

Abstract: Learning representations that capture both intrinsic data geometry and target-relevant structure remains a fundamental challenge, particularly in settings where data reduction must balance compression with predictive fidelity. While distributional reduction-encompassing joint clustering and dimensionality reduction-offers a principled way to summarize data, its supervised variants remain relatively under-explored, despite the importance of retaining task-relevant signal for downstream prediction and decision-making. We propose Supervised Distributional Reduction (SDR), an algorithm for learning target-aware representations by combining optimal transport with explicit dependence maximization. SDR builds on the Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) objective to align the relational structure of the input distribution with a set of representative points, while augmenting it with a direct dependence term that encourages the learned embeddings to capture predictive signal more explicitly. This results in compact representations that reflect both geometric structure and supervision. Beyond representation learning, SDR naturally induces a data-dependent, non-stationary geometry that can be leveraged for settings such as Gaussian Process (GP) modelling. By redefining distances through target-aware distributional alignment, SDR enables the construction of adaptive kernels that respond to local variations in both data geometry and supervision, offering an optimal transport-based perspective on non-stationary kernel design.

</details>


### [15] [Transferable Reinforcement Learning via Probabilistic Latent Embeddings and Dynamic Policy Adaptation for Sim-to-Real Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27659)
*Gengyue Han,Yiheng Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于概率潜变量嵌入和动态策略适应的强化学习框架，用于安全高效地解决Sim2Real迁移问题，通过元学习推断环境上下文并通过分布强化学习动态调整风险水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本方法（鲁棒安全强化学习、域随机化）在应对未建模系统动态时，往往以性能下降或残余安全风险为代价，无法同时保证迁移的安全性和效率。

Method: 考虑一族不同环境上下文下的约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDPs），利用元强化学习中的潜上下文变量从模拟经验中推断环境表征，结合分布强化学习公式，根据潜上下文变量的估计精度动态调整部署策略的风险水平。

Result: 在部署初期提升安全性，并通过快速策略适应提高Sim2Real迁移效率。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够同时保证Sim2Real迁移的安全性和效率，克服了现有零样本方法的局限。

Abstract: Due to limited resources and public safety concerns, deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents for many cyber-physical systems (e.g., autonomous vehicles) are first trained in simulators. However, when deployed in real world environments, they often suffer from performance degradation or safety violations because of the inevitable Sim2Real gap. Existing zero-shot approaches, such as robust safe RL and domain randomization, mitigate this issue but typically at the cost of degraded performance or residual safety risks when experiencing unmodeled system dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework that enables safe and efficient policy transfer via probabilistic latent embeddings and dynamic policy adaptation. We consider a family of Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) under different environment contexts. By leveraging latent context variable in meta-RL, the proposed framework infers the latent representation of the environment from simulated experiences. Furthermore, it incorporates a distributional RL formulation, which allows risk levels of the deployed policy to be adjusted dynamically, based on the estimation accuracy of the latent context variable. This strategy promotes safety at the early deployment stage and improves efficiency through fast policy adaptation under the Sim2Real gap.

</details>


### [16] [Aligning LLMs with Human Uncertainty: A Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator for LLM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27668)
*Hui Dai,Ryan Teehan,Parsa Torabian,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Beta-Bernoulli校准器(BBC)，通过同时利用二元结果和人类预测信号，将任意模型的点估计预测转化为事件可能性的分布，实现更好的校准和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测方法通常仅从二元结果学习输出语言化预测，但聚合的人类预测中包含丰富的群体概率估计和预测者一致程度信息，如何利用这些信号尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出Beta-Bernoulli校准器(BBC)，建模事件可能性p服从Beta分布，结果y服从伯努利分布，将初始点估计转化为概率分布，均值作为校准后的点预测，方差作为 epistemic uncertainty。

Result: BBC在校准和准确性上优于传统事后校准方法和专门微调的预测模型，同时保持轻量级和良好泛化性；BBC捕获的epistemic uncertainty比语言化置信度更能可靠预测预测误差。

Conclusion: 利用人类群体预测中的分布信息（均值和一致程度）可以显著提升LLM概率预测的校准质量与可靠性。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting estimates the likelihood of uncertain future events. To improve LLM forecasting, existing methods typically learn from binary outcomes to output verbalized forecasts. However, while aggregated human forecasts contain rich information in both the crowd probability estimate and the degree of agreement among forecasters, how to utilize these signals remains underexplored. To address this, we propose the Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator (BBC), which converts an initial point estimate forecast from any model into a distribution over event likelihood, using supervision from both binary outcomes and human forecasts. BBC models event likelihood $p \sim \text{Beta}(α, β)$ and outcome $y \sim \text{Bernoulli}(p)$, with the mean as the calibrated point forecast and the variance as the epistemic uncertainty. Our results show that BBC generally provides better calibrated and more accurate forecasts than both traditional post-hoc calibration methods and models fine-tuned specifically for forecasting, while remaining lightweight and having good generalization. We also show that the epistemic uncertainty captured by BBC is a more reliable predictor of forecasting error than verbalized confidence.

</details>


### [17] [Heterogeneous Parallelism for Multimodal Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27678)
*Yashaswi Karnati,Kamran Jafari,Akash Mehra,Li Ding,Pranav Prashant Thombre,Ali Roshan Ghias,Shifang Xu,Parth Mannan,Yu Yao,Hao Wu,Eric Harper,Ashwath Aithal,Nima Tajbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多模态大语言模型训练的异构并行方法，允许端到端计算图中的不同模块使用独立的布局和排名放置，支持同地执行（共享GPU）和非同地执行（独立排名集）。通过边界通信器实现前向和反向布局变换，解决跨独立布局的张量语义一致性问题。实验表明，同地异构在TFLOPS/GPU上提升高达49.3%，非同地异构在聚合token吞吐量上提升13.0%，并验证了损失收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单一大语言模型中心的TP/CP/PP/DP/EP布局方案在多模态训练中导致编码器被迫继承LLM驱动的分片和放置策略，增加了通信开销、限制了编码器并行性，尤其在长上下文场景下，LLM需要上下文并行处理融合的多模态序列，而编码器输入仍受限，造成严重不匹配。

Method: 提出异构并行抽象，支持模块独立的布局和排名放置；设计边界通信器实现前向激活到目标布局的物化以及反向梯度回传；扩展调度机制支持同地与非同地两种放置模式。

Result: 同地异构配置下TFLOPS/GPU提升最高49.3%，非同地异构配置下聚合token吞吐量提升13.0%、TFLOPS/GPU提升9.6%，且与同构基线相比损失收敛一致。

Conclusion: 异构并行通过解耦模块的并行布局和放置，有效缓解了多模态训练中编码器与LLM之间的并行不匹配问题，在多种GPU规模和工作负载下找到更优的运行点。该系统已作为Megatron-LM的开源扩展发布。

Abstract: Foundation model training is becoming multimodal, from post-training pipelines to large-scale pretraining. As modality coverage broadens, context windows grow, and encoder LLM scales diverge, a single LLM-centric TP/CP/PP/DP/EP layout increasingly limits throughput. This coupling forces encoders to inherit LLM-driven sharding and placement choices that can add communication, limit encoder parallelism, or constrain the LLM schedule; the mismatch is most pronounced at long contexts, where LLM context parallelism is needed for the fused multimodal sequence but encoder inputs remain bounded. We present heterogeneous parallelism for multimodal large language model training, an abstraction that lets modules in one end-to-end graph use independent layouts and rank placements, supporting colocated execution on shared GPUs and non-colocated execution on disjoint rank sets. The key challenge is preserving boundary tensor semantics across independent layouts: forward activations must be materialized for the destination layout, while backward gradients must be routed back to the source layout. We address this with boundary communicators that implement forward and backward layout transforms, plus scheduling extensions for both placement modes. We evaluate optimized homogeneous, colocated heterogeneous, and non-colocated heterogeneous configurations across multimodal workloads and GPU scales to characterize when added layout and placement freedom exposes a better operating point. Across this sweep, colocated heterogeneity improves TFLOPS/GPU by up to 49.3%, while non-colocated heterogeneity improves aggregate token throughput by up to 13.0% and TFLOPS/GPU by up to 9.6%. We validate loss convergence parity against homogeneous baselines and release the system as an open-source Megatron-LM extension.

</details>


### [18] [Bayesian Deployment Approval for Learned Landing Controllers under Finite Rollout Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27720)
*Fei Jiang,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯批准框架，用于在有限仿真轨迹下评估学习型自主着陆控制器的部署就绪度。通过后验批准概率和后验部署风险等指标，结合序贯验证机制，解决了传统强化学习评估指标（如累计奖励和成功率）在有限验证数据下可能产生的过度自信问题。实验表明，该框架比传统RL评估方法能提供更符合不确定性校准的部署能力评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习评估使用累计奖励和经验成功率，但这类指标在有限仿真轨迹下无法提供充分的统计证据来支撑部署决策，容易产生过度自信的判断，缺乏对不确定性的量化考量。

Method: 1) 提出概率化着陆能力公式，基于不确定运行条件下触地安全满足度来定义；2) 利用贝叶斯后验推断量化学习策略真实部署能力的不确定性；3) 引入后验批准概率和后验部署风险作为部署导向评估指标；4) 建立序贯验证框架，支持渐进式测试中的批准/拒绝/继续决策。

Result: 使用PPO和SAC控制器的仿真实验表明，经验成功率和奖励优化在有限验证证据下可能产生过度自信的部署解读，而后验批准推断能提供更符合不确定性校准的部署就绪度评估。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了传统RL评估与面向部署的验证之间的统计联系，可推广至更广泛的学习型自主系统。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning and data-driven autonomous controllers are commonly evaluated using cumulative reward and empirical success frequency under finite simulation trajectories. However, such empirical metrics do not necessarily provide sufficient statistical evidence regarding deployment readiness under uncertainty. This work develops a Bayesian approval framework for learned autonomous landing controllers under finite rollout evidence. A probabilistic landing capability formulation is introduced based on touchdown safety satisfaction under uncertain operating conditions, while Bayesian posterior inference is used to quantify uncertainty regarding the true deployment capability of learned policies. Posterior approval probability and posterior deployment risk are further introduced for deployment-oriented evaluation, together with a sequential validation framework supporting approve/reject/continue decisions during progressive rollout testing. Simulation experiments using PPO and SAC controllers demonstrate that empirical success and reward optimization may produce overconfident deployment interpretation under limited validation evidence, whereas posterior approval inference provides a more uncertainty-calibrated assessment of deployment readiness. The proposed framework provides a practical statistical connection between conventional reinforcement-learning evaluation and deployment-oriented validation under uncertainty and may be generalized to broader classes of learned autonomous systems.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练中的损失尖峰通常由随机梯度噪声引起，尤其是语言数据中罕见表达经多层组合后产生重尾噪声，现有方法（如向量范数剪裁忽略矩阵结构，谱归一化成本过高）在结构与成本间存在权衡，需要一种平衡方案。

Method: 通过一阶扰动分析揭示重尾噪声的局部化特性，推导出高斯信号先验下贝叶斯最优条目级估计器的可计算替代形式，实现条目级剪裁以达到谱控制效果。

Result: 在柯西污染噪声下建立了$O(ε^{-4})$收敛保证；实验表明平滑收缩改进了NanoGPT上的Adam优化，节省约7%训练token；在Muon之前应用条目级剪裁可额外节省约2% token。

Conclusion: 条目级方法能够以较低计算成本实现类似谱归一化的稳定性效果，在理论和实践上均有效，可作为一种轻量级正则化或预处理手段与现有优化器结合使用。

Abstract: Training instabilities such as loss spikes are frequently the result of stochastic gradient noise. Because of rare expressions in language training data, and multiple layer composition, the noise impact is heavy-tailed and survives mini-batch averaging. Existing remedies trade off structure against cost: vector-norm clipping ignores the matrix structure of weight updates, while spectral normalization (e.g., Muon (Jordan et al., 2024)) respects it at additional cost. We show that this trade-off can be balanced. Real gradient noise appears to be similar to entry-wise heavy-tailed contamination, and a first-order perturbation analysis reveals a localization property of such noise, under which a simple entry-wise method achieves spectral control. Exploiting this, we derive a tractable surrogate for the Bayes-optimal entry-wise estimator under a Gaussian signal prior. We establish $O(ε^{-4})$ convergence guarantee under Cauchy-contaminated noise. Empirically, we find that smooth shrinkage improves Adam on NanoGPT pretraining, saving ${\sim}7\%$ of training tokens. We further find that applying the entry-wise clipping before spectral normalization yields a ${\sim}2\%$ token saving on top of Muon.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型安全评测通常将服务配置视为固定背景，但批次条件（单独、同步批处理、连续批处理调度）作为未测试的处理变量，可能影响同一提示的安全评估结果。

Result: 本地测试发现安全标签变更比能力标签变更更频繁（0.51% vs 0.14%），但裁决63个候选行后仅17个真实行为翻转，修正全集率为0.16%。15模型扩展未发现通用安全-能力偏斜（翻转比0.94x，p=0.942，η²=0.033），输出不稳定性是最强脆弱性筛查指标（r=0.909，95%CI [0.65, 0.97]）。核消融中标准vLLM重现22/55标签翻转，启用VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1后降至0/55；组合测试无聚合效应（4.7pp灵敏度）。

Conclusion: 建议采用精确栈验证：在服务批次设置下评估拒答行为，将安全提示与能力控制配对报告，并将低率定向翻转与聚合零效应分开报告。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of language models often treat serving configuration as fixed background infrastructure, but batch condition is an untested treatment variable whenever the same prompt may be evaluated alone, in a synchronized batch, or inside a continuous-batching scheduler. We synthesize four artifact-backed studies into a paired testing protocol: Study A combines local discovery, scorer-corrected adjudication, and true-batching confirmation; Study B tests cross-model generalization; Study C tests continuous-batch composition; and Study D runs a batch-invariant-kernel ablation. The local test finds safety-label changes more often than capability-label changes (0.51% vs. 0.14%), but adjudication of 63 candidate rows leaves only 17 genuine behavioral flips, implying a corrected full-set rate of 0.16%. The 15-model extension finds no detectable universal safety-over-capability skew: flips are near parity (0.94x), alignment type has no detectable association ($p=0.942$, $η^2=0.033$), and output instability is the strongest tested fragility screen ($r=0.909$, bootstrap 95% CI [0.65, 0.97]). In the targeted kernel ablation, standard vLLM reproduces 22/55 label flips on current score-flip candidates, while enabling VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1 reduces the same test to 0/55 flips; the composition test separately finds no aggregate effect at 4.7pp sensitivity. The testing recommendation is exact-stack validation: evaluate refusal at the served batch setting, pair safety prompts with capability controls, and report low-rate directional flips separately from aggregate null effects.

</details>

*Yvonne Zhou,Mingyu Liang,Ivan Brugere,Danial Dervovic,Yue Guo,Antigoni Polychroniadou,Min Wu,Dana Dachman-Soled*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对全同态加密（FHE）下的机器学习训练进行了理论收敛性分析，并结合了一种专为加密计算设计的差分隐私（DP）训练算法，在提高计算效率的同时实现了可比的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在FHE环境下训练机器学习模型面临计算瓶颈和隐私保护需求，现有的DP-GD方法因逐样本梯度裁剪而代价高昂，亟需一种兼具理论保证、计算高效且适用于加密计算的训练方法。

Method: 使用多项式逼近激活函数和损失函数以实现FHE兼容性；采用不依赖逐样本梯度裁剪的差分隐私集成方式；提出数据无关的超参数选择策略和多项式逼近的理论指导策略。

Result: 证明了基于多项式近似的近似梯度下降的收敛性；实现了比标准DP-GD更高的计算效率，同时保持可比的效用；提供了可独立使用的超参数和多项式逼近理论指导。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了在敏感数据上实现高效、隐私且安全的机器学习训练的可行性。

Abstract: We present the first theoretical convergence analysis of machine learning training under fully homomorphic encryption (FHE), combined with a differentially private (DP) training algorithm tailored to encrypted computation. Our approach improves computational efficiency over standard differentially private gradient descent (DP-GD) while achieving comparable utility. In particular, we prove convergence of approximate gradient descent using polynomial approximations of activation and loss functions, which are required for FHE compatibility. To preserve privacy in downstream tasks, we integrate differential privacy without relying on costly per-sample gradient clipping, enabling scalable encrypted learning. We also provide data-independent hyperparameter selection and theoretically grounded strategies for polynomial approximation which can be of independent interest. Together, these contributions advance the feasibility of efficient, private, and secure machine learning on sensitive data.

</details>


### [22] [Restoring the Sweet Spot: Pass-Rate Weighted Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27765)
*Zehao Liu,Yuanpu Cao,Jinghui Chen,Vasant G. Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SC-SDPO，通过引入基于$[\hat{p}(1-\hat{p})]^{1/2}$的问题级权重，为SDPO增加了难度感知能力，使其在科学推理和工具使用基准上一致优于原始SDPO，且计算开销为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDPO的KL-based优势缺乏隐式的难度感知能力，而GRPO的组相对优势天然能聚焦于中等难度问题。作者分析GRPO优势归一化机制，发现其能吸收方差项p(1-p)，平衡各问题的可学习性，从而指出SDPO需要引入类似的问题级难度权重。

Method: 将可学习性框架扩展到归一化奖励，证明归一化吸收了方差项p(1-p)，留下√[p(1-p)]作为每问题梯度的残差缩放因子。据此提出SC-SDPO，对每个问题的SDPO损失乘以权重√[p(1-p)]，其中p̂通过批自适应归一化的在线 rollout 零成本获得，形成隐式课程学习。

Result: 在科学推理和工具使用基准上，SC-SDPO在Qwen3-8B上提升+3.2(mean@16)/+4.3(maj@16)，在OLMo-3-7B上提升+1.8/+3.0，同时保持稳定的训练动态。

Conclusion: 通过零成本的尺度一致性修正，SC-SDPO弥补了SDPO在难度感知方面的缺陷，实现了对GRPO优势的等价补偿，是一种简洁有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) provides dense token-level credit assignment for reinforcement learning with large language models by leveraging the model's own feedback-conditioned predictions as a self-teacher. Unlike GRPO, however, whose group-relative advantage naturally concentrates learning on a sweet spot of intermediate-difficulty questions, SDPO's KL-based advantage lacks an implicit notion of difficulty awareness.
  We analyze this gap through the lens of GRPO's advantage normalization. Extending the learnability framework to normalized rewards, we show that normalization absorbs the variance term $p(1-p)$, equalizing leading-order learnability across questions and leaving $\sqrt{p(1-p)}$ as the sole residual scaling factor in the per-question gradient. This analysis yields a simple prescription: weight each question's SDPO loss by $[\hat{p}(1-\hat{p})]^{1/2}$, resulting in SC-SDPO, a scale-consistent variant of SDPO.
  The proposed weights are obtained as a zero-cost byproduct of on-policy rollouts with batch-adaptive normalization, inducing an implicit curriculum that dynamically tracks the model's evolving competence. Experiments on scientific reasoning and tool-use benchmarks demonstrate that SC-SDPO consistently improves over SDPO, yielding gains of +3.2/+4.3 (mean@16/maj@16) on Qwen3-8B and +1.8/+3.0 on OLMo-3-7B, while preserving stable training dynamics throughout optimization.

</details>


### [23] [Density-aware Sample-specific Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27809)
*Qiyuan Wang,Yao Li,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于密度比估计的双层优化后门攻击方法，通过将触发样本置于干净数据分布的低密度区域，在微调和剪枝防御下分别保持50-85%和100%的攻击成功率，暴露了现有防御范式的根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击方法在微调、剪枝等训练后防御面前容易被擦除后门，缺乏对最优触发样本构造的理论指导。

Method: 1) 理论推导出在贝叶斯最优受害者训练模型下，最优样本特定触发应满足将触发样本推向干净数据分布低密度区域；2) 提出双层优化框架，利用条件时间分数匹配估计密度比，并优化混合模型目标以实现该分布条件。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、GTSRB、TinyImageNet上，防御前ASR>99%；微调防御下ASR比最强基线高50-85个百分点；神经元剪枝防御下完全免疫（零神经元被移除）。

Conclusion: 现有防御范式存在根本性缺陷，迫切需要设计能够在干净分布支撑集之外起作用的防御机制。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in backdoor attacks, existing methods remain susceptible to post-training defenses that erase the backdoor through fine-tuning or pruning. We revisit the core objectives of backdoor attacks and derive principled criteria characterizing optimal sample-specific trigger construction under a Bayes-optimal model of the victim's training. Our analysis reveals that both attack success and clean-accuracy preservation are simultaneously optimized when triggered samples are steered into low-density regions of the clean data distribution, a distributional condition that controls all moments of the poisoned distribution at once rather than a handful of input-space summary statistics. We introduce a bilevel optimization framework that estimates density ratios via conditional time-score matching and optimizes a mixture-model objective to place triggered samples in these sparse regions. Extensive evaluations on MNIST, CIFAR-10, GTSRB, and TinyImageNet demonstrate that our method achieves above 99\% attack success rate before defense and retains 50--85 percentage points higher post-defense ASR than the strongest baselines under fine-tuning defenses. Against neuron-pruning defenses, the method exhibits complete immunity, with zero neurons identified for removal across all pruning thresholds. These results expose a fundamental gap in current defense paradigms and underscore the need for defenses that operate beyond the support of the clean distribution.

</details>


### [24] [Fine-Tuning Dynamics of In-Context Factual Recall in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27774)
*Ruomin Huang,Eshaan Nichani,Jason D. Lee,Rong Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入"上下文事实回忆(IC-recall)"任务，理论分析了Transformer如何利用存储在参数中的事实知识进行上下文学习，证明单层Transformer在少量样本微调后能收敛到特定的成对注意力模式，成功实现事实知识检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文学习的理论工作多假设模型仅从提示中提取信息，但实际应用中模型需要从参数中检索事实知识。本文旨在形式化研究上下文学习如何利用事实知识回忆这一重要但缺乏理论分析的机制。

Method: 定义了IC-recall任务：给定隐藏关系生成的(主语, 答案)对作为上下文，加上查询主语，模型需推断隐藏关系并检索答案。通过预构建的MLP联想记忆存储(主语, 关系, 答案)三元组建模事实知识。理论分析单层Transformer在IC-recall数据上的监督微调动力学，证明其收敛到特定成对注意力模式。

Result: 证明了模型成功执行IC-recall，微调所需样本量仅与知识三元组数量呈多对数关系（polylogarithmic）。实验验证了理论预测，并表明即使MLP层是预训练而非预构建的，成对注意力模式仍会涌现。

Conclusion: 本文为上下文学习中的事实知识回忆提供了理论基础，揭示了少量样本即可高效激活模型参数中存储的事实知识这一关键机制。

Abstract: In-context learning \ -- performing tasks based on examples given in the prompt \ -- is an important capability that has emerged in large language models and has received significant attention in both theory and practice. Existing theoretical work often focuses on settings where the learning uses information purely from the prompt. However, many practical instances of in-context learning require the model to retrieve factual knowledge stored in the model's parameters, with the context serving to identify which knowledge is relevant. In this work, we study how in-context learning leverages factual knowledge recall. We formalize this behavior by introducing the \emph{in-context factual recall (IC-recall)} task, where a transformer is provided a context of (subject, answer) pairs generated from a hidden relation, along with a query subject, and must both infer this hidden relation and retrieve the corresponding answer. Factual knowledge is modeled by the transformer having access to a simple pre-constructed MLP associative memory storing (subject, relation, answer) triplets. We analyze the supervised fine-tuning dynamics of a one-layer transformer on IC-recall data and prove that the model successfully performs IC-recall by converging to a particular pairwise attention pattern. This fine-tuning stage requires a very small number of samples \ -- only polylogarithmic in the number of stored knowledge triplets. Experiments verify our theoretical predictions and show that the pairwise attention pattern emerges even when the MLP layer is pretrained instead of constructed.

</details>


### [25] [Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning for LLM Depth Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27786)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Youngrae Kim,Woosang Lim,YoungJin Heo,Minkyu Kim,Sunwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LoRP，一种无需训练的单次深度剪枝框架，通过表示局部性（RLS指标）识别层间冗余，在多种LLM上提升困惑度和下游任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次剪枝方法依赖局部层重要性或固定的冗余假设，未能考虑不同LLM架构中层间冗余的分布差异（局部化或全局分布）。

Method: 1) 提出表示局部性分数（RLS）量化全局层间隐藏状态相似性；2) 基于小校准集计算逐层相似性并聚类；3) 根据聚类内残差冗余分配剪枝比例。

Result: 在多种LLM系列上的实验表明，LoRP在困惑度和下游任务准确率上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: LoRP通过感知层间冗余的局部性分布，实现了无需训练的高效单次深度剪枝，且具有跨架构通用性。

Abstract: Large language models are known to contain representational redundancy across network depth, making depth pruning an effective approach for improving inference efficiency. Existing one-shot pruning methods rely on local layer importance or fixed redundancy assumptions across architectures. We propose Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning (LoRP), a training-free one-shot depth pruning framework guided by representation locality. We show that inter-layer redundancy can be either localized or globally distributed depending on the LLM architecture. To characterize this phenomenon, we introduce Representation Locality Score (RLS), derived from global inter-layer hidden-state similarity. Using a small calibration set, LoRP computes pairwise layer similarity, clusters layers by representational similarity, and allocates pruning according to residual intra-cluster redundancy. Experiments across diverse LLM families show improvements in both perplexity and downstream task accuracy.

</details>


### [26] [Knowing When to Ask: Segment-Level Credit Assignment for LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27788)
*Abhijit Kumar,Zoey Wu,Mohit Suley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARL方法，通过强化学习训练评论家模型，让语言模型学会判断何时需要调用外部工具，在5个基准上提升6.7-9.7个点的精确匹配准确率，同时减少53%的不必要工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型无法自主判断何时需要外部工具辅助。基于提示的方法无法教会模型识别自身知识边界；基于轨迹级奖励的RL方法无法区分单个工具调用是否真正有益。

Method: CARL使用RL训练一个评论家模型，在自然工具使用边界（如代码分隔符、上下文块转换处）对每个轨迹分段，从单一二元结果出发为每个段分配独立优势值，使错误、不必要或正确的工具调用分别获得相应符号的advantage。

Result: 评论家捕捉模型领域能力，区分参数化可解与工具依赖问题的AUC达0.93（7B）。在5个基准上，7B提升6.7 EM，3B提升9.7 EM；Musique上增益最大（7B +8.3 EM，3B +9.0 EM）。工具调用减少53%，参数化可解答问题准确率仍高~10 EM。小模型收益更大（3B提升是7B的1.4倍）。

Conclusion: CARL通过细粒度信用分配有效训练模型判断自身知识边界，减少不必要工具调用，尤其对参数量较小的模型帮助更大。

Abstract: Humans know when to reach for help e.g. $347 \times 28$ warrants a calculator while $2+2$ does not. Language models do not. Prompt-based approaches can instruct a model when to invoke tools, but this scaffolding does not teach it to recognize the boundary of its own knowledge. RL approaches that assign a single outcome reward to the whole trajectory fare no better: trajectory-level credit cannot isolate which tool call in a successful episode actually helped, nor penalize unnecessary calls. We propose \textbf{CARL} (\textbf{C}ompetence-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which trains a critic on the model's own rollouts to learn where parametric knowledge suffices and where it needs external help. By decomposing each rollout at natural tool-use boundaries (e.g., code fence delimiters and context block transitions), CARL assigns independent credit to each segment from a single binary outcome, without external judges or step-level annotations. As a result, erroneous tool calls, incorrect extractions, and unnecessary calls each receive appropriately signed advantages. The trained critic captures the model's domain competence: it separates parametrically solvable from tool-dependent questions with AUC 0.93 at 7B. On five benchmarks spanning arithmetic, multi-hop factual QA, and numerical reasoning over financial tables, CARL improves exact-match accuracy by 6.7 points at 7B and 9.7 points at 3B over the best RL baseline, with the largest gain (+8.3 EM at 7B, +9.0 EM at 3B) on Musique. The model issues 53\% fewer tool calls on parametrically answerable questions while remaining ${\sim}10$ EM points more accurate on them. Gains are largest at small scale: the 3B improvement is $1.4\times$ the 7B improvement, suggesting that knowing when to ask disproportionately benefits models with smaller parametric memory.

</details>


### [27] [SYNAPSE: Neuro-Symbolic Visual Thought-to-Text Decoding via Topological Semantic Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27790)
*Akshaj Murhekar,Abhijit Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SYNAPSE是一个轻量级神经符号框架，通过推理时符号正则化（利用常识图结构和潜在样本净化EEG语义候选）来稳定开放词汇的EEG到想象文本解码，在不微调LLM的情况下提升语义稳定性，同时保护生物特征隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG到想象文本解码系统对生物噪声高度敏感，受损的神经投影会导致冻结语言模型产生幻觉或语义不稳定的生成，亟需一种无需端到端LLM微调的稳定化方法。

Method: 提出SYNAPSE框架，采用推理时符号正则化：利用常识图结构（commonsense graph structure）和潜在样本（latent exemplars）对EEG衍生的语义候选进行净化，从而稳定神经文本生成过程。

Result: 在多个EEG解码基准和多种冻结LLM后端上，相比无约束提示基线取得一致提升；在对象标签消融下保持鲁棒性；性能与资源密集型的微调系统相当，同时通过将原始EEG处理限制在编码器栈内保护生物特征隐私。

Conclusion: SYNAPSE作为一种轻量级、无需微调的神经符号框架，能有效提升EEG到文本解码的语义稳定性与鲁棒性，兼顾性能与隐私保护。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have accelerated open-vocabulary EEG-to-imagined-text decoding, where non-invasive neural activity recorded during visual perception is translated into coherent natural language descriptions of viewed stimuli. However, existing systems remain highly vulnerable to biological noise, where corrupted neural projections induce hallucinated or semantically unstable generation in frozen language models. We introduce SYNAPSE (Symbolic Neural Alignment for Precise Semantic Extraction), a lightweight neuro-symbolic framework that stabilizes neural text generation through inference-time symbolic regularization. By purifying EEG-derived semantic candidates using commonsense graph structure and latent exemplars, SYNAPSE improves semantic stability without end-to-end LLM fine-tuning. Experiments across popular EEG decoding benchmarks and multiple frozen LLM backends demonstrate consistent gains over unconstrained prompting baselines, robustness under object-label ablation, and performance commensurate with substantially more resource-intensive fine-tuned systems, while preserving biometric privacy by localizing raw EEG processing entirely within the encoder stack.

</details>


### [28] [ReSAE: Residualized Sparse Autoencoders for Multi-Layer Transformer Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27819)
*Prathyush Poduval,Calvin Yeung,Neel Desai,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Residualized Sparse Autoencoders (ReSAEs)，通过在层间拟合仿射映射，让后续层的SAE只编码前一层未解释的残差，从而减少字典冗余、提升多层干预效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏自编码器逐层独立训练，忽略了Transformer残差流跨层间的强耦合性，导致多层干预时不同层的字典重复表示相同的前向信息，替换多层时产生无法由单层行为预测的交互效应。

Method: 在选定层之间拟合仿射映射，使后续层的SAE只编码前一层无法解释的残差部分，而非完整激活；重建时通过拟合的仿射链将表示映射回原始激活空间，保持与普通SAE相同的干预协议。

Result: 在Pythia-1.4B和Gemma-2-9B上，残差化减少了解码器冗余，提升了稀疏探针和定向扰动性能；尽管重建的原始激活方差更少，但多层替换下恢复了更多Transformer交叉熵损失，在教师强制和在线高稀疏度下最为明显。

Conclusion: 移除线性可预测的跨层结构是进行多层SAE干预的有效默认策略。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are usually trained one layer at a time, even though transformer residual stream activations are strongly coupled across depth. This creates a practical problem for multi-layer interventions: different layerwise dictionaries can spend capacity representing the same carried-forward information, and replacing several layers at once can produce interactions that are not predicted by single-layer behavior. We introduce Residualized Sparse Autoencoders (ReSAEs), which fit an affine map between selected layers and train each later-layer SAE on the unexplained residual rather than on the full activation. Reconstructions are mapped back into the original activation space through the fitted affine chain, so ReSAEs can be evaluated with the same intervention protocols as ordinary SAEs. On Pythia-1.4B and Gemma-2-9B, residualization reduces decoder redundancy and improves sparse probing and targeted perturbation in most tested settings. Despite reconstructing less of the raw activation variance, ReSAEs recover more transformer cross entropy under multi-layer replacement. This gain is clearest under teacher-forcing and at sufficient sparsity online, indicating that ReSAEs preserve the components of the activation most relevant to the model's downstream computation. These results suggest that removing linearly predictable cross-layer structure is a useful default for multi-layer SAE interventions.

</details>


### [29] [Reward Transfer from Inverse Reinforcement Learning: A Coupled Minimax Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27834)
*Guang-Yuan Hao,Lars van der Laan,Aurélien Bibaut,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究逆向强化学习中跨环境奖励迁移问题，提出联合求解源-目标贝尔曼方程组的耦合方法，相比顺序方法可消除源贝尔曼残差的一阶影响，并给出理论保证与实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当专家演示在受控环境中收集，而强化学习需要在不同环境中部署时，如何有效迁移逆向强化学习学到的奖励函数是一个自然且重要的问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为源环境与目标环境联合的贝尔曼方程组，开发目标软Q函数的最小极大估计器。提出耦合方法（联合求解源-目标方程组）与顺序方法（先估计源奖励再代入目标控制问题）对比。

Result: 耦合方法相比顺序方法能消除源贝尔曼残差误差的一阶影响；给出了两种方法的局部行为刻画、有限样本软Q函数误差界以及软控制策略的遗憾保证；在败血症模拟器上验证了理论对比。

Conclusion: 耦合求解方法在跨环境奖励迁移中理论上优于顺序方法，具有更小的误差传播，为实际应用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the transfer of rewards learned using inverse reinforcement learning from expert demonstrations in one environment to reinforcement learning in a new, different environment. This arises naturally when demonstrations are collected in a controlled environment. We formulate the problem as a joint system of Bellman equations across the source and target environments and develop minimax estimators for the target soft-$q$-function. Whereas a sequential solution approach first estimates the source reward and then plugs it into the target control problem, a coupled approach solves the source and target system of equations jointly. We show that, in contrast to the sequential approach, the coupled approach removes the first-order influence of source Bellman residual error. We characterize the local behavior of each approach, develop finite-sample soft-$q$-function error bounds, and prove regret guarantees for the resulting soft-control policy. An empirical investigation using a sepsis simulator validates the theoretical comparison.

</details>


### [30] [CAREF: Calibration-Aware Regularization for Explanation Faithfulness Without Rationale Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27835)
*Naphat Nithisopa,Teerapong Panboonyuen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAREF是一个参数高效的微调框架，通过校准感知正则化联合优化预测准确性和解释忠实性，核心是统一熵校准与token级稀疏控制的单一损失函数LSCED，无需理性监督。在四个NLE基准上，其轻量变体CAREF-AQ仅用6.43%可训练参数即达到最优平均准确率(89.04)和解释对齐度(81.00 nBERT)，优于LoRA和AdaLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效微调方法在提升预测准确性的同时，往往无法保证模型解释的忠实性，且缺乏将校准与稀疏性统一在单一训练目标中的方法。

Method: 提出CAREF框架，核心是校准感知解释忠实性正则化损失(LSCED)，将基于熵的校准与token级稀疏控制耦合为统一损失函数，无需人工标注的理性解释作为监督信号。

Result: 在COS-E、ECQA、ComVE、e-SNLI四个NLE基准上，CAREF-AQ变体以仅6.43%的可训练参数取得最佳平均准确率(89.04)和nBERT解释对齐度(81.00)，全面超越LoRA和AdaLoRA。

Conclusion: CAREF是首个将熵正则化与稀疏正则化统一在单一训练目标中的方法，为可解释LLM微调提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: We introduce CAREF, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework that jointly optimizes predictive accuracy and explanation faithfulness via calibration-aware regularization. At its core, CAREF couples entropy-based calibration with token-level sparsity control through a single unified loss, the Calibration-Aware Regularization for Explanation Faithfulness (LSCED), without requiring rationale supervision. Evaluated on four NLE benchmarks (COS-E, ECQA, ComVE, e-SNLI) with Flan-T5, our lightweight CAREF-AQ variant attains the best average accuracy (89.04) and explanation alignment (81.00 nBERT) using only 6.43% of trainable parameters, outperforming LoRA and AdaLoRA. To our knowledge, CAREF is the first method to unify entropy and sparsity regularization in a single training objective for interpretable LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [31] [From Detection to Mechanism: Cross-Attention Graph Neural Networks Enable Drug-Drug Interaction Type Prediction An Ablation Study with Acetylsalicylic Acid Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27861)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统消融实验比较三种GNN架构在药物相互作用预测上的表现，发现交叉注意力机制（CrossAtt）在多元分类任务上比简单拼接提升F1-macro达45%，而二分类提升仅1.3%，证实原子级分子间通信专门用于机制类型分类；三元架构因训练不稳定而表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物相互作用预测包含二分类（是否交互）和多元分类（交互机制类型）两个本质上不同的任务，现有研究缺乏对GNN架构在这两个任务上差异化的系统比较。

Method: 在包含38,337个正例对、86种交互类型的公开基准数据集上（共61,339对），对三种GNN架构（双路MPNN+拼接Concat、双路MPNN+四头交叉注意力CrossAtt、三元MPNN+交互图Ternary）在相同训练条件下进行消融实验，并在10个预先留出的乙酰水杨酸药物对上进行验证。

Result: CrossAtt在多元分类F1-macro上比Concat提升+0.186绝对值（+45%），但在二分类AUC上仅提升+0.012（+1.3%）；Ternary架构表现不佳且与训练不稳定假说一致；CrossAtt在10个ASA对中全部正确预测DDI类型，Ternary全部错误。

Conclusion: 原子级分子间通信（交叉注意力）对机制类型分类至关重要，但对二分类贡献甚微；三元架构存在训练不稳定问题；所有架构均存在两个一致的失败案例，与伴随毒性研究中的结构限制相关。

Abstract: Predicting whether two drugs interact (binary detection) is a substantially dif- ferent task from predicting the mechanism type of that interaction (multi-class classification). This study presents a systematic ablation study of three Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures for drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction on a publicly available benchmark dataset comprising 38,337 positive pairs across 86 interaction types. Three architectures are compared under identical training conditions (n = 61,339 pairs): a siamese dual Message Passing Neural Network (MPNN) with concatenation (Concat), a dual MPNN with four-head cross-attention (CrossAtt), and a ternary MPNN incorporating an interaction graph (Ternary). CrossAtt improves multi-class F1-macro by +0.186 absolute (+45%) over Concat, while improving binary AUC by only +0.012 (+1.3%) - confirming that atom-level inter-molecular communication specifically enables mechanism-type classification. The ternary architecture underperforms despite equivalent training data, with its failure consistent with a training instability hypothesis. Validation on ten acetylsali- cylic acid (ASA) drug pairs, held out prior to training, demonstrates 10/10 correct DDI-type predictions for CrossAtt versus 0/10 for Ternary. Two consistent failure cases are identified across all architectures, linking to structural limits established in a companion toxicity study.

</details>


### [32] [SPAR: Support-Preserving Action Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27877)
*Jiaxin Zhao,Weihang Pan,Xun Liang,Binbin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPAR提出了一种支持保留的动作修正框架，通过将全局学习重构为锚定在冻结行为克隆策略上的局部残差修正，并引入潜在自模仿的加权回归机制，解决了离线策略改进中价值最大化与数据分布拟合之间的冲突，在D4RL上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略改进面临价值最大化与数据分布拟合之间的固有冲突：样本内加权回归虽然稳定但过于保守，抑制了分布尾部的高价值动作；而基于梯度的方法存在拟合-优化梯度冲突，驱动策略偏离数据流形。

Method: 提出SPAR框架，将全局学习重构为锚定在冻结行为克隆策略上的局部残差修正。在残差空间中进行细粒度拟合和局部策略改进，收缩搜索空间。引入潜在自模仿机制，利用潜在采样加权回归来解决残差空间中的拟合-改进梯度冲突。

Result: 理论上证明了该机制消除了标准价值梯度的流形法向漂移；D4RL实验表明SPAR从次优基线中提取显著收益，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SPAR通过局部残差修正和潜在自模仿加权回归有效解决了离线策略改进中的拟合-优化冲突，实现了既保持数据支持又最大化价值的目标。

Abstract: Offline policy improvement faces an inherent conflict between maximizing value and fitting the data distribution. While in-sample weighted regression is stable, it suffers from over-conservatism that suppresses high-value actions in the distribution tail; conversely, gradient-based approaches often exhibit a fitting-optimization conflict of gradients, which drives the policy off the data manifold. To address this, we propose Support-Preserving Action Rectification (SPAR), which reframes global learning as a local residual rectification anchored to a frozen pure behavior cloning policy. This framework performs fine-grained fitting and local policy improvement in the residual space, thereby contracting the search space. We further introduce Latent Self-Imitation, utilizing a latent-sampling weighted-regression mechanism to address fitting-improvement gradient conflict in the residual space. Theoretically, we prove this mechanism eliminates the manifold-normal drift of standard value gradients, while extensive D4RL experiments show SPAR extracts significant gains from suboptimal baselines to achieve state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [33] [FedEHR-Gen: Federated Synthetic Time-Series EHR Generation via Latent Space Alignment and Distribution-Aware Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27892)
*Jun Bai,Ziyang Song,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedEHR-Gen，首个用于分布式医院间合成时序电子健康记录生成的联邦学习框架，通过两阶段学习范式（联邦自编码器+联邦时序条件变分自编码器）解决高维稀疏性和跨医院异质性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR生成模型多为集中式，需跨医院汇集数据，但真实场景中数据共享受限；直接联邦建模因EHR数据的高维性、稀疏性和跨医院异质性而易崩溃或发散。

Method: 两阶段学习范式：1）联邦自编码器，通过层对齐匹配聚合机制将高维稀疏EHR特征投影到统一紧凑隐空间；2）在该对齐隐空间上训练联邦时序条件变分自编码器（TCVAE），采用分布感知聚合以应对跨医院异质性。

Result: 在eICU和MIMIC-III数据集上，FedEHR-Gen在生成保真度、下游效用和隐私风险方面达到与集中式训练相当的水平，且一致优于标准联邦基线方法。

Conclusion: 首次验证了在严格隐私约束下通过联邦学习实现高质量时序EHR数据合成的可行性，为跨医院数据增强和建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Synthetic Electronic Health Record (EHR) generation provides a promising avenue for data augmentation and cross-hospital modeling in privacy-constrained healthcare settings. However, most existing EHR generative models are centralized and require pooling data across hospitals, which is often infeasible when real-world data sharing is restricted. While federated EHR generation offers a natural solution, direct federated modeling often collapses or diverges due to the high dimensionality, sparsity, and cross-hospital heterogeneity of EHR data. In this work, we propose FedEHR-Gen, the first federated framework for synthetic time-series EHR generation across distributed hospitals. FedEHR-Gen uses a two-stage learning paradigm. First, we introduce a federated autoencoder that projects high-dimensional and sparse EHR features onto a compact latent space. To ensure semantic consistency across hospitals, we develop a layer-wise matching aggregation mechanism that aligns local encoders into a unified global latent space. Second, operating on this aligned latent space, we train a federated temporal conditional variational autoencoder (TCVAE) with distribution-aware aggregation, enabling stable temporal generative modeling under severe cross-hospital heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on the eICU and MIMIC-III datasets demonstrate that FedEHR-Gen achieves generation fidelity, downstream utility, and privacy risk comparable to centralized training, while consistently outperforming the standard federated baseline.

</details>


### [34] [Cyclical Entropy Eruption: Entropy Dynamics in Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27954)
*Wendi Li,Shawn Im,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现智能体强化学习中存在周期性熵爆发现象，并提出了SEAL方法通过分离正确与错误轨迹的表示空间来稳定训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体强化学习的训练动态尚不明确，限制了对训练不稳定性诊断和更有效算法设计的能力。

Method: 识别了周期性熵爆发（cyclical entropy eruption）现象，将其分解为三个阶段进行理论和实证分析；提出SEAL（Separation-Enhanced Agent Learning）轻量级辅助损失，在表示空间分离正确与错误轨迹。

Result: SEAL在多个基准、模型和RL算法上稳定了训练过程，并提升了智能体下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 熵爆发会导致句子重复和幻觉等退化模式跨周期积累，SEAL通过直接解决熵爆发的根本原因来缓解这一问题。

Abstract: Agentic large language models are increasingly used to solve real-world tasks by reasoning over goals, invoking tools, and interacting with external environments. Reinforcement learning provides a natural framework for improving these behaviors, and recent agent RL methods have achieved strong results across domains. However, the training dynamics of agent RL remain poorly understood, limiting our ability to diagnose instabilities and design more effective training algorithms. In this work, we identify a previously underexplored phenomenon in agent RL, which we term cyclical entropy eruption. Unlike single-turn reasoning RL, where entropy typically collapses and stays low, agent RL training exhibits unique recurring cycles of sharp entropy eruption and gradual subsidence. We decompose this dynamic into three phases and provide theoretical and empirical analyses of each, explaining the mechanisms underlying its cyclical oscillation. We further show that degenerate patterns such as sentence duplication and hallucination, once acquired during eruption, can persist and accumulate across cycles. Motivated by these findings, we propose SEAL (Separation-Enhanced Agent Learning), a lightweight auxiliary loss that separates correct and incorrect trajectories in representation space, directly targeting the root cause of entropy eruption. Experiments across multiple benchmarks, models, and RL algorithms demonstrate that SEAL stabilizes training and yields stronger downstream agent performance.

</details>


### [35] [Law of Neural Interaction: Depth-Width Shape, Interaction Efficiency, and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27989)
*Wenjie Sun,Jinning Yang,Shuai Zhang,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经交互概念，将叠加从参数空间扩展到梯度空间，发现固定预算下模型深度-宽度比（R_{D/W}）影响资源利用效率，存在高效交互区间，该区间随预算扩展保持稳定，靠近该区间的密集小模型在MMLU-Pro上表现更好，为模型形状初始化和泛化机制提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM在缩放定律指导下资源需求不断增长，但在固定预算下模型是否有效利用资源仍存疑。此前研究已证明叠加（superposition）是损失的重要来源，本文旨在进一步探索资源利用效率与泛化之间的关系。

Method: 基于神经特征假设（Neural Feature Ansatz），将叠加概念从参数空间扩展到梯度空间，定义为神经交互（neural interaction）。通过调整模型深度-宽度比（R_{D/W}）寻找高效交互区间，并在不同规模预算下验证区间的稳定性。

Result: 固定预算下，良好的泛化通常伴随高效的神经交互；通过调整R_{D/W}可将模型置于高效交互区间；该区间随预算扩展保持相对稳定；靠近该区间的小规模密集LLM在MMLU-Pro基准上表现更好。

Conclusion: R_{D/W}通过影响资源利用效率进而影响泛化能力，为模型形状初始化和泛化机制的理解提供了新见解。

Abstract: The guidance of scaling laws has increased the resource demands of modern large language models (LLMs), yet it remains questionable whether these models utilize resources effectively under a fixed budget. Previous research has proved superposition as a key contributor to loss. By leveraging the Neural Feature Ansatz, we extend superposition from parameter space to gradient space and define it as neural interaction. We find that under a fixed budget, good generalization is usually accompanied by efficient neural interactions, and the model can be placed in an efficient interaction interval by adjusting its depth-width ratio ($R_{D/W}$). In addition, as the budget scales up, the efficient interaction interval of the model remains relatively stable. By comparing existing small scale dense LLMs, we observe that models operating near this interval tend to perform better on the MMLU-Pro benchmark. Our findings reveal that the $R_{D/W}$ influences resource utilization efficiency and thereby affects generalization, providing insights into model shape initialization and the understanding of model generalization mechanisms. Code for Neural Interaction Law is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Neural_Interaction_Law-D788

</details>


### [36] [Geometry-Correct Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Denoiser-Pullback Curvature Guidance and Manifold-Aligned Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27990)
*Seunghyeok Shin,Minwoo Kim,Dabin Kim,Hongki Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种替代传统标量引导权重的扩散后验采样方法，使用每噪声水平的阻尼高斯-牛顿校正，在扩散状态坐标中执行，通过矩阵-free GMRES求解，并在加速MRI重建等逆问题中取得了竞争性或最优的性能指标，同时运行速度显著快于多数基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散后验采样中，数据一致性更新依赖手工调节的标量引导权重，且在刚度大、算子依赖的曲率下容易导致采样不稳定。

Method: 将标量引导替换为每噪声水平的阻尼高斯-牛顿校正，在扩散状态坐标中计算；通过去噪器反向传播似然梯度，使用单侧曲率模型避免前向去噪器雅可比矩阵，采用扩散校准的秩一阻尼与去噪残差对齐；利用自动微分的矩阵-free GMRES求解每次校正；采用方差保持的朗之万转移，具有闭式漂移/噪声分离。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet上的多种逆问题中，取得了与基线竞争性的PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS指标，且运行速度显著快于多数对比基线；在加速MRI重建任务中，取得了对比基线中的最佳PSNR/SSIM。

Conclusion: 所提出的阻尼高斯-牛顿校正方法有效解决了传统标量引导的不稳定性问题，在多种逆问题上实现了高质量和高效的扩散后验采样。

Abstract: Diffusion posterior sampling conditions diffusion priors on measurements, but data-consistency updates are typically scaled by hand-tuned guidance weights and can destabilize sampling under stiff, operator-dependent curvature. We replace scalar guidance with a per-noise-level damped Gauss--Newton correction computed in diffusion-state coordinates. The correction pulls likelihood gradients back through the denoiser, uses a one-sided curvature model that avoids forward denoiser Jacobians, and applies diffusion-calibrated rank-one damping aligned with the denoiser residual. Each correction is solved with matrix-free GMRES using automatic differentiation, and sampling proceeds with a variance-preserving Langevin transition with a closed-form drift/noise split. On FFHQ and ImageNet across inverse problems, it achieves competitive PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS while running markedly faster than most of the compared baselines; on accelerated MRI reconstruction, it achieves the best PSNR/SSIM among the compared baselines.

</details>

*Meraj Mammadov,Pedro Zuidberg Dos Martires,Johannes Andreas Stork*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过自监督对比学习构建共享嵌入空间的方法，在训练教师策略时主动隐藏其特权状态信息，从而缩小模仿学习中的模仿间隙，提升学生策略性能，无需额外的RL微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决模仿学习中因教师策略依赖特权状态信息（学生无法从观测中推断）而产生的不可约模仿间隙问题，避免使用RL微调这一需要全新训练设置的复杂方案。

Method: 提出一种新算法：与教师策略并行训练自监督对比学习构建共享嵌入空间，通过限制梯度更新来阻止编码器提取私有信息，从而隐藏智能体特定的观测，确保教师策略天生可模仿。
