Method: 通过算法稳定性（algorithmic stability）理论，将SVRG更新分解为类似SGD的步骤加上一个零均值校正项，并引入新型Lyapunov函数来吸收由参考点引入的额外梯度项，从而推导出凸设定和强凸设定下的稳定性界。

Result: 获得了数据依赖的尖锐稳定性界（训练误差沿轨迹被纳入），并在凸和强凸设定下得到了最优的过量风险界（excess population risk bounds）。

Conclusion: 本文的分析框架不仅适用于SVRG，还可推广至其他方差缩减方法（如SAGA），为理解VR方法的泛化行为提供了通用理论工具。

Abstract: Variance reduction (VR) methods employ stochastic gradients with decreasing variance, and they have been widely applied to solve large-scale optimization problems in machine learning because of their efficiency. Existing theoretical studies of VR methods are mainly focused on the convergence analysis, leaving the generalization behavior largely unexplored. In this paper, we bridge this gap by developing the first non-vacuous generalization analysis of the representative VR method: Stochastic Variance Reduced Gradient (SVRG), through the lens of algorithmic stability. In particular, we establish sharp stability bounds of the SVRG in both convex and strongly convex settings by exploiting its algorithmic structure. The obtained bounds are data-dependent, because the training errors are incorporated along the trajectory. Our analysis clarifies the interplay between optimization and generalization, leading to optimal excess population risk bounds in both settings. Our approach differs substantially from existing analyses of stochastic algorithms in the sense that we decompose the SVRG update as an SGD-like step plus a zero-mean correction term and then introduce novel Lyapunov functions to absorb the additional gradient terms induced by the reference points. Our analytical framework can be generalized to other VR methods, and we demonstrate the generalization by the well-known Stochastic Average Gradient Accelerated (SAGA) method.

</details>


### [78] [Stochastic Gradient Descent with Momentum is Algorithmically Stable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28517)
*Yunwen Lei,Zimeng Wang,Xiaoming Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过算法稳定性视角，对带动量的随机梯度下降（SGDM）的泛化性能进行了全面的理论分析，建立了针对Polyak和Nesterov两种动量方案的平均模型稳定性界，并获得了最优的过种群风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管SGDM被广泛使用，但其能否以及何时能对未见数据具有良好的泛化性能仍不明确。已有猜测认为动量虽能加速训练，但可能损害泛化能力。本文旨在通过严格的泛化理论分析填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用算法稳定性分析框架，引入统一的广义SGDM框架（涵盖Polyak和Nesterov动量），建立平滑凸问题上的平均模型稳定性界。该界利用了轨迹上的小优化误差界，适用于$[0,1)$区间的任意动量参数，且不依赖于常用的损失函数Lipschitz连续性假设。同时推导了广义SGDM的优化误差界，并与泛化分析结合得到最优过种群风险界。

Result: 建立了SGDM紧的（tight）平均模型稳定性界；该界适用于任意动量参数$[0,1)$；不要求损失函数Lipschitz连续；同时获得了SGDM（含Polyak和Nesterov动量）的最优过种群风险界。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论分析证明，在适当的条件下，SGDM不仅能够加速训练，还能保持良好的泛化性能，打破了"动量损害泛化"的猜测，为动量在机器学习中的使用提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent with momentum (SGDM) is one of the most widely used optimization algorithms in machine learning. While optimization properties of SGDM have been extensively studied in the literature, it remains insufficiently understood whether and when SGDM can generalize well to unseen data. In particular, it has been conjectured that while momentum accelerates training, it may degrade generalization. In this paper, we close this gap by developing a comprehensive generalization analysis of SGDM through the lens of algorithmic stability. More specifically, we introduce a generalized SGDM framework that encompasses both Polyak's and Nesterov's momentum schemes, and establish tight on-average model stability bounds for smooth and convex problems. Notably, the obtained bounds exploit small optimization error bounds along the trajectory, apply to any momentum parameter in the interval $[0, 1)$, and do not require the commonly assumed Lipschitzness of loss functions. We further derive optimization error bounds for the generalized SGDM, and combine them with our generalization analyses to obtain optimal excess population risk bounds for SGDM with both Polyak's and Nesterov's momentum.

</details>


### [79] [Semi-Supervised Hypothesis Testing by Betting on Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28533)
*Yaniv Tenzer,Elad Tolochinsky,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种测试-投注框架，利用未标注数据的预测来增强序贯假设检验的效能。通过有限的(X,Y)联合分布样本和额外的X边际分布未标注样本，构建了e统计量和序贯检验，在标签偏移或概念偏移下保持任意时段有效性，且即使预测不准确也能保留检验性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅有少量标注数据但有大量未标注数据的情况下，如何利用未标注数据提升统计假设检验的效能，特别是在数据分布可能发生变化（标签偏移或概念偏移）的场景中。

Method: 提出测试-投注（testing-by-betting）框架，构建基于预测的e统计量，设计序贯假设检验；在标签偏移和概念偏移的标准分布假设下验证其任意时段有效性；分析二元数据下e统计量的功效。

Result: 该方法在任意时段上有效，二元数据下具有非平凡功效；即使预测不准确也能保持这些性质；在模拟和大型语言模型评估中的应用显示，比预测驱动推断等基线方法有更高的功效，即使在未标注数据有限或X与Y弱相关导致预测精度低的情况下也能保持优势。

Conclusion: 测试-投注框架能有效利用未标注数据提升序贯假设检验的效能，且对预测质量具有鲁棒性，在多种实际场景中优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: We introduce a testing-by-betting framework that leverages predictions on unlabeled data to enhance the power of sequential hypothesis testing. Given limited samples from the joint distribution of $(X,Y)$, and additional unlabeled samples from the marginal of $X$, we ask how unlabeled data can be used to hypothesize about the distribution of $Y$, and the conditional distribution of $Y\mid X$. We introduce an e-statistic and use it to construct a sequential test. Under standard distributional assumptions -- label shift or concept shift -- we establish that the test is anytime valid. Furthermore, we show that for binary data, the e-statistic has non-trivial power. Crucially, our approach retains these properties even when the underlying predictions are inaccurate. Through simulations and applications to large language models evaluation, we demonstrate power gains over baseline approaches, including prediction-powered inference. These gains persist even with relatively limited unlabeled data and when predictions have low accuracy due to weak correlation between $X$ and $Y$.

</details>


### [80] [High Performance, Low Reliability: Uncertainty Benchmarking for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28554)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过112个数据集的TALENT基准测试，发现表格基础模型（TFMs）虽然预测性能（AUC）优于梯度提升决策树（GBDTs），但在不确定性量化（条件覆盖率和SSCS指标）上表现更差，揭示了性能与不确定性之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型在预测性能上已超越传统方法，但其可信赖性（尤其是不确定性量化能力）被广泛忽视，作者旨在系统性地填补这一研究空白。

Method: 在TALENT基准的112个数据集上，对比TFMs、GBDTs和经典基线模型，使用AUC衡量预测性能，使用保形预测下的条件覆盖率（SSCS）衡量不确定性量化能力，并在合成数据集上进一步验证。

Result: TFMs在AUC上表现最佳，但SSCS指标低于GBDTs，呈现出性能-不确定性权衡；合成数据实验揭示该效应加剧的条件。

Conclusion: TFMs虽推动了预测性能前沿，但实现良好校准的不确定性量化仍是其可靠部署面临的主要开放挑战。

Abstract: Recent Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have demonstrated state-of-the-art predictive performance, often surpassing Gradient-Boosted Decision Trees (GBDTs). However, the trustworthiness of these models, particularly their uncertainty quantification, has been largely overlooked. We investigate this gap through an extensive study comparing TFMs, GBDTs, and classical baselines on the 112 datasets of the TALENT benchmark. Our results reveal a performance-uncertainty trade-off: although TFMs achieve the highest predictive performance, measured by AUC, they exhibit lower conditional coverage under conformal prediction, measured by SSCS, compared to GBDTs. Complementary experiments on synthetic datasets further characterize the regimes in which this effect intensifies. We conclude that while TFMs advance predictive frontiers, achieving well-calibrated uncertainty remains a major open challenge for their reliable adoption. Code is available at: https://github.com/jose-melo/high-performance-low-reliability

</details>


### [81] [A Multi-dimensional Framework for Evaluating Generalization in EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28563)
*Aditya Kommineni,Emily Zhou,Kleanthis Avramidis,Tiantian Feng,Shrikanth Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多维评估框架，用于在低资源条件下评估EEG基础模型，发现基础模型在长上下文任务（如睡眠阶段预测）上有优势，但在短窗口BCI任务上监督模型表现相当，且现有基础模型对短窗口任务和通道受限设置的鲁棒性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型通常在完整微调和精心整理的下游数据集上评估，这不符合生物医学领域的实际约束（如标注数据有限、传感器覆盖减少、参数高效适应等），因此需要更真实的评估框架。

Method: 提出一个多维评估框架，在低资源条件下对监督EEG模型和多种EEG基础模型（LaBraM、CSBrain、CBraMod）在6个不同数据集上进行实证分析。

Result: EEG基础模型在长上下文任务（睡眠阶段预测、心理健康状态分类）上表现优异；但在短窗口BCI任务上，监督模型参数量更少但性能相当；基础模型对短窗口任务和通道受限场景的鲁棒性有限。

Conclusion: 应使用多维评估协议来刻画模型在实际使用约束下的行为，以更全面地理解EEG模型的真实能力。

Abstract: Evaluating foundation models under appropriate adaptation settings is essential for understanding the quality and transferability of the learned representations. Recent EEG foundation models have demonstrated promising transfer capabilities across tasks and datasets, motivating their growing use in neurotechnology and clinical applications. However, these models are typically evaluated under full fine-tuning on well-curated downstream datasets, a setting that does not reflect biomedical domain constraints such as limited labeled data, reduced sensor coverage, or parameter-efficient adaptation. In this work, we propose a multi-dimensional evaluation framework for assessing EEG models under realistic low-resource conditions. Empirical analysis of both supervised EEG models and recent EEG foundation models, including LaBraM, CSBrain, and CBraMod, across 6 different datasets is performed under the proposed multi-dimensional evaluation framework. We find that EEG foundation models consistently provide performance gains on long-context tasks such as sleep stage prediction and mental health state classification. In contrast, for short-window Brain Computer Interface style tasks, supervised models achieve comparable despite having substantially fewer parameters. Additional analyses demonstrate that current foundation models provide limited robustness to short-window tasks and channel constrained settings. Together, these findings motivate the use of multi-dimensional evaluation protocols that characterize model behavior under realistic use constraints.

</details>


### [82] [Semantic Optimal Transport for Sparse Autoencoder Feature Matching and Circuit Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28567)
*Tue M. Cao,Nguyen Do,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个分布性框架，通过将SAE特征表示为激活加权的隐藏状态分布，并利用Wasserstein距离在共享参考空间中比较语义距离，统一解决了跨层特征匹配和特征电路压缩两个难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）已成为解释语言模型的核心工具，但两个关键分析仍难以规模化：（1）跨层匹配语义相似的特征；（2）将大型特征电路压缩为可解释的超节点。现有方法将二者视为独立问题，而本文指出它们本质上共享一个更根本的挑战——估计不同激活流形上SAE特征之间的语义距离。

Method: 提出一个分布性框架：每个特征不再用单一的编码器向量表示，而是用其表达的隐藏状态的激活加权分布来表示。通过将这些分布投影到共享参考空间，并使用Wasserstein距离进行比较，提供了一种统一的跨层特征比较语义度量。证明了该表示具有激活重缩放不变性、扰动稳定性，并在有限样本边界条件下能恢复真实匹配。

Result: 该方法在跨层特征匹配和电路压缩任务上优于基于编码器向量和基于LLM的基线方法，能够捕捉相关特征之间细微的功能区别，并自动将大型特征电路压缩为可解释的超节点。

Conclusion: 跨层特征匹配和特征电路压缩可以统一为分布流形上的语义距离估计问题，本文提出的分布性框架为此提供了理论保证和实证优势。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a central tool for interpreting language models. However, two key SAE analyses that remain difficult to scale are (1) matching semantically similar features across multi-layers and (2) compressing large feature circuits into interpretable supernodes. Although these have been treated as separate problems, we show that both are instances of a more fundamental challenge, which we frame as the estimation of semantic distances between SAE features that lie on different activation manifolds. We introduce a distributional framework for this problem, in which each feature is represented not by a single decoder vector like in the literature, but by an activation-weighted distribution over the hidden states that express it. By projecting these distributions into a shared reference space and comparing them with Wasserstein distance, our method provides a unified semantic metric for cross-layer feature comparison. We prove that our representation is invariant to activation rescaling, stable under perturbations, and recovers true matches under finite-sample margin conditions. Empirically, our method outperforms decoder-vector and LLM-based baselines and captures subtle functional distinctions between related features. Notably, our method compresses large feature circuits into interpretable supernodes automatically.

</details>


### [83] [Efficient Pre-Training of LLMs through Truncated SVD Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28573)
*Kaivan Kamali,Kajetan Schweighofer,Hormoz Shahrzad,Olivier Francon,Babak Hodjat,Risto Miikkulainen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TSVD框架，通过保持低秩和严格正交性来降低LLM预训练的计算成本，采用基于频谱能量的自适应秩选择启发式方法和缓存机制维持正交性，理论分析和实验证明该方法在降低计算需求的同时能达到或超越全参数基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型（LLM）的预训练成本日益高昂，现有低秩表示和正交权重矩阵方法存在静态秩选择和计算成本过高无法强制正交性的问题。

Method: 提出TSVD框架：（1）在整个训练过程中保持低秩和严格正交性；（2）基于频谱能量的启发式方法实现自适应秩选择；（3）引入缓存机制来维持正交性。

Result: 在各种模型规模上，TSVD匹配或超越了全参数基线的性能，同时显著降低了计算需求。

Conclusion: TSVD提供了一种有理论基础、实用且可扩展的高效高性能LLM预训练路径。

Abstract: The massive scaling of Large Language Models (LLMs) has made pretraining increasingly cost-prohibitive. While low-rank representation and orthonormal weight matrices could in principle reduce parameter counts and computational overhead, most existing methods rely on static rank selection and do not enforce weight orthonormality due to high computational cost. This paper introduces TSVD, a framework that maintains low rank and strict orthonormality throughout the training process. It utilizes a spectral energy-based heuristic for adaptive rank selection, and a caching mechanisms to maintain orthonormality. Theoretical analysis justifies the advantage of the approach in pretraining dynamics and experiments across various model scales demonstrate that it is effective empirically. TSVD matches or exceeds the performance of full-parameter baselines while significantly reducing compute requirements. The approach thus offers a well-founded, practical, and scalable path toward efficient high-performance LLM pretraining.

</details>


### [84] [Outer-Momentum Restarting in High-Dimensional Two-Phase Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28585)
*Kristi Topollai,Allan Ma,Tolga Dimlioglu,Sui Jiet Tay,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在分布式通信高效优化器（如DiLoCo）中，周期性重置外层动量作为控制外层记忆的补充机制。在经验NTK线性化模型下推导了模态级重置收缩，证明重置通过丢弃过期动量并保留内循环进程来利用相位取消。实验验证了收缩行为，并表明周期性重置拓宽了外层学习率和动量值的稳定范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式优化器中，外层优化器作用于内循环诱导的有效谱，外层动量的选择控制着跨通信轮次的进度积累方式。本文旨在研究周期性重置外层动量这一简单机制，作为控制外层记忆的补充手段。

Method: 在经验NTK下的线性化平方损失模型中，推导模态级重置收缩公式；通过在玩具实验和语言模型预训练实验中验证周期性重置的效果。

Result: 重置利用相位取消：丢弃过期动量同时保留内循环进度；周期性重置拓宽了外层学习率和动量值的稳定范围。

Conclusion: 周期性重置外层动量是一种有效的简单机制，可改善分布式通信高效优化器的稳定性和性能。

Abstract: Communication-efficient distributed optimizers such as DiLoCo reduce synchronization costs by letting workers perform many local updates before aggregating their progress with an outer momentum optimizer. Recent theory suggests that the outer optimizer acts on an effective spectrum induced by the inner optimization loop, and that the choice of outer momentum controls how progress from local updates is accumulated across communication rounds. We study periodic restarting of the outer momentum as a simple complementary mechanism for controlling this outer memory. In a linearized squared-loss model where prediction-space residuals evolve under the empirical NTK, we derive a mode-wise restart contraction showing that resets exploit phase cancellation by discarding stale momentum while preserving inner-loop progress. Toy experiments verify the predicted contraction behavior, and language-model pretraining experiments show that periodic restarts widen the stable range of outer learning rates and momentum values across communication periods.

</details>


### [85] [Transformers Provably Learn to Internalize Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28600)
*Yixiao Huang,Hanlin Zhu,Zixuan Wang,Jiantao Jiao,Stuart Russell,Somayeh Sojoudi,Song Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上分析了隐式思维链（ICoT），提出Log-ICoT课程使得L层Transformer能够以多项式样本学习k-奇偶性，且训练阶段数仅为log₂k，在匹配显式CoT样本效率的同时消除了推理开销，并通过实验验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式CoT虽能提升Transformer的样本效率（如将奇偶性学习从指数复杂度降至多项式），但推理时生成显式推理步骤的计算开销大。隐式CoT（ICoT）虽在实践中通过将中间步骤内化到隐藏状态中展现了潜力，但其理论基础尚不清晰，缺乏严格的理论分析。

Method: 提出Log-ICoT课程训练方法：与标准ICoT每次移除一个思维token不同，Log-ICoT以几何级数方式批量移除思维token，将训练阶段从线性于k（标准ICoT）降至对数级别log₂k。理论证明L层Transformer（L=log₂k训练阶段）可在多项式样本下学习k-奇偶性。

Result: 理论证明Log-ICoT训练下的L层Transformer能以poly(n)样本学习k-奇偶性，样本效率与显式CoT相当，但消除了推理时显式生成中间步骤的开销。实验在多层Transformer上验证了理论，并可视化了推理过程如何逐步被更深层吸收。

Conclusion: Log-ICoT为隐式思维链提供了首个严格理论分析，证明其能在保持显式CoT样本效率的同时消除推理开销，且通过几何级数减少思维token实现了训练阶段的对数级缩减。这为理解Transformer如何内化推理过程提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting substantially improves the sample efficiency of transformers, reducing the complexity of tasks like parity learning from exponential to polynomial in the input length. However, generating explicit reasoning steps at inference is computationally expensive. Implicit Chain-of-Thought (ICoT) has emerged as a promising empirical remedy that trains models to internalize intermediate steps within their hidden states, but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. We give the first theoretical analysis of ICoT, proving that an $L$-layer transformer trained under our proposed Log-ICoT curriculum learns $k$-parity with $\mathsf{poly}(n)$ samples and $L = \log_2 k$ training stages. This matches the sample efficiency of explicit CoT while eliminating its inference overhead, and extends prior one-layer parity guarantees to multi-layer architectures. Compared to standard ICoT, which removes thinking tokens one at a time, Log-ICoT removes them in geometric chunks, reducing the number of stages from linear in $k$ to logarithmic. Experiments on multi-layer transformers confirm the theory and visualize how reasoning is progressively absorbed into deeper layers.

</details>


### [86] [Online Irregular Multivariate Time Series Forecasting via Uncertainty-Driven Dual-Expert Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28603)
*Haonan Wen,Hanyang Chen,Songhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对不规则多变量时间序列（IMTS）在线预测问题，提出不确定性驱动的双专家校准框架（Under-Cali），通过不确定性估计、自适应路由和双专家模块实现轻量级在线适应，在保持源模型冻结的情况下仅校准模块更新，在基准测试上以低计算成本取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IMTS预测方法在离线场景表现良好，但部署到在线环境时因数据分布动态变化（分布漂移）导致性能显著下降。由于不规则采样破坏了时间连续性和周期性，无法直接利用规则MTS中的在线学习技术，因此需要专门针对在线IMTS预测的适应方法。

Method: 提出Under-Cali框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 不确定性估计器，作为控制信号管理推理与适应过程；(2) 双专家校准模块，包括可靠专家和不可靠专家；(3) 自适应路由模块，根据不确定性高低将样本路由到不同专家：高不确定性样本由不可靠专家处理并校准，低不确定性样本由可靠专家处理；随后用校准后的可靠样本更新可靠专家和不确定性估计器，用困难样本更新不可靠专家。

Result: 在IMTS基准数据集上的大量实验表明，Under-Cali以较低的计算成本取得了持续一致的性能改进。

Conclusion: Under-Cali通过不确定性驱动的双专家校准机制，在不修改源预测模型的前提下，实现了轻量级、模型无关的在线适应，有效解决了不规则MTS在线预测中的分布漂移问题。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series forecasting is critical in many real-world applications, where time series are irregularly sampled and exhibit dynamically evolving missingness patterns. Although existing methods perform well in offline settings, they often suffer from significant performance degradation when deployed online due to dynamic shifts in data distribution. Maintaining forecasting capability in such dynamic scenarios typically necessitates online adaptation techniques. Since irregular sampling fundamentally undermines temporal continuity and periodicity, we cannot leverage these widely studied characteristics from regular MTS for online learning. To this end, we study the problem of online IMTS forecasting and propose Under-Cali, an uncertainty-driven dual-expert calibration framework consisting of three core components: an uncertainty estimator, a dual-expert calibration module, and an adaptive routing module. We design an uncertainty estimator that serves as the core control signal to jointly manage inference and adaptation processes. In our framework, the uncertainty estimator first assesses uncertainty for each incoming batch. The adaptive routing module then directs samples with high uncertainty to the unreliable expert for calibration, while low uncertainty samples remain with the reliable expert. Subsequently, the system updates the reliable expert and the uncertainty estimator using well-calibrated reliable samples, and updates the unreliable expert with challenging samples, enabling stable and efficient online learning. Under-Cali keeps the source forecasting model frozen and performs adaptation only through a lightweight, model-agnostic calibration module, enabling efficient adaptation. Extensive experiments on IMTS benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements with low computational cost. Our code is available at https://github.com/HaonanWen/Under-Cali.

</details>


### [87] [Random Process Flow Matching: Generative Implicit Representations of Multivariate Random Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28625)
*Julien Lalanne,David Picard,Lionel Boillot,Lina-María Guayacán-Carrillo,Leon Barens,Jean-Michel Pereira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RP Flow，一种基于Flow Matching的框架，利用随机傅里叶特征学习神经隐式函数表示，从稀疏观测中生成具有不确定性校准的高质量样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型在处理稀疏观测数据时，难以同时保证样本质量和不确定性量化，尤其在高频、高稀疏或高维场景下表现不佳。

Method: RP Flow将向量场表示为神经隐式函数，采用随机傅里叶特征学习任意位置的隐式信号表示，并通过集成采样编码不确定性；同时在源空间中通过高斯过程回归构建贝叶斯后验以生成高质量样本。

Result: 在挑战性条件（高频、高稀疏、高维）下，RP Flow能够生成逼真样本并提供校准后的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: RP Flow是面向数据稀缺且需保持不确定性可追踪的重建任务生成模型的一个重要里程碑。

Abstract: Generative modeling provides a powerful framework for learning data distributions. These models initially relied on probabilistic methods such as Gaussian Processes (GP) for uncertainty-aware predictions and shifted towards larger trainable models to learn more complex distributions. In this work, we introduce Random Process (RP) Flow, a Flow Matching-based framework that represents the vector field as a neural implicit function. Unlike modern generative methods, our setting involves a single observed field, from which only sparse measurements are available. RP Flow uses Random Fourier Features to learn an implicit signal representation that can be queried at any arbitrary location from a limited set of observations, while encoding uncertainty through ensemble sampling. We propose constructing a Bayesian posterior by GP regression in the source space to generate high-quality samples. Our empirical results demonstrate that this framework generates realistic samples along with calibrated uncertainty estimates, even under challenging conditions such as high frequency, high sparsity, or high dimensionality. These findings position RP Flow as a milestone towards generative models for reconstruction tasks where data is scarce and uncertainty must remain traceable.

</details>


### [88] [When Interpretability Is Unequally Distributed: Fairness in Hybrid Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28626)
*Ziba Jabbar Zare,Ulrich Aïvodji,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 混合可解释模型通过将部分样本分配给透明组件、其余样本交给黑盒模型，在准确性与可解释性之间实现灵活权衡，但可能造成某些群体系统性地获得可解释决策而其他群体被不成比例地路由到黑盒，引发程序公平性问题。本文形式化了这种"可解释性覆盖差距"（ICD），并在多种混合可解释学习方法、数据集和敏感属性上进行实验，发现中等透明度区间存在显著ICD，而简单的覆盖差距约束能有效降低ICD，且对准确率和稀疏性影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合可解释模型虽然能灵活平衡准确性与可解释性，但可能产生一种程序公平性问题：不同人口群体被系统性地分配不同类型的决策解释（可解释模型 vs 黑盒模型），这种分配差异尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 形式化定义了可解释性覆盖差距（ICD），采用人口均等风格的度量方法衡量路由决策中的差异；利用预测多重性工具，在四种混合可解释学习方法、三个标准公平基准数据集和多个敏感属性上进行实验分析，并通过约束优化来缓解ICD。

Result: 在中等透明度（即解释器与黑盒组件均被积极使用）的区间内发现了显著的ICD；通过简单覆盖差距约束可以大幅降低ICD，且对准确率和稀疏性影响很小；在某些设定下，降低ICD还能改善标准算法公平性指标。

Conclusion: 混合可解释模型不仅应接受预测公平性的审计，还应审计其在个体和群体之间分配可解释性的方式是否存在不公平。

Abstract: Hybrid interpretable models combine a transparent component with a black-box model by assigning some examples to the former and deferring the rest to the latter. While this design enables flexible tradeoffs between accuracy and interpretability, it also raises a distinct procedural fairness concern: some demographic groups may systematically receive interpretable decisions, while others are disproportionately routed to a black box.
  We formalize this issue as Interpretability Coverage Disparity (ICD), a demographic-parity-style measure applied to the routing decision of hybrid interpretable models. Using tools from predictive multiplicity, we study ICD across four hybrid interpretable learning methods, three standard fairness benchmark datasets, and multiple sensitive attributes. Our experiments reveal substantial ICD in intermediate transparency regimes, where both the interpretable and black-box components are actively used. We further show that simple coverage-disparity constraints can significantly reduce ICD in exact hybrid learning methods, with marginal impact on accuracy and sparsity. In several settings, ICD mitigation also improves standard algorithmic fairness metrics. These results show that hybrid interpretable models should be audited not only for predictive fairness, but also for how they allocate interpretability across individuals and groups.

</details>


### [89] [Interpretability-Guided Layer Selection over Subspace Projection: SAEs as Stethoscopes, Not Scalpels, for Raw Task Vector Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28649)
*Li Lei,Madalina Ciobanu,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）投影任务向量进行模型编辑时，会丢弃约97%的修改能量，导致无显著效果。作者提出将SAE从"手术刀"转向"听诊器"：仅用SAE识别关键层，再注入未滤波的原始任务向量。在Minerva Math基准上，数论准确率从29.6%提升至39.4%，5/7个数学科目显著提升且无退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE引导的模型编辑方法直觉上将任务向量投影到SAE特征子空间，但这种做法存在根本性失败模式。作者旨在揭示该失败原因并提出更有效的替代方案。

Method: 1）实验发现SAE投影会丢弃约97%的修改能量，是一种信息瓶颈；2）通过几何分析证明激活空间的SAE方向与权重空间的任务向量存在几何错配；3）提出"SAE作为听诊器"策略：使用SAE导出的特异性分数识别关键层，仅在识别出的层注入未滤波的原始任务向量。

Result: 在Minerva Math基准上：数论从29.6%提升至39.4%（z=+3.41, p=0.0007）；5/7个数学科目显著提升，无科目显著退化。方法完全确定、无额外推理代价。

Conclusion: SAE更适合作为诊断工具（听诊器）而非直接干预工具（手术刀）；SAE驱动的层诊断结合原始任务向量注入是一种有效、轻量且可解释的模型编辑框架。

Abstract: LLMs increasingly require surgical model editing to enhance domain-specific capabilities without incurring the computational cost or catastrophic forgetting associated with full fine-tuning. Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a promising tool in this setting, in principle allowing for feature-level identification of where to intervene. In this work, we rigorously evaluate an SAE-guided editing pipeline for mathematical reasoning on Gemma-3-4B-IT and uncover a fundamental failure mode: the intuitively appealing approach of projecting task vectors onto SAE feature subspaces acts as an information bottleneck that discards approximately 97% of the modification energy, yielding no statistically significant improvements across seven math subjects. We show that this failure stems from a geometric misalignment between activation-space SAE directions and weight-space task vectors. We then propose a shift in perspective: SAE as a Stethoscope, Not a Scalpel, where SAEs are used for layer-level diagnosis rather than intervention-level filtering. By injecting unfiltered raw task vectors only into layers identified by an SAE-derived specificity score, we improve Number Theory accuracy from 29.6% to 39.4% (z=+3.41, p=0.0007) on the Minerva Math benchmark; 5 of 7 math subjects significantly improved and none significantly degraded. Our method is fully deterministic, requires no additional inference cost, and provides a principled framework for interpretability-guided model editing.

</details>


### [90] [Activation Steering for Synthetic Data Generation: The Role of Diversity in Downstream Safety Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28664)
*Vijeta Deshpande,Tootiya Giyahchi,Veena Padmanabhan,Leman Akoglu,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活引导（Activation Steering, AS）能否为下游安全检测分类器生成高质量训练数据。通过内在评估（引入样本级和集合级多样性作为新维度）和外在评估（替换HHH违规训练数据），发现AS在3/4概念上优于提示生成数据，但仅有41/136配置表现更佳，表明下游效用需同时满足引导成功、连贯性和多样性。三者调和平均与AUROC的相关性优于单独的成功和连贯性，为AS超参数调优提供了实用启发式目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全检测模型需要大量HHH违规样本以实现鲁棒泛化，但此类样本稀缺。激活引导作为一种数据高效的方法能够生成目标概念对齐的响应，但其能否为下游分类器生成高质量训练数据集尚未被验证。

Method: 在4个概念×2个模型×4种引导方法下进行双重研究：内在评估引入样本级和集合级多样性作为新质量维度；外在评估将AS生成的样本替换训练数据中的HHH违规样本，微调检测分类器。

Result: AS生成的数据在3/4概念上优于提示生成数据训练的检测器；但仅41/136的AS配置优于提示生成，表明下游效用存在于同时满足引导成功、连贯性和多样性的狭窄区域。

Conclusion: 激活引导在安全检测的合成数据生成方面具有潜力，多样性是此前被忽视的AS调优关键维度，三者调和平均可作为实际调优的启发式目标。

Abstract: Safety detection models require examples of HHH (Helpful, Harmless, Honest)-violating outputs for robust generalization, however such examples are scarce. Activation Steering (AS) has emerged as a data-efficient method for generating target-concept-aligned responses. We investigate whether AS can generate high-quality training datasets for downstream classifiers, a question that remains untested. We present a two-fold study with intrinsic and extrinsic evaluation across $4$ concepts $\times\,2$ models $\times\,4$ steering methods. Intrinsically, beyond the field-standard rubric of steering success (concept alignment) and coherence, we introduce sample- and set-level diversity as a quality axis previously absent from the literature, and find that increasing steering strength reduces response diversity. Extrinsically, we replace HHH-violating examples in the available training data with steered generations and fine-tune detection classifiers. AS-generated data results in a better classifier than the prompting-generated data on $3$ of $4$ concepts. However, only $41$ of $136$ AS configurations outperform prompting, indicating that downstream utility lies in a narrow regime that jointly satisfies success, coherence, and diversity. The harmonic mean of these three axes correlates with downstream AUROC more consistently across concepts than success and coherence alone, providing a practical heuristic target for practitioners tuning AS hyperparameters. Together, our results highlight the potential of AS in synthetic data generation for improving safety detection and identify diversity as a critical, previously overlooked axis for tuning AS.

</details>


### [91] [Stage-wise Distortion-Perception Traversal in Zero-shot Inverse Problems with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28711)
*Jiawei Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Leon Yan,Zhenyu Xiao,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAP-RPS方法，通过"MAP估计+重噪声后验采样"两阶段框架，利用单扩散模型在零样本逆问题中实现失真-感知权衡的灵活遍历，并扩展至潜在空间LMAP-RPS，实验验证其有效性和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失真-感知权衡是贝叶斯逆问题的基本现象，现有扩散模型虽在零样本逆问题求解中取得成功，但缺乏高效且 principled 的策略来在推理时灵活遍历D-P权衡曲线。

Method: 提出MAP-RPS两阶段框架：第一阶段进行MAP估计，近似MMSE解并提供低失真初始化；第二阶段进行重噪声后验采样，逐步提升感知质量。理论分析了两阶段的有效性，并扩展至潜在空间得到LMAP-RPS，利用大规模预训练潜在扩散骨干。

Result: 实验表明，MAP-RPS和LMAP-RPS在多种任务上实现了更有效的D-P权衡遍历，同时作为真实世界逆问题的高效求解器也表现出强劲性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法为扩散模型在逆问题中实现灵活的失真-感知权衡遍历提供了一种理论完备且实践有效的解决方案，潜在空间的扩展进一步提升了适用性。

Abstract: The distortion-perception (D-P) tradeoff is a fundamental phenomenon of Bayesian inverse problems, which characterizes the inherent tension between distortion performance and perceptual quality. Enabling flexible traversal of the D-P tradeoff at inference time is crucial for practical applications. Despite the recent success of diffusion models in zero-shot inverse problem solving, efficient and principled strategies for D-P traversal in diffusion-based inverse algorithms remain inadequately characterized. In this paper, we propose a stage-wise framework for realizing D-P traversal using a single diffusion model in zero-shot inverse problems. Our proposed method, termed MAP-RPS, starts with an MAP estimation stage that approximates the MMSE solution and provides a low-distortion initialization, followed by a re-noised posterior sampling stage that progressively improves perceptual quality. We provide theoretical analyses for both stages, establishing the validity and effectiveness of the proposed design. Furthermore, we extend MAP-RPS to the latent space, yielding LMAP-RPS, which enjoys broader applicability by leveraging large-scale pre-trained latent diffusion backbones. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MAP-RPS and LMAP-RPS enable more effective D-P traversal on various tasks, while also exhibiting strong performance as efficient solvers for real-world inverse problems.

</details>


### [92] [Extrapolative Weight Averaging Reveals Correctness-Efficiency Frontiers in Code RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28751)
*Kunhao Zheng,Pierre Chambon,Juliette Decugis,Jonas Gehring,Taco Cohen,Benjamin Negrevergne,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在竞争性编程的强化学习中，通过外推权重平均（extrapolative weight averaging）在正确性与效率之间扩展帕累托前沿，无需额外RL训练即可在推理时获得互补策略，并将LCB/hard上的pass@250提升3.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管线性插值可以在微调检查点之间追踪帕累托前沿，但外推权重平均是否能在不经过额外RL训练的情况下扩展这一前沿、生成推理时可用的新检查点，尚不清楚。

Method: 从共享初始化出发，在嵌套单元测试覆盖率下训练检查点（低覆盖率奖励通过小输入测试，高覆盖率奖励通过逐步增大的测试），然后在低覆盖率与高覆盖率检查点之间进行外推权重平均，并在纯推理、工具使用和智能体编码三种推理设置以及32B和7B两种模型规模下验证。

Result: 1）嵌套覆盖率奖励揭示了正确性-效率前沿；2）插值可恢复该前沿，外推可扩展至训练端点之外；3）外推检查点在问题级别上改变了解决的问题集合，成为推理时可互补的策略；4）集成外推权重平均在LCB/hard上使pass@250提升3.3%。

Conclusion: 嵌套单元测试覆盖率在代码RL中诱导出可被外推权重平均导航、扩展和利用的前沿，为推理时扩展提供互补策略。

Abstract: Linear interpolation between fine-tuned checkpoints has been shown to trace the Pareto front between competing objectives, but whether extrapolative weight averaging can extend such frontiers to new checkpoints useful at inference time, without additional RL training, remains unclear. We study this question in RL for competitive programming, where hidden unit tests under time and memory limits enforce both functional correctness and computational efficiency. Starting from a shared initialization, we train checkpoints under nested unit-test coverage: low-coverage rewards require passing smaller-input tests, while high-coverage rewards require passing progressively larger tests up to the full suite. This sweep reveals the emergence of a correctness-efficiency frontier: on hard problems, higher-coverage reward reduces optimization failures but increases correctness failures, leaving solve rate nearly unchanged. Interpolation between low- and high-coverage checkpoints recovers this frontier, while extrapolation extends it beyond the trained endpoints. Both the frontier and its extrapolative continuation appear across three inference settings, pure reasoning, tool use, and agentic coding, and across two model scales, 32B and 7B. At the problem level, moving along the frontier changes which problems are solved, making extrapolated checkpoints complementary policies in inference-time scaling. Ensembles with extrapolative weight averaging broaden coverage and improve pass@250 on LCB/hard by 3.3% over the best single checkpoint at matched sample budget. These results show that nested unit-test coverage in code RL induces a frontier that extrapolative weight averaging can navigate, extend, and exploit.

</details>


### [93] [LLM Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning is an Inference Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28760)
*Zelin Li,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM的零阶（ZO）微调实质上是推理主导的工作负载，通过将重复评分阶段迁移到推理运行时（vLLM）执行，替代传统的训练循环框架，在OPT-13B SST-2任务上实现8.13倍加速，精度保持相当，并提出了将ZO更新表示为动态适配器状态、实现推理时训练的新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZO微调实现将算法嵌入传统训练循环中，但ZO算法的主要计算开销是重复的评分（类似推理），而非反向传播，导致工作量与运行时结构不匹配——算法需要结构化的推理式评分，系统却按碎片化的训练循环步骤执行，造成效率低下。

Method: 将ZO微调中的重复评分阶段提取出来，部署到vLLM推理运行时中执行，替代原有的训练循环框架。同时将ZO更新表示为动态适配器状态（类似LoRA），使轻量级适配可以像推理任务一样调度。

Result: OPT-13B SST-2上，20k步LoZO训练从4.15小时降至0.51小时（8.13x加速），最终评估准确率0.922，全验证准确率0.931。OPT-1.3B到OPT-13B的核心步扩展实验获得2.34x-7.72x加速。MeZO风格的高秩分解实验获得高达2.55x加速且损失轨迹可追踪。

Conclusion: ZO微调本质是推理主导的负载，应通过推理运行时执行；将ZO更新表示为动态适配器状态为推理时训练提供了可行路径，轻量级适配可像推理任务一样调度而非独立训练任务。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) fine-tuning is attractive for large language models because it replaces backpropagation with forward objective evaluations. Existing implementations nevertheless execute ZO algorithms inside conventional training loops, even though their dominant work is repeated scoring under nearby parameter states. This creates a workload-runtime mismatch: the algorithm asks for structured inference-style scoring, while the system exposes a sequence of fragmented training-loop steps. We show that LLM ZO fine-tuning is an inference-dominated workload and execute its repeated scoring phase through a serving runtime. On OPT-13B SST-2, the resulting vLLM execution path completes the 20k-step LoZO run in 0.51 estimated training hours versus 4.15 hours for the official LoZO baseline under the matched LoRA-only setting, an 8.13x speedup, while reaching 0.922 final evaluation accuracy and 0.931 final full-validation accuracy. In core-step scaling experiments across OPT-1.3B to OPT-13B, the same runtime reorganization gives 2.34x--7.72x speedups. A MeZO-style high-rank factorized experiment shows that the same runtime paradigm can track a MeZO-like loss trajectory while running up to 2.55x faster. More broadly, representing ZO updates as dynamic adapter states suggests a practical path toward inference-time training, where lightweight adaptation can be scheduled as an inference-like workload rather than as a separate training job.

</details>


### [94] [Multi-Mixer Models: Flexible Sequence Modeling with Shared Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28769)
*Kevin Y. Li,Asher Trockman,Ananda Theertha Suresh,Ziteng Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Oryx是一种新型混合模型，能够在序列维度上灵活切换注意力机制和线性递归模式，二者共享至少90%的参数。实验表明，在1.4B规模下，Oryx在所有实例上均优于单一混合器基线，且在仅使用不到10%token的注意力模式下即可达到与Transformer相当的检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力在长序列上存在内存线性增长和计算二次增长的问题；线性递归模型虽高效但在长上下文检索和上下文学习任务上表现不足。现有混合架构仅静态交错或合并注意力与递归模块，未能充分利用二者的互补优势。

Method: 提出Oryx混合模型，在序列维度上动态切换不同的混合器（如quadratic注意力用于丰富上下文利用，线性递归用于高效生成）。模型至少90%的参数在混合器间共享，使注意力和递归模式在共享内部表示上运行。基于Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet变体验证，规模达1.4B。

Result: 在固定token预算和混合训练策略下，Oryx性能与单一混合器基线相当或更优。1.4B规模下，所有Oryx实例在平均语言建模任务上比基线高出至少0.7个百分点。检索任务中，仅用不到10%的token以注意力模式处理即可达到与Transformer基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 注意力与线性递归模型可以共享内部表示，序列轴混合是一种有前景的发展方向。

Abstract: Softmax attention is the cornerstone of modern large language models, but its memory scales linearly and compute quadratically with sequence length. Linear recurrent models, such as linear attention and state space models, have become widely studied as alternatives to attention due to their linear compute and constant memory. While these sub-quadratic token mixing methods, or mixers, achieve promising efficiency gains and competitive results on a wide range of benchmarks, current linear recurrent models still lag behind on tasks that require long-context retrieval or in-context learning. A growing body of work studies hybrid architectures that attempt to mitigate these trade-offs by statically interleaving or merging attention and recurrent blocks. In this work, we explore a new axis of developing hybrid models: across the token sequence. We propose Oryx, a hybrid model that can, throughout a sequence, flexibly switch between different mixers, for example quadratic attention for rich context utilization and linear recurrences for efficient generation. Oryx ties at least 90% of its parameters across mixers, enabling attention and recurrent modes to operate over shared internal representations. We validate our design with Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet variants, up to 1.4B models. Under fixed token budgets and a mixed-training strategy, Oryx achieves comparable or better performance than its single-mixer baselines. At the 1.4B scale, all instances of Oryx outperform their respective baselines by at least 0.7 percentage points on averaged language modeling tasks. On retrieval tasks, Oryx achieves performance comparable to the Transformer baseline even when processing only a tiny fraction (<10%) of the tokens in attention mode. These results suggest that attention and linear recurrent models can share internal representations, and motivate sequence-axis hybridization as a promising direction.

</details>


### [95] [Learn from Weaknesses: Automated Domain Specialization for Small Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28775)
*Suji Kim,Kangsan Kim,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LearnWeak框架，利用更强的参考智能体识别小型计算机使用智能体的弱点，合成针对性任务并自动构建监督信号，通过解耦规划与执行错误的感知目标实现更精确的模型更新，在OSWorld基准上平均提升11.6和11.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为每个软件领域部署大型专家模型成本高昂，而小型开源计算机使用智能体虽更具实用性和专门化潜力，但性能显著较弱且存在不均匀的领域特定失败。简单的解决方案（大规模合成目标领域训练数据）效果甚微，亟需更高效的专门化方法。

Method: LearnWeak框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 利用更强的参考智能体识别学生模型在目标领域的弱点；(2) 根据识别出的弱点合成针对性任务；(3) 自动构建监督信号，并引入错误感知的专门化目标函数，将规划错误与执行错误解耦，实现比统一监督更精确的行为更新。

Result: 在OSWorld基准的8个领域上，LearnWeak相比EvoCUA-8B平均提升11.6个百分点，相比OpenCUA-7B平均提升11.1个百分点。同时验证了学生感知的数据生成和训练方法优于现有的自主轨迹生成和训练基线。

Conclusion: 学生感知（student awareness）在数据合成和智能体训练中均至关重要，LearnWeak为在不同领域专门化小型计算机使用智能体提供了一条更原则性和高效的路径。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have recently made substantial progress, but deploying a separate large expert for each software domain remains expensive. Small open computer-use agents are more practical specialization targets, but they remain substantially weaker and exhibit uneven domain-specific failures. A straightforward remedy is to synthesize large-scale training data for the target domain, yet we find that this naive approach yields only marginal improvements. Building on this observation, we introduce LearnWeak, an annotation-free specialization framework for small computer-use agents that uses a stronger reference agent to identify the student's weaknesses in the target domain, synthesize targeted tasks, and construct supervision automatically. LearnWeak further introduces an error-aware specialization objective that disentangles planning and execution errors, enabling more behaviorally precise updates than broad uniform supervision. On OSWorld, LearnWeak achieves average gains of 11.6 and 11.1 percentage points over EvoCUA-8B and OpenCUA-7B, respectively, across eight domains. We also validate that our student-aware dataset generation and training approaches outperform existing autonomous trajectory generation and training baselines. Our work highlights the importance of student awareness in both data synthesis and agent training, pointing toward a more principled and efficient path for specializing small computer-use agents in diverse domains.

</details>


### [96] [Affective Music Recommendation: A Rollout-Based World Model for Offline Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28810)
*Audrey Chan,Aaron Labbé,Jacob Lavoie,Jordan Bannister,Arsène Fansi Tchango,Guillaume Lajoie,Laurent Charlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMRS（情感音乐推荐系统），针对在线实验在情感层面存在伦理约束（尤其临床人群）的问题，构建了一个基于因果Transformer的卷展世界模型，利用离线日志数据联合预测参与度、评分和情感维度（效价与 arousal），并采用直接偏好优化（DPO）进行离线策略微调，在冷启动条件下实现了可用的情感信号预测，避免分布式坍缩，为情感推荐在伦理受限场景下的应用提供了早期部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能性音乐应用（从消费级睡眠辅助到临床干预）面临一个独特的推荐挑战：成功与否由用户的情感状态定义，但在情感层面上进行在线实验存在伦理约束，尤其对于临床人群（如神经认知障碍的老年用户），他们无法可靠地跳过歌曲或报告不适感，因此需要一种无需在线情感实验即可优化推荐效果的方法。

Method: AMRS构建了一个基于因果Transformer的卷展世界模型（rollout-based world model），在LUCID健康与 wellness 平台上部署，服务于临床用户（主要为神经认知障碍的老年群体）和消费级 wellness 用户（能量、专注、平静、睡眠四种模式）。世界模型利用离线收听日志数据联合预测用户参与度、二元评分以及自我报告的效价（valence）和唤醒度（arousal）。该模型作为离线策略训练的模拟器和部署前的压力测试工具。推荐策略初始通过行为克隆（behavior cloning）初始化，随后使用直接偏好优化（DPO）针对可配置的多目标效用函数进行离线微调。

Result: 在严格的冷启动协议下，世界模型能够以可用的保真度预测行为和情感信号。相比行为克隆基线，DPO在保持相似多样性分布的同时提升了预测的效价和唤醒度，并且避免了贪婪优化产生的分布式坍缩（distributional collapse）。

Conclusion: 该工作定位为情感推荐在在线实验伦理不可行场景下的早期部署验证，证明了基于离线世界模型和DPO的多目标优化方法可在实际健康与 wellness 平台中有效运作。

Abstract: Functional music applications, from consumer focus and sleep aids to clinical interventions, share a distinctive recommendation problem: success is defined by the listener's affective state, but online experimentation on emotion is ethically constrained, particularly for clinical populations who cannot reliably skip a song or report distress. We describe AMRS, the Affective Music Recommendation System deployed on LUCID's health-and-wellness platforms, which serve clinical users (primarily older adults with neurocognitive conditions) and consumer-wellness users across energize, focus, calm, and sleep modes. AMRS is built around a rollout-based world model: a causal transformer trained on logged listening data to jointly predict engagement, binary rating, and self-reported valence and arousal. The world model serves both as an in-silico simulator for offline policy training and as a stress-testing tool before deployment. A recommender policy initialized by behaviour cloning is fine-tuned offline with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) against a configurable multi-objective utility function. Under a strict cold-start protocol, the world model predicts both behavioural and affective signals with usable fidelity; DPO improves predicted valence and arousal over the cloned baseline while maintaining a similar diversity profile and avoiding the distributional collapse produced by greedy optimization. We position the work as an early deployed validation of a methodology for affective recommendation when online experimentation is ethically untenable.

</details>


### [97] [PEFT-Arena: Understanding Parameter-Efficient Finetuning from a Stability-Plasticity Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28819)
*Yangyi Huang,Ruotian Peng,Zeju Qiu,Jiale Kang,Yandong Wen,Bernhard Schölkopf,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PEFT-Arena基准，从稳定性-可塑性困境角度评估参数高效微调方法，发现正交微调在帕累托前沿上表现最优，并通过权重空间和激活空间的几何分析解释了不同PEFT方法的遗忘机制，最后提出路径回退的后处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT评估过于侧重下游任务准确率，忽略了预训练能力的保持。作者主张应从稳定性-可塑性困境（即目标任务适应与遗忘抵抗之间的权衡）来评估PEFT方法。

Method: 1) 提出PEFT-Arena基准，同时测量下游性能和通用能力保持。2) 从权重空间（谱分析）和激活空间（表示保真度）两个几何视角分析PEFT更新的影响。3) 发现SFT最终检查点通常超出最优目标-保持权衡点，提出路径回退作为后处理改进方法。

Result: 不同PEFT方法展现出不同的稳定性-可塑性特征；在可比参数预算下，正交微调实现了最优的帕累托前沿；遗忘与非等距表示失真相关。

Conclusion: PEFT评估应兼顾下游性能和能力保持，正交微调在权衡上更优，路径回退可作为后处理手段改善目标-保持平衡。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning (PEFT) has become the standard approach for adapting large language models, yet evaluations largely emphasize downstream accuracy while overlooking the retention of pretrained capabilities. We argue that PEFT should be assessed through the stability-plasticity dilemma: the trade-off between target-task adaptation and resistance to forgetting. We introduce PEFT-Arena, a benchmark that jointly measures downstream performance and general capability retention. Across methods, we find distinct stability-plasticity profiles; under comparable parameter budgets, orthogonal finetuning achieves the most favorable Pareto frontier. To explain these differences, we analyze PEFT updates from two geometric perspectives. In weight space, spectral analysis reveals how parameterizations interact with the pretrained singular-value structure. In activation space, retention metrics show whether finetuning preserves or distorts general-capability representations, with forgetting linked to non-isometric representation distortion. Finally, an analysis shows that final SFT checkpoints often overshoot a better target-retention operating point. Inspired by this, we present case studies of a post-hoc improvement with path-wise rewinding.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [A Factory-Floor Deployment Case Study of VLA Pipelines for Industrial Packaging Task: Workflow, Failures, and Lessons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27461)
*Brian Zhu,Philipp Schmitt,Philine Meister,Lukas Gensler,Momen Khalil,Emmanuele Poggi,Johannes Hechtl,Carsten Braunroth,Kai Wurm,Gokul Narayanan,Eugen Solowjow,Georg von Wichert,Andre Scholz,Felix Albrecht,Maxmillian Metzner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报告了在西门子工厂部署VLA策略（Pi0.5）完成工业包装任务的实证研究，通过2535个episode（10小时）的迭代微调和部署驱动改进，揭示了实际部署中的常见失败模式和教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在实际工业部署中面临可靠性挑战，现有研究缺乏对工厂环境下部署工作流的实证理解，因此需要探索如何将预训练策略适配到单一工厂任务中。

Method: 采用迭代式微调与部署驱动改进的流程，包括数据收集、数据筛选、微调、评估和针对性的恢复数据收集的多次循环，在西门子工厂现场采集了2535个episode（10小时）数据。

Result: 积累了2535个episode的工厂现场数据；识别了实际部署中反复出现的失败模式；提出了改进部署工作流的经验教训。

Conclusion: 工厂环境下VLA策略的部署需要持续的迭代微调和部署驱动改进，实际部署中存在特有的失败模式，本研究为工业VLA部署提供了有价值的实证经验。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have shown promising manipulation capabilities, yet their practical impact is often limited by the reliability demands of real-world deployment. We present a deployment study of an industrial packaging task at Siemens Factory (GWE, Erlangen, Germany), where a robot must pick a transparent accessory bag from a cluttered pile, insert it into the remaining cavity of a cardboard package, and ensure that the bag and its contents remain below the closing plane. Our goal is to understand the practical effort required to adapt a pretrained Pi0.5 policy to a single factory-floor task through iterative fine-tuning and deployment-driven refinement. The pipeline consists of repeated loops of data collection, curation, fine-tuning, evaluation, and targeted recovery data collection. We have accumulated 2535 episodes (10 hours) from the on-site factory settings. In this paper, we contribute an empirical account of a factory-floor VLA deployment, highlighting recurring failure modes and lessons that inform how to improve the deployment workflow.

</details>


### [99] [GE-Sim 2.0: A Roadmap Towards Comprehensive Closed-loop Video World Simulators for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27491)
*Boxiang Qiu,Liliang Chen,Yue Liao,Nan Wang,Lintao Wang,Jiayi Luo,Wenzhi Zhao,Shengcong Chen,Di Chen,Ye Li,Chen Gao,Shuicheng Yan,Si Liu,Maoqing Yao,Guanghui Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GE-Sim 2.0是一个面向机器人操作的闭环视频世界模拟器，基于动作条件视频生成框架，在数千小时真实机器人数据上重新训练，新增状态专家、世界评判者和加速框架三个模块，以2B参数规模在WorldArena排行榜上超越专用模型和闭源视频生成器，支持策略学习并可转化为真实世界性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人世界模型在动作跟随精度、轨迹覆盖范围以及从视频模拟到策略学习的闭环能力上存在不足，需要一种可扩展、可闭环的高保真视频世界模拟器来支撑机器人操作策略的评估与学习。

Method: 1) 基于Genie Envisioner的动作条件视频生成框架，在数千小时真实机器人数据（遥操作、接触交互、策略部署）上重新训练；2) 引入状态专家(State Expert)从视频隐空间中解码本体感知状态，支持下游VLA策略的下一块预测；3) 引入世界评判者(World Judge)根据任务指令对生成轨迹评分，提供机器可验证的成功信号和奖励；4) 加速框架实现单H100上25帧/2.3秒的推理速度，支持最多4倍跳帧的长期评估。

Result: 仅2B参数即在公开WorldArena排行榜上位居第一，超越专用机器人世界模型和闭源通用视频生成器；基于其生成轨迹和奖励训练的策略可转化为真实的物理世界性能提升。

Conclusion: GE-Sim 2.0建立了从视频模拟到策略学习的完整闭环，为机器人操作策略的可扩展评估与闭环学习提供了一个实用平台。

Abstract: We introduce GE-Sim 2.0 (Genie Envisioner World Simulator 2.0), a closed-loop video world simulator for robotic manipulation. Building on the action-conditioned video generation framework of Genie Envisioner, GE-Sim 2.0 is re-trained on thousands of hours of real-world robot data spanning teleoperation, contact-rich interaction, and on-robot policy deployment, substantially improving action-following fidelity and trajectory coverage. On top of this foundation, three new modules close the loop from video simulation to policy learning: a state expert that decodes proprioceptive state from video latents to support next-chunk prediction by downstream VLA policies; a world judge that scores generated rollouts against task instructions, yielding machine-verifiable success signals and rewards in place of manual inspection; and an acceleration framework that delivers a 25-frame rollout in 2.3 seconds on a single H100, with up to 4* frame skipping at inference for long-horizon evaluation. GE-Sim 2.0 tops the public WorldArena leaderboard at only 2B parameters, outperforming both dedicated robotic world models and closed-source general video generators, and policies trained against its rollouts and rewards translate into measurable real-world gains, establishing GE-Sim 2.0 as a practical platform for scalable evaluation and closed-loop learning of manipulation policies.

</details>


### [100] [SCALE-COMM: Shared, Contrastively-Aligned Latent Embeddings for MARL Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27532)
*Mahmoud Abouelyazid,Eman Hammad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SCALE-COMM框架，通过自监督对比对齐学习解耦通信与策略优化，在分散式多智能体强化学习中学习紧凑、稳定且与策略相关的潜在通信表征，显著提升了协调稳定性、样本效率和任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有涌现通信方法存在通信协议不稳定、消息语义无根基、通信学习与策略优化相互干扰等问题，导致协调性能随时间退化。

Method: 提出SCALE-COMM框架，通过自监督对比对齐学习低维潜在消息，解耦通信学习与策略优化，跨智能体和时间步强制表征一致性。

Result: 在标准MARL基准和真实仓库协调任务中，SCALE-COMM在表征质量和任务性能上均优于现有通信框架，并在策略微调下展现出更好的稳定性、样本效率和吞吐量。

Conclusion: 表征驱动的通信方法能够有效提升多智能体协调的可扩展性，通过解耦通信学习与策略优化解决了现有方法的稳定性问题。

Abstract: Emergent communication enables partially observant Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs) to coordinate effectively in decentralized multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) settings. However, existing approaches often struggle with unstable communication protocols, ungrounded message semantics, and interference between communication learning and policy optimization, leading to degraded coordination over time. We propose SCALE-COMM (Shared, Contrastively-Aligned Latent Embeddings for COMMunication), a self-supervised framework for learning compact, stable, and policy-relevant communication representations. SCALE-COMM decouples communication learning from policy optimization by training low-dimensional latent messages that capture task-relevant planning and traffic information, while enforcing consistency across agents and time. Across standard MARL benchmarks and a realistic warehouse coordination task, SCALE-COMM consistently outperforms existing communication frameworks in both representation quality and task performance. The learned communication space yields improved stability, sample efficiency, and throughput under policy fine-tuning, demonstrating the effectiveness of representation-driven communication for scalable multi-agent coordination.

</details>


### [101] [Inducing Calmness With Pocket-Sized Robotics: Reducing Movement and Heart Rate in Children through Hand-Held Tactile Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27533)
*Morten Roed Frederiksen,Kasper Støy,Maja Matarić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了一种手持触觉设备（节奏振动匹配游戏）对典型发育儿童生理和行为层面的镇静效果。实验发现，触觉交互可显著降低儿童心率（平均降低3.56 bpm）和身体躁动（整体运动减少38%），尤其是注意力相关身体区域活动减少45%，表明短时触觉游戏互动能有效下调生理唤醒并促进平静专注状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童的高唤醒或烦躁状态会干扰其注意力、自我调节和身体平静能力。现有技术通过触觉交互促进具身自我调节可能是一种简单易行的镇静方法，但缺乏对触觉交互如何影响全身运动与姿势稳定性的系统研究。

Method: 采用手持式节奏振动匹配游戏设备，开展18名典型发育儿童的被试内实验，设置有无触觉交互两种条件，同步记录心率和身体运动数据。

Result: 触觉游戏交互使心率显著降低3.56 bpm（p<0.01），整体运动减少38%（p<0.05），注意力相关身体区域运动减少45%。

Conclusion: 短暂的触觉游戏式手持设备交互能够下调生理唤醒，促进平静与专注状态，有助于持续注意力和行为调节。

Abstract: Periods of heightened arousal or restlessness can interfere with children's ability to focus, self-regulation, and physically calm. Technologies that encourage embodied self-regulation through tactile interaction may provide a simple and accessible means of promoting calmness. This paper investigates how interaction with a pocket-sized tactile device influences physiological and behavioral markers of calmness in typically developing children. Building on prior work examining heart rate modulation, we present new findings on how tactile interaction affects full-body movement and postural stability. We employ a device that engages children through a hand-held rhythmic vibration-matching game, designed to focus attention and encourage stillness. Eighteen children participated in a within-subjects study that involved two conditions: with and without tactile interaction with a hand-held device, while having their heart rate and body movement recorded. Results show that the tactile game interaction reduced physiological arousal (heart rate decreased by 3.56 bpm, p < 0.01) and physical restlessness (overall movement decreased by 38%, p < 0.05), with attention-related body regions showing the greatest change toward stillness (45% reduction in movement). These findings demonstrate that brief tactile game-like engagement with a hand-held device can down-regulate physiological activation, promoting the calm and focused states toward sustained attention and behavior regulation.

</details>


### [102] [Synthetic Emotions vs. Gamification: Exploring Engagement Strategies for Small Social Robots in Different Age Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27539)
*Morten Roed Frederiksen,Kasper Støy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索了触觉社交机器人在焦虑儿童日常互动中的两种参与策略（情感反馈与积分奖励），通过对6-8岁儿童（偏好评估）和20-27岁大学生（行为研究）的两项实验发现：儿童偏好情感互动，而大学生在积分系统下表现出更高的任务准确率（p<0.05）和更持续的表现，表明用户偏好与实际行为可能存在分歧，需通过观察验证设计假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多儿童在情绪调节和社交互动方面存在困难，限制了他们在日常活动和治疗项目中的参与。为了使社交辅助机器人有效运作，需要确保儿童能够持续且有意义地参与其中。本文旨在探索触觉机器人的参与策略，以支持焦虑症儿童。

Method: 设计了触觉机器人的两种参与策略：合成情感反馈（emotional feedback）和积分奖励（point rewards）。通过两项研究评估：1) 16名6-8岁学龄儿童的偏好评估；2) 14名20-27岁大学生的自然情境行为研究（全天互动）。

Result: 学龄儿童偏好情感参与而非积分奖励；大学生在全天互动中，积分系统产生显著更高的任务准确率（p<0.05）和更持续的表现。不同用户群体的结果表明：陈述性偏好与实际行为结果可能因参与情境而存在分歧。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了人机交互设计中参与策略有效性的年龄差异，强调了通过观察实际互动来验证设计假设的重要性。

Abstract: Many children experience challenges in emotional regulation and social interaction, which can limit their participation in everyday activities and therapeutic programs. For socially assistive robots to be effective in this context, it is essential that children remain consistently and meaningfully engaged. We explore engagement strategies for a tactile robot designed to support children suffering from anxiety disorders through daily interactions. The robot delivers either synthetic emotional feedback or point rewards to encourage user participation. We evaluated these strategies through two studies: a preference assessment with 16 school children aged 6-8 years, and a behavioral study with 14 university students aged 20-27 years in naturalistic environments. The study with school children indicated a preference for emotional engagement over points-based approaches. The follow up study with university students across a full day of interactions revealed contrasting results: points-based systems produced significantly higher task accuracy (p < 0.05) and sustained performance over time. Findings from different user groups suggest that stated preferences and behavioral outcomes can diverge depending on engagement context, highlighting the importance of validating design assumptions through observed interaction. This work contributes insights into age-related differences in engagement strategy effectiveness in human-robot interaction design.

</details>


### [103] [Uni-LaViRA: Language-Vision-Robot Actions Translation for Unified Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27582)
*Hongyu Ding,Sizhuo Zhang,Ziming Xu,Jinwen Guo,Hongxiu Liu,Xingzhi Cheng,Zixuan Chen,Haifei Qi,Duo Wang,Hao Xu,Jieqi Shi,Yifan Zhang,Jing Huo,Jian Cheng,Yang Gao,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-LaViRA是一种无需训练的统一导航智能体架构，将语言-视觉-机器人动作翻译（LaViRA）作为导航的核心决策结构，利用预训练多模态大语言模型的自然输出流形，在四个任务族和四种异构机器人上实现零样本导航，性能匹敌甚至超越需要大量训练数据的导航基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航方法依赖大规模机器人轨迹数据来缩放视觉-语言-动作基础模型，但本文认为导航的通用性可以通过结构获得而不仅靠数据规模。作者希望利用导航决策本身可简化为单一翻译过程这一结构特性，避免昂贵的机器人数据收集和训练。

Method: 提出Uni-LaViRA统一智能体架构，核心是将导航决策分解为语言动作（语义级方向指令）和视觉动作（像素级视觉目标），两者均在预训练多模态大语言模型的输出流形内，因此可通过推理而非学习完成。设计两个智能体循环机制：TODO列表记忆（TDM）维护结构化待办子目标清单，以及二次机会回溯（SCB）实现错误回滚与自我修正。

Result: 零训练条件下，Uni-LaViRA在VLN-CE R2R上达到60.7% SR，VLN-CE RxR达51.3%，HM3D-v2达77.7%，HM3D-OVON达60.0%，MP3D-EQA达54.7%，OpenUAV达40.0%，匹敌甚至超越消耗数百万样本和数千GPU小时训练的最新导航基础模型。

Conclusion: 导航任务的结构化特性可以被显式利用，通过统一架构和推理机制替代大规模数据训练，证明了零样本跨任务、跨机器人泛化的可行性。

Abstract: Embodied navigation requires an agent to map language and visual observations to a stream of spatial actions that drive a real robot through environments it has never seen. The dominant approach has been to scale vision-language-action (VLA) foundation models on ever-larger collections of robot trajectories. This paper argues that, for navigation specifically, generality can be obtained structurally, not only through data scale. The underlying decision structure of navigation reduces to a single Language-Vision-Robot Actions Translation. The language action emits semantic-level directional command and the vision action emits a pixel-level visual target. Both outputs lie inside the natural output manifold of pretrained multimodal large language models (MLLMs), so the task can be reasoned about by an agent rather than learned from robot data. Therefore, we present Uni-LaViRA, a unified agentic architecture that extends the same insight to four task families (VLN-CE, ObjectNav, EQA, and Aerial-VLN) and to four heterogeneous real robots (Wheeled, Quadruped, Humanoid robot, and a self-built UAV) in a zero-shot manner. Two agent-loop mechanisms make this unification practical. TODO List Memory (TDM) rewrites a structured checklist of pending sub-goals at every step, reciting the unfinished items back into the agent's most recent attention window. Second Chance Backtrack (SCB) rolls the robot back to the pre-error state and conditions the agent's next plan on the failed sub-trajectory, turning single-pass navigation into a self-correcting process. With zero training effort, Uni-LaViRA reaches 60.7% SR on VLN-CE R2R, 51.3% on VLN-CE RxR, 77.7% on HM3D-v2, 60.0% on HM3D-OVON, 54.7% on MP3D-EQA, and 40.0% on OpenUAV, matching or even surpassing recent training navigation foundation models that consume millions of samples and thousands of GPU-hours.

</details>


### [104] [Agentic Language-to-Objective Synthesis for Optofluidic Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27643)
*Ivan Saraev,Elena Erben,Weida Liao,Fan Nan,Gerhard Neumann,Eric Lauga,Moritz Kreysing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Speak-to-Objective模块化智能体流水线，利用大语言模型将自然语言指令转化为可微目标函数，结合逆向求解器和光流控平台实现微粒的自动化组装，构建了从意图到驱动的闭环数字制造工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光基先进制造中，将人类设计意图转化为可执行机器指令存在瓶颈，目标函数的手动设定方式繁琐、难以复用，缺乏可编程、闭环的自动化工具。

Method: 提出Speak-to-Objective模块化智能体流水线，核心为“感知→组合→提议→执行→报告与学习”闭环，使用条件化LLM将自然语言指令翻译为可微目标函数，通过约束感知逆向求解器(SLSQP)和实验光流控平台驱动激光诱导热黏性流实现微粒组装。

Result: 实现了自然语言可编程的微观粒子图案组装，能够从部分轨迹恢复、抗扰动，支持精确放置，且与具体执行器无关。

Conclusion: 该工作为面向自主AI辅助光基制造平台奠定了基础，将自然语言、可微目标和激光驱动耦合为可复用的数字工作流，推动自驱动光制造平台的发展。

Abstract: Light-based advanced manufacturing increasingly requires programmable, closed-loop tools that translate human design intent into executable operations at small length scales. Yet a key bottleneck persists across robotic and manufacturing modalities: turning user intent into machine-readable objectives that are reliably executable. While micro-robotics offers versatile manipulation via optical actuation of fluids, mathematically tractable goal specification remains manual and hard to reuse. Here, we introduce Speak-to-Objective, a modular agentic pipeline that uses a conditioned Large Language Model (LLM) to translate spoken or written commands into fully differentiable objective functions for assembling microparticles in a constraint-aware inverse solver (SLSQP) and on an experimental optofluidic platform. The approach employs a compact loop - perceive -> compose -> propose -> act -> report & learn - that treats the objective as the interface between intent and actuation, separating what to assemble or pattern from how to actuate, while learning from user feedback. The pipeline composes geometry, spacing, and assignment/topology terms to generate robust descriptive objectives that assemble from partial traces and recover after perturbations, as well as explicit objectives for precise placement, all in an actuator-agnostic fashion. Using laser-induced thermoviscous flows as the physical actuation modality, we demonstrate natural-language-programmable, light-based microscale assembly of particle patterns in a microfluidic environment. Beyond its immediate impact on programmable microassembly, and using laser-induced optofluidic actuation as a reduced-complexity experimental platform, our work points toward self-driving, AI-assisted optical manufacturing platforms in which natural language, differentiable objectives, and laser-based actuation are coupled into a reusable digital workflow.

</details>


### [105] [Simulation-Informed Diffusion for Decentralized Multi-robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27697)
*Jinhao Liang,Sven Koenig,Ferdinando Fioretto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出仿真信息扩散（SID），一种基于约束感知扩散模型（CADM）的去中心化多机器人运动规划框架。SID首先利用CADM从当前观测状态模拟邻接机器人的未来轨迹，再基于这些模拟信息在安全约束下规划自身轨迹。该方法在多达108台机器人和160个障碍物的场景中，在规划有效性和约束满足方面优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化多机器人运动规划方法（无论是经典方法还是基于学习的方法）都仅从局部观测的静态快照生成轨迹，无法预判邻接机器人的未来行为。随着机器人数量增加和环境变得拥挤，这一局限性变得尤为关键。

Method: SID框架包含两个阶段：首先使用约束感知扩散模型（CADM）从邻接机器人的当前观测状态模拟其未来轨迹；然后利用同一CADM在模拟信息提供的安全约束下规划自身轨迹。该方法还设计了一种最小化通信机制，仅在高度拥挤场景下触发协调。

Result: 在多种环境下的实验表明，SID在规划有效性和约束满足方面一致优于基线方法，并能扩展到108台机器人和160个障碍物的复杂场景。

Conclusion: 通过引入仿真信息来预判邻居行为，SID有效克服了传统方法依赖静态观测的局限性，显著提升了去中心化多机器人运动规划在拥挤环境下的可扩展性和安全性。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-robot motion planning requires each robot to generate collision-free trajectories from local observations, without global sensing or reliable communication. However, most existing planners, whether classical or learning-based, generate trajectories from a static snapshot of the local observation, which limits their ability to anticipate the future behavior of neighboring robots. This limitation is critical as the number of robots increases and the environment becomes more cluttered. To overcome this challenge, this paper introduces Simulation-Informed Diffusion (SID), a decentralized framework built on constraint-aware diffusion models (CADM). SID first uses CADM to simulate the future trajectories of neighboring robots from their currently observed states, and then uses the same CADM to plan each robot's own trajectory under safety constraints informed by these simulations. Crucially, the accurate simulation of neighbors enables a minimal communication scheme that triggers coordination only when necessary in highly congested scenarios. Experiments across diverse environments show that SID consistently outperforms baseline methods in terms of planning effectiveness and constraint satisfaction, and scales to scenarios with 108 robots and 160 obstacles.

</details>


### [106] [AURA: Asymptotically Optimal Uncertainty-Robust Replanning Algorithm for Kinodynamic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27699)
*Seyedali Golestaneh,Zhuoyun Zhong,Donghyung Lee,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为\method的渐进最优元规划框架，通过在线重规划和优化控制输入，在运动规划执行过程中同时提升轨迹质量和跟踪精度，适用于存在运动不确定性的高维、欠驱动或非完整系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于采样的运动规划器通常只能离线使用，需在轨迹计算完成后才开始执行；且存在运动不确定性时，规划轨迹难以被精确跟踪，导致偏离名义解。

Method: 提出了一个统一的元规划框架\method，包含三个组件：主执行线程、持续探索状态空间并细化轨迹的在线重规划方法，以及优化未来控制输入以减少跟踪误差的优化过程。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的多个系统上进行了评估，与基线方法相比，在轨迹质量、跟踪精度和整体性能方面均展现出一致改进。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在线利用渐进最优规划，同时在不确定性条件下提高执行精度，有效解决了离线规划与轨迹跟踪偏差的问题。

Abstract: Sampling-based motion planners offer a practical and scalable approach to kinodynamic motion planning, notably for high-dimensional, underactuated, or non-holonomic systems. However, these planners are typically used offline, requiring execution to begin only after the trajectory has been computed. In addition, the planned trajectory may not be accurately tracked in the presence of motion uncertainty, leading to deviations from the nominal solution. In this work, these limitations were addressed within a unified framework, \method, an asymptotically-optimal meta-planner framework that improves both path quality and tracking performance during execution. In addition to the main execution thread, this framework comprises a replanning method that continuously explores the state space and refines the trajectory during execution, and an optimization process that refines future control inputs to reduce tracking error. Together, these components enable \method to leverage asymptotically optimal planning online while improving execution accuracy under uncertainty. The proposed approach is evaluated in both simulation and real-world environments across multiple systems, demonstrating consistent improvements in trajectory quality, tracking accuracy, and overall performance compared with baseline methods.

</details>


### [107] [Turning Video Models into Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27817)
*Sizhe Lester Li,Evan Kim,Xingjian Bai,Tong Zhao,Tao Pang,Max Simchowitz,Vincent Sitzmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VERA框架，将视频生成模型（视频规划器）与逆动力学模型（IDM）解耦。视频规划器保持无关具身，IDM基于具身雅可比矩阵设计，实现从视频到动作的映射。该方法在零样本Panda机械臂操作和16自由度Allegro手灵巧操作等任务上表现优异，支持跨具身迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将视频模型与动作标签数据联合微调作为机器人基础模型，本文探索替代路线：保留视频规划器不变，仅训练具身特化的逆动力学模型（IDM），以实现具身无关、模块可互换、利用自对弈数据独立训练等优势。

Method: 提出VERA（Video-to-Embodied Robot Action Model），采用闭环视频到动作策略，包含：（1）无动作视频世界模型作为规划器；（2）基于机器人具身雅可比矩阵精心设计的IDM，实现数据高效和可扩展到高维动作空间。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界基准测试中表现强劲，包括零样本Panda机械臂操作和16自由度Allegro手灵巧立方体重定向。同一视频规划器可通过不同具身IDM实现多具身迁移。

Conclusion: 解耦的视频规划加上忠实的视频到动作翻译，是实现零样本、跨具身、可泛化机器人控制的可行替代路线。

Abstract: Video generative models have emerged as a promising robotics backbone, capable of generating videos that depict the completion of complex tasks across embodiments and environments. Recent work proposes robot foundation models that jointly predict future observations and actions by finetuning video models with action-labeled data. In this paper, we test the limits of an alternative approach: leave the video planner as-is while training an embodiment-specific inverse dynamics model (IDM). This decoupling offers several natural benefits: the video planner remains embodiment-agnostic, different video models can be interchanged easily without re-training the IDM, and the IDM can be independently trained with readily available self-play data. We present a closed-loop, video-to-action policy that combines an action-free video world model with a carefully-designed IDM based on the robot embodiment Jacobian. We demonstrate that our IDM design is both data-efficient and scalable to high-dimensional action spaces. Our policy, which we coin the Video-to-Embodied Robot Action Model (VERA), achieves strong performance across simulated and real-world benchmarks, including zero-shot Panda arm manipulation and 16-DoF Allegro-hand dexterous cube re-orientation. The same video planner can be used across multiple embodiments by pairing it with different embodiment-specific IDMs. Our results show that decoupled video planning plus faithful video-to-action translation is a viable alternative route towards zero-shot, cross-embodiment, and generalizable robot control. More results are available on our project website: https://vera.csail.mit.edu.

</details>


### [108] [Tabero: Learning Gentle Manipulation with Closed-Loop Force Feedback from Vision, Touch, and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27886)
*Qiwei Wu,Rui Zhang,Xin Xiang,Tao Li,Weihua Zhang,Junjie Lai,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tabero是一个面向机器人温柔操作的基准和模型套件，通过解耦力-位置命令接口和混合控制器，在保持高任务成功率的同时，将平均握力降低了70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型因缺乏对齐的视觉-触觉-语言数据和有效的闭环力反馈机制，难以利用触觉反馈实现类人的温柔操作。

Method: 1）Tabero基准：通过数据高效流水线复用开源机器人操作轨迹生成多样化视觉-触觉-语言任务，建立多维评估协议；2）Tabero-VTLA架构：采用解耦力-位置命令接口，由固定混合控制器执行，实现实时力感知操作。

Result: 在温柔指令下，模型保持高任务成功率的同时，平均握力降低超过70%。

Conclusion: Tabero证明了基于多模态经验调节交互力的可行性，为机器人温柔操作提供了有效的数据和模型方案。

Abstract: Tactile sensing is essential for robots to achieve human-like gentle manipulation. However, existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models struggle to exploit tactile feedback for gentle manipulation due to scarce aligned vision-tactile-language data and the lack of effective closed-loop force feedback mechanisms. To address these challenges, we introduce Tabero, a benchmark and model suite for gentle, language-conditioned robotic manipulation that demands fine-grained contact force perception. First, the Tabero benchmark addresses the scarcity of tactile data by presenting a data-efficient pipeline that repurposes open-source robot manipulation trajectories to generate diverse vision-tactile-language tasks, and establishes a multidimensional evaluation protocol that measures task success alongside physical interaction quality. Second, we propose Tabero-VTLA, an architecture with a decoupled force-position command interface; the resulting force-position commands are executed by a fixed hybrid controller to enable real-time, force-aware manipulation. Evaluated on Tabero, our model maintains high task success while reducing average grip force by over 70\% under gentle instructions, demonstrating its ability to modulate interaction forces based on multimodal experience. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/NathanWu7/Tabero.

</details>


### [109] [S-Cheetah: A Novel Quadrupedal Robot with a 3-DOF Active Spine Learning Agile Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27909)
*Zimu Li,Weibang Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了S-Cheetah四足机器人，其核心创新在于一个3自由度的仿生串联主动脊柱，能够实现生物脊柱的空间三轴旋转。通过专门设计的强化学习框架（包含加速度课程学习、奔跑步态奖励、脊柱波动奖励和脊柱转向奖励），S-Cheetah实现了6.9 m/s的峰值速度（旋转G2奔跑步态）、7.2 rad/s的原位转向速率，并展现出猫科动物启发的空中自翻正能力。实验证明，3-DOF脊柱全面增强了四足机器人的运动敏捷性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足机器人研究中，虽然已集成主动脊柱关节以提升敏捷性，但多数设计通过减少脊柱自由度来简化控制复杂性，未能实现生物脊柱的空间三轴旋转特征。因此，复现多自由度仿生脊柱并有效利用它来赋能四足机器人敏捷运动仍是一个重要的研究挑战。

Method: 提出了S-Cheetah机器人，配备3-DOF仿生串联主动脊柱实现空间三轴旋转。开发了专门的强化学习框架，通过加速度课程学习策略与定制奖励函数（奔跑步态奖励、脊柱波动奖励、脊柱转向奖励）来主动促进脊柱参与运动，最大化机器人运动能力。

Result: S-Cheetah实现6.9 m/s峰值速度（旋转G2奔跑步态），7.2 rad/s原位转向速率；展现出猫科动物启发的空中自翻正能力，能从任意方向自由落体稳定四足着陆；通过多项运动任务评估证明3-DOF脊柱全面增强了四足机器人运动敏捷性。

Conclusion: 提出的3-DOF仿生主动脊柱能够全面增强四足机器人的运动敏捷性，包括高速奔跑、快速转向和空中自翻正等能力，证明了多自由度脊柱在仿生四足机器人中的关键价值。

Abstract: The biological spine of quadrupeds enables sagittal flexion/extension, lateral bending, and axial rotation, playing a crucial role in highly agile and dexterous locomotion. While numerous studies have integrated active spinal joints into quadrupedal robots to enhance agility, most designs simplify control complexity by reducing spinal degrees of freedom (DOF), failing to achieve the spatial tri-axial rotation characteristic of biological spines. Consequently, replicating a multi-DOF biomimetic spine and effectively leveraging it to empower the agile locomotion of quadrupedal robots remains a significant research challenge. In this study, we present S-Cheetah, a quadrupedal robot featuring a 3-DOF bio-inspired serial active spine capable of biomimetic spatial tri-axial rotation. To empower the robot to fully utilize this active spine, we developed a specialized reinforcement learning framework to actively promote the engagement of the introduced spine and maximize the robot's locomotive capabilities by integrating an acceleration curriculum learning strategy with tailored reward functions, such as a gallop gait reward, a spine undulation reward, and a spine steering reward. Experimental results demonstrate that S-Cheetah can achieve a peak speed of 6.9 m/s using the rotary G2 gallop gait and an in-place turning rate of 7.2 rad/s. Besides, the system exhibits an emergent, feline-inspired aerial self-righting capability, allowing it to land stably on four feet from arbitrary orientations during free fall. Finally, through extensive evaluations across diverse locomotion tasks, we prove that the introduction of the proposed 3-DOF spine comprehensively enhances the locomotive agility of quadrupedal robots. Project website: himmy-robotics.github.io/scheetah

</details>


### [110] [Frequency-Guided Action Diffusion via Sub-Frequency Manifold Traversal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27919)
*Junlin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出频率引导算子(FGO)，通过渐进式频谱扩展引导扩散策略生成更平滑的动作序列，在15个机器人操作任务上提升了动作平滑性和时间一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示数据中存在高频噪声（间歇性抖动、暂停、动作震颤），扩散策略的迭代去噪过程会放大这些伪影，导致模型继承次优行为，丢失精细细节。

Method: FGO通过在扩散策略生成过程中，将带噪样本逐步引导经过中间子频率流形，并不断扩展频谱带，实现隐式频谱调控，生成平滑动作。

Result: 在5个基准的15个机器人操作任务上验证，FGO在提升动作平滑度和时间一致性的同时，保留了任务执行所需的细节。

Conclusion: FGO有效解决了行为克隆中高频噪声在扩散策略中被放大的问题，实现了更优的动作生成质量，且无需改变训练数据或模型架构。

Abstract: Learning visuomotor policies via behavior cloning typically involves mimicking expert demonstrations collected by human operators. However, natural human demonstrations inherently contain high-frequency noise, such as intermittent jerks, pauses, and action jitter. Training policies to directly imitate these raw trajectories inevitably causes the model to inherit these suboptimal behaviors. This pathology is particularly pronounced in diffusion-based policies, where iterative denoising steps can inadvertently amplify high-frequency artifacts at the expense of meaningful fine-grained details. To address these limitations, we present a novel frequency-based algorithm that enables implicit spectral maneuvering and smooth action generation. Our method, Frequency Guidance Operator (FGO), steers the generation process of diffusion polices by progressively driving the noisy samples through intermediate sub-frequency manifolds with expanding spectral bands. Validated on 15 robotic manipulation tasks from 5 benchmarks, FGO achieves superior performance in enhancing action smoothness and temporal consistency while preserving the details necessary for successful task execution. Project website: https://henrywjl.github.io/frequency-guidance-operator/

</details>


### [111] [SANTS: A State-Adaptive Scheduler for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27947)
*Yirui Sun,Guangyu Zhuge,Keliang Liu,Jie Gu,Xinyu Bing,Zhongxue Gan,Chunxu Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出状态自适应噪声轨迹调度器（SANTS），通过动态选择视频扩散过程中的最优去噪深度来生成动作，避免完全去噪带来的冗余计算，在RoboTwin 2.0上实现94.4%成功率和81.7%延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素空间的世界动作模型（WAMs）中，完全去噪的视频并非最优动作条件；视频去噪对动作误差的改善存在状态依赖的饱和甚至反转点，因此需要根据状态自适应地选择去噪深度，而非固定使用终端去噪深度。

Method: 提出SANTS，一个轻量级调度器，集成于视频到动作的扩散策略中。在每个视频决策点，SANTS读取当前视频状态表示和噪声水平，联合预测累积停止风险（hazard）和相对噪声推进比（noise-progression ratio）。通过冻结动作分支生成最终动作后，基于路径级奖励（path-level reward）进行后训练，优化下游动作质量并惩罚冗余视频状态更新。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上达到94.4%总体成功率；在7个真实机器人任务上平均成功率达73.1%；相对于完全去噪，延迟分别降低81.7%和79.0%。

Conclusion: 自适应地沿视频噪声轨迹选择停止点，既能保留WAM式未来推理的控制收益，又能大幅消除冗余推理成本。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) improve robot manipulation by using video-based future representations to condition action generation. In pixel-space WAMs, however, the best action condition is not necessarily the fully denoised video. Controlled denoising-depth scans show that video refinement can reduce action error up to a state-dependent point, after which the gain may saturate or even reverse when late predictions become less action-relevant or physically unreliable. This suggests that action generation should use a state-dependent point along the video noise trajectory rather than a fixed terminal denoising depth. We introduce State-Adaptive Noise Trajectory Scheduler (SANTS), a lightweight scheduler for video-to-action diffusion policies. At each video decision point, SANTS reads the current video-state representation and noise level, then jointly predicts a cumulative stopping hazard and a relative noise-progression ratio. SANTS is post-trained with a path-level reward computed after the frozen action branch generates the final action chunk, so the scheduler is optimized for downstream action quality rather than intermediate video fidelity, while redundant video-state updates are explicitly penalized. Experiments show that SANTS reaches \(94.4\%\) overall success on RoboTwin 2.0 and \(73.1\%\) average success across seven real-robot tasks, while reducing latency by \(81.7\%\) and \(79.0\%\) relative to full video denoising, respectively. These results indicate that adaptive selection along the video noise trajectory can preserve the control benefits of WAM-style future reasoning while removing much of its redundant inference cost.

</details>


### [112] [VLM-Based Advanced Rider Assistance System for Motorcycle Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27948)
*Mohamed Elnoor,Francesca Baldini,Ananya Trivedi,Faizan M. Tariq,Jovin D'sa,David Isele,Sangjae Bae,Dinesh Manocha,Yosuke Sakamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对摩托车碰撞风险高、高级骑手辅助系统(ARAS)发展不足的问题，本文提出了一种融合视觉语言模型(VLM)语义感知与风险感知规划的ARAS系统。该系统通过VLM进行上下文危险推理，结合分割检测构建稠密风险地图，编码语义特征(如坑洼严重程度、水坑湿滑度)和物理属性(如尺寸、深度)，生成逐像素危险成本。基于采样规划的控制器根据摩托车动力学推荐油门和转向动作，在向目的地行进的同时最小化危险暴露。在CARLA模拟器中评估，相比基线方法，该方法实现了更高的成功率和更低的危险暴露，且定性结果展示了可解释的风险地图和安全轨迹推荐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩托车相比汽车面临不成比例的高碰撞风险，其保护有限且对路面危险更敏感，然而高级骑手辅助系统(ARAS)的发展远落后于高级驾驶辅助系统(ADAS)，亟需提升摩托车安全性的智能辅助技术。

Method: 1) 利用视觉语言模型(VLM)进行上下文危险推理；2) 结合分割检测构建稠密风险地图，编码语义特征(坑洼严重度、水坑湿滑度)和物理属性(尺寸、深度)，生成逐像素危险成本；3) 采用基于采样的规划器，结合摩托车动力学模型，推荐最小化危险暴露且向目的地前进的油门和转向动作。

Result: 在CARLA模拟器中，相比基线方法，本方法实现了更高的任务成功率和更低的风险暴露，定性结果显示了可解释的风险地图和安全轨迹推荐。

Conclusion: 提出的融合VLM语义感知与风险感知规划的ARAS系统能有效提升摩托车安全性，证明了结合语义理解和风险量化进行骑手辅助的可行性。

Abstract: Motorcycles face disproportionately high crash risks compared to cars due to limited protection and heightened sensitivity to surface hazards, yet Advanced Rider Assistance Systems (ARAS) remain underdeveloped relative to Advanced Driver Assistance Systems (ADAS).
