We propose a novel ARAS that enhances motorcycle safety through semantic perception and risk-aware planning. Our approach leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for contextual hazard reasoning and integrates them with segmentation-based detection to construct dense risk maps. These maps encode both semantic characteristics (e.g., pothole severity, puddle slipperiness) and physical attributes (e.g., size, depth), which produce per-pixel hazard costs that capture motorcycle-specific risks. These maps are used by a sampling-based planner tailored to motorcycle dynamics to recommend throttle and steering actions that minimize hazard exposure while advancing toward the destination. We evaluate our system in different scenarios in the CARLA simulator. Compared to the baseline method, our method achieves higher success rates and lower hazard exposure, while qualitative results demonstrate interpretable risk maps and safe trajectory recommendations.

</details>


### [113] [How Should We Teach Robots? A Comparison of Kinesthetic, Joystick, and Gesture-Based Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28033)
*Petr Vanc,Jan Kristof Behrens,Václav Hlaváč,Karla Stepanova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过用户研究比较了三种机器人示教方式（动觉引导、摇杆遥操作、手势控制）在三个操作任务中的表现，发现动觉引导在接触敏感任务中成功率最高、工作负载最低，摇杆在简单任务中表现最佳，手势控制虽整体可靠性较低但某些情况下表现接近动觉引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从示教中学习可以通过不同的教学模态实现，但不同模态在可用性和性能上存在权衡，需要系统性地比较以指导实际应用中的选择。

Method: 在8名参与者中开展用户研究，比较动觉引导、摇杆遥操作和手势控制三种示教方式，评估三类操作任务中的回放成功率、修改版NASA-TLX工作负载和常见示教错误。

Result: 动觉引导在方向敏感和接触密集的任务中示教时间最短、工作负载最低、成功率最高；摇杆遥操作在简单插桩任务中表现最佳；手势控制整体可靠性较低，但某些情况下结果与动觉引导相当。

Conclusion: 不同示教模态在不同任务类型中存在显著的性能差异，动觉引导适合复杂接触任务，摇杆适合简单任务，手势控制有潜力但需改进可靠性。

Abstract: Instructing robots from demonstrations can be done through different teaching modalities, each with different usability and performance trade-offs. This paper compares kinesthetic guidance, joystick teleoperation, and hand gestures in a user study with eight participants. We evaluate replay success, modified NASA-TLX workload, and common teaching errors across three manipulation tasks. Kinesthetic guidance produced the shortest demonstrations, lowest workload, and highest success on the more orientation-sensitive and contact-rich tasks. Joystick teleoperation performed best on simple peg picking. Hand-gesture teaching, although less reliable overall, performed better than expected and in some cases achieved results comparable to kinesthetic guidance.

</details>


### [114] [SAFEVPR: Patch-Based Conformal Verification for Safe Cross-Condition Sequence Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28048)
*Ha Sier,Jiaqiang Zhang,Zhuo Zou,Xianjia Yu,Tomi Westerlund*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAFEVPR提出了一种面向跨条件序列视觉地点识别的非训练式验证-校准流水线，通过MNN补丁匹配分数和Mondrian Conformal LTT校准，在23个跨条件设置中以目标FDR α=0.10实现了23/23的经验有效性，平均接受FDR仅0.014，平均TPR达0.75。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列VPR中，基于保形预测的接受/拒绝决策依赖可交换性假设，这在跨条件部署（如季节、光照变化）下被违反，导致标准方法失效。现有方法缺乏在分布偏移下保证统计有效性的机制。

Method: 1) 用基于冻结DINOv2 ViT特征的互近邻（MNN）补丁匹配分数替代标准余弦相似度；2) 用Mondrian Conformal LTT替代flat Learn-Then-Test校准，在每个分数分箱中拟合经Bonferroni校正的阈值，实现分区FDR控制。

Result: 在Oxford RobotCar、NCLT、St Lucia的23个跨条件设置中，SAFEVPR在目标FDR α=0.10下全部通过经验有效性检验，平均接受FDR为0.014，平均TPR为0.75。对比方法（AnyLoc-VLAD、SuperPoint+LightGlue）AUROC相当但在相同校准下失效更多。

Conclusion: 原始判别能力不足以保障保形有效性；SAFEVPR在纹理重复场景中能安全弃权而非接受不可靠匹配。代码已开源。

Abstract: Sequence-based visual place recognition (VPR) for SLAM and robot relocalization must decide whether the retrieved top-1 candidate is safe to accept. Conformal prediction is a natural framework for this accept/reject decision, but its finite-sample guarantees rely on exchangeability between calibration and deployment (test) data, which is violated under cross-condition deployment. We introduce SAFEVPR, a non-trainable verification-and-calibration pipeline for safe cross-condition sequence VPR. SAFEVPR replaces the standard backbone cosine similarity with a mutual-nearest-neighbour (MNN) patch-matching score computed from frozen DINOv2 ViT features, and replaces flat Learn-Then-Test calibration with Mondrian conformal LTT, fitting separate Bonferroni-corrected thresholds across score bins. Under exchangeability, these thresholds would provide finite-sample false-discovery-rate (FDR) control; under condition shift, we evaluate empirical validity per deployment. Across 23 cross-condition setups from Oxford RobotCar, NCLT, and St Lucia datasets, using three frozen VPR backbones, SAFEVPR is empirically valid on 23/23 setups at target FDR alpha = 0.10, achieving mean accepted FDR 0.014 and mean true-positive rate (TPR) 0.75. The results show that raw discrimination alone is not sufficient for conformal validity: AnyLoc-VLAD and Super-Point+LightGlue reach comparable area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) but fail more setups under the same calibration. On textureless repetitive scenery, SAFEVPR safely abstains rather than accepting unreliable matches. Code is available at https://github.com/Hasar12139/SafeVPR.

</details>


### [115] [Whose Is This?: Context-Aware Object Ownership Inference with Uncertainty-Guided Questioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28087)
*Saki Hashimoto,Akira Taniguchi,Shoichi Hasegawa,Yoshinobu Hagiwara,Tadahiro Taniguchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出COIN框架，利用大语言模型推断物体所有权，并引入共形预测处理不确定性，在需要时主动向用户提问。实验表明该方法在准确性和鲁棒性上显著优于基线，尤其在临时共享场景下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务机器人需要推断物体所有权以正确执行指令（如"把我的杯子拿来"），但所有权是無法直接观测的潜在属性，现有方法依赖有限线索（如近期使用记录），在临时共享等场景下不可靠。

Method: 提出COIN框架：使用大语言模型（LLM）融合用户背景信息和物体使用历史来估计所有权分数；利用共形预测（conformal prediction）构造一组合理的所有者集合；在预测不确定时选择性生成用户查询以获取额外信息。

Result: 在模拟家庭环境实验中，COIN的Subset Accuracy达0.988，Mean Jaccard index达0.991，持续优于基线方法，在临时使用和共享所有权场景下仍保持高性能。

Conclusion: 将上下文推理与不确定性感知的交互相结合，能够同时提升所有权估计的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Service robots must infer object ownership to correctly interpret instructions such as "bring me my cup." However, ownership is a latent attribute that cannot be directly observed, and existing methods often rely on limited cues such as recent usage, making them unreliable in scenarios such as temporary sharing. We propose a framework for context-aware ownership inference with uncertainty-guided interaction (COIN). The method integrates user background information and object usage history using a large language model (LLM) to estimate ownership scores. To handle uncertainty, we apply conformal prediction to construct a set of plausible owners and selectively generate user queries when the prediction is uncertain. Experiments in a simulated home environment show that the proposed method consistently outperforms baseline approaches, achieving a Subset Accuracy of 0.988 and a Mean Jaccard index of 0.991. The method also maintains high performance in scenarios involving temporary use and shared ownership. The results demonstrate that combining contextual reasoning with uncertainty-aware interaction improves both estimation accuracy and robustness. The project page is available at https://emergentsystemlabstudent.github.io/COIN/.

</details>


### [116] [An Operator-Based Approach to STL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28092)
*Panagiotis Rousseas,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于可达性值函数算子的信号时序逻辑（STL）新方法，能够处理复杂多层嵌套公式并支持在线控制综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STL验证与控制综合方法在处理公式的复杂性和嵌套深度方面存在局限性。

Method: 基于作用于可达性值函数的算子，直接开发算子级嵌套规则，而非设计STL可达性或控制障碍函数。

Result: 理论上提取了STL公式满足的必要充分条件，仿真中验证了复杂片段的有效性。

Conclusion: 该算子框架为处理复杂多层嵌套STL公式提供了新的理论工具，并支持在线控制综合。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL), has recently seen extensive development, owing to its rich expressivenes for autonomous planning and control. Nevertheless, existing verification and control synthesis methods are limited with respect to the complexity and degree of nesting of the formulae. In this work, we propose a novel approach to STL based on an operator acting on reachability value functions. This constitutes a new theoretical framework for handling complex multi-nested formulae while at the same time providing tools for on-line control synthesis. In contrast to focusing on the design of STL-based reachability (or control barrier) functions, we develop operator-based nesting rules directly. Our method's expressiveness is demonstrated both theoretically, where necessary and sufficient conditions for STL formula satisfaction are extracted, as well as in simulations with complex fragments.

</details>


### [117] [STR Robot: Design of an Autonomous Mobile Robot from Simulation to Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28110)
*Vinh Nguyen,Gia-Uy Le,Tien-Dat Nguyen,Tri-Tin Nguyen,Vinh-Hao Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于现有机械平台的自主移动机器人仿真到现实的实现方法，重点开发了车载控制、自定位和自主导航系统，先在仿真中开发测试，再部署到真实机器人上验证，实验结果证明了该方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用仿真工具快速开发和验证自主机器人系统，提高从仿真到现实部署的效率和可靠性。

Method: 基于现有机械平台，开发车载控制、自定位和自主导航系统；先在仿真环境中开发和测试整体框架，再部署到真实机器人上进行实验评估。

Result: 仿真为开发可靠的自主移动机器人系统提供了有效基础，所提方法在真实机器人上被验证可行。

Conclusion: 该仿真到现实的实现方法有效可靠，能够为自主移动机器人的开发提供扎实基础。

Abstract: With the rapid development of simulation tools, the development and validation of autonomous robotic systems have become more efficient before real-world deployment. This paper presents a simulation-to-real implementation of an autonomous mobile robot based on an existing mechanical platform. Instead of focusing on mechanical design, our work concentrates on the development of the onboard control, self-localization, and autonomous navigation system. The proposed robot is equipped with onboard sensing and computation to estimate its pose and navigate autonomously in the environment. The overall framework is first developed and tested in simulation, and then deployed on the real robot for experimental evaluation. The results demonstrate the feasibility of the proposed approach and show that simulation provides an effective foundation for developing reliable autonomous mobile robot systems. The source code will be released at https://ntdathp.github.io/outdoor-robot-web.

</details>


### [118] [Provably Guaranteed Polytopic Uncertainty Quantification for SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28172)
*Guangyang Zeng,Yulong Gao,Yuan Shen,Lingpeng Chen,Haoying Li,Guodong Shi,Junfeng Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套具有形式化保证的不确定性量化算法，用于3D-3D地标SLAM，通过多面体表示不确定性集，结合共形预测校准测量不确定性，实现了可证明的确定性保证和实际可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键的机器人应用中，感知环节的不确定性量化需要形式化保证，但现有工作要么缺乏保真性保证，要么依赖 restrictive 建模假设，或仅关注位姿估计而非完整SLAM流程。

Method: 提出三个基础UQ模块：前向UQ（建图）、后向UQ（位姿跟踪）和位姿复合。采用多面体表示不确定性集，实现可计算性；引入共形预测从数据中校准测量不确定性，赋予概率保证。

Result: 仿真和实验表明，该算法兼具强理论保证和实际可用性，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 多面体表示与共形预测的结合成功实现了SLAM中可证明的不确定性量化，兼顾形式化保证与实用效率。

Abstract: In safety-critical robotics applications, guaranteed and practical uncertainty quantification (UQ) in perception is vital. Many existing works either offer no formal containment guarantee, rely on restrictive modeling assumptions, or focus only on pose estimation rather than a complete SLAM pipeline. This paper presents provably guaranteed UQ algorithms for 3D-3D landmark-based SLAM. The algorithms consist of three basic UQ modules: forward UQ for mapping, backward UQ for pose tracking, and pose compound. Each module produces a certified uncertainty set; when the input uncertainty bounds are deterministic, the output sets inherit deterministic guarantees, i.e., they provably contain the true poses and landmarks. Specifically, we use polytopes to represent uncertainty sets, enabling tractable computations and a unified treatment of pose uncertainty. To enhance algorithms' practical usability, we incorporate conformal prediction to calibrate measurement uncertainty from data with prescribed probability. Simulations and experiments demonstrate that the proposed algorithms provide both strong theoretical guarantees and practical usability. The code is open-sourced at https://github.com/LIAS-CUHKSZ/Polytopic-SLAM-Uncertainty-Quantification.

</details>


### [119] [Natural Functional Gradients for Smooth Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28202)
*Kisang Park,Chanwoo Kim,Kyungjae Lee,Sungjoon Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于自然函数梯度的轨迹优化框架，通过在函数空间中进行几何感知更新，生成无碰撞且平滑的运动，在狭窄环境和高约束任务中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在杂乱环境和狭窄通道中，可行区域高度受限且碎片化，生成无碰撞和平滑的运动是机器人操作中的核心挑战。

Method: 提出自然函数梯度轨迹优化框架：在函数空间中直接进行几何感知更新；优化高斯平滑的替代目标函数，通过平滑轨迹扰动正则化优化景观；推导实用的蒙特卡洛估计器，仅需黑箱轨迹评估即可计算自然函数梯度。

Result: 在约束机器人操作任务中，该方法相比代表性规划和轨迹优化基线，提高了轨迹可行性并生成了更平滑的运动，尤其在具有狭窄几何间隙的环境中表现更优。

Conclusion: 自然函数梯度方法通过函数空间中的内在更新，实现了轨迹正则化与时间离散化的解耦，在狭窄环境下的机器人操作中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Generating collision-free and smooth motions remains a central challenge in robotic manipulation, particularly in cluttered environments and narrow passages where feasible regions are highly constrained and fragmented. We propose a trajectory optimization framework that performs geometry-aware updates directly in function space using natural functional gradients. The method optimizes a Gaussian-smoothed surrogate objective that regularizes the optimization landscape through smooth trajectory perturbations while preserving trajectory-level structure. Because the updates are defined intrinsically in function space, trajectory regularity can be controlled independently of a particular time discretization. We derive a practical Monte-Carlo estimator of the natural functional gradient that requires only black-box trajectory evaluations, making the method applicable when analytic gradients are unavailable or unreliable due to collision checking and contact-rich simulation. Experiments on constrained robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that the proposed method improves trajectory feasibility and produces smoother motions than representative planning and trajectory optimization baselines in environments with narrow geometric clearances. Additional results, videos, and implementation details are available at the project page: https://kisangpark.github.io/natural-functional-gradient/

</details>


### [120] [ProgVLA: Progress-Aware Robot Manipulation Skill Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28231)
*Seungsu Kim,Jinyoung Choi,Seungmin Baek,Jean-Michel Renders*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ProgVLA是一种紧凑的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过双阶段Perceiver重采样压缩多模态序列，并引入基于离线强化学习的进度头来估计任务进展，在长程机器人操作任务上以0.1B参数达到或超越更大规模基线模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在计算和内存受限场景下难以高效处理长多模态序列，特别是在需要明确跟踪任务进度的长程操作任务中表现不佳。作者旨在设计一种紧凑模型，在保持跨模态对齐的同时显著压缩序列长度，并利用任务进度信息提升长程和多目标任务的可靠性。

Method: 1) 双阶段Perceiver重采样编码器：将可变长度的视觉、语言和本体感受流压缩为固定数量的控制上下文令牌；2) 进度头：使用离线强化学习目标（归一化剩余时域目标上的评论家）联合训练，为策略提供内部任务进度估计；3) 基于优势加权和成功加权的流匹配模仿学习。

Result: 0.1B参数的ProgVLA在两个多任务机器人操作基准上与更大规模预训练基线竞争，在长程和困难任务上超过后者；消融实验表明上下文重采样器和任务自适应视觉微调贡献最大，进度感知训练在长程和多目标任务上提供一致增益；在真实玩具厨房环境中验证有效。

Conclusion: ProgVLA证明了紧凑VLA模型通过显式的进度感知设计和高效的多模态序列压缩，可以在计算和内存受限条件下实现可靠的机器人操作，尤其是在长时域任务上具有显著优势。

Abstract: We present ProgVLA, a compact vision-language-action (VLA) model designed for reliable robot manipulation under tight compute and memory budgets. The model specifically focuses on efficiently processing long multi-modal sequences by maintaining an explicit representation of task progress over extended horizons. To this end, ProgVLA integrates two key components. First, a multi-modal encoder with a two-stage Perceiver resampling scheme compresses variable-length visual, language, and proprioceptive streams into a fixed set of control-ready context tokens, substantially reducing sequence length while preserving cross-modal grounding. Second, an auxiliary set of progress heads is trained with offline reinforcement learning (RL) objectives to jointly learn critics over normalized remaining-horizon targets. This provides the policy with an internal estimate of task progress and enables advantage- and success-weighted flow-matching imitation learning. On two well-established multi-task robot manipulation benchmarks, a 0.1B-parameter ProgVLA model reaches success rates that are competitive with, and on long-horizon and harder task tiers exceed, substantially larger pretrained baselines. Ablations indicate that the learned context resampler and task-adaptive visual fine-tuning are the largest single contributors, while progress-aware training provides a consistent additional gain that is concentrated on long-horizon and multi-object tasks. We further validate the approach in real-world toy-kitchen environments.

</details>


### [121] [POINav: Benchmarking and Enhancing Final-Meters Arrival in Real-World Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28237)
*Ruiyan Gong,Meisheng Zhang,Yuxiang Zhao,Mingchao Sun,Yanfen Shen,Zedong Chu,Zhining Gu,Wei Guo,Xiaolong Cheng,Qiming Li,Kangning Niu,Yanqing Zhu,Xiaolong Wu,Tianlun Li,Mu Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: POINav-Bench是首个面向真实世界POI目标导航的闭环评测基准，包含基于3DGS重建的11个商业区域、126,398m²面积和163个POI，并提出POINav脑-动作框架及70K真实路标-入口数据集，实验验证了其在解决"最后几米"导航问题上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准中POI目标导航存在粒度粗糙或由于场景生成导致的显著虚实差距，亟需一个高保真、细粒度的真实世界闭环评测基准来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 1) 构建POINav-Bench：利用3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)从真实场景捕获重建11个商业区域，提供可通行性标注和参考轨迹；2) 提出POINav脑-动作框架：脑模块执行POI基础推理，动作模块预测连续路标点供真实世界执行；3) 整理POINav-Dataset：包含70K真实路标-入口对。

Result: 所提出的框架为改进真实世界POI目标导航提供了一条可行路径，实验验证了方案的有效性。

Conclusion: POINav-Bench填补了真实世界POI目标导航评测的空白，结合脑-动作框架为从粗粒度导航到精确POI抵达的"最后几米"问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world navigation is fundamentally driven by Points of Interest (POIs), yet reaching a precise POI remains a critical "final-meters" challenge. Existing Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) benchmarks of POI-goal navigation often suffer from coarse granularity or significant sim-to-real gaps due to generated scene. To bridge this gap, we present POINav-Bench, the first benchmark designed for closed-loop evaluation of real-world POI-goal navigation. It comprises 11 commercial areas reconstructed from real-world captures using 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), covering 126,398 $m^{2}$ in total and spanning 163 distinct POIs. With traversability-aware annotations and reference trajectories, POINav-Bench enables high-fidelity evaluation of navigation agents in realistic, POI-rich real-world environments. Building on this, we propose the POINav Brain-Action Framework where a Brain module performs POI-grounded reasoning to guide an Action module in predicting continuous waypoints for real-world execution. We further curate the POINav-Dataset, containing 70K real-world signage-entrance pairs. Experiments show that our framework provides a viable path toward refining real-world POI-goal navigation.

</details>


### [122] [Natural Locomotion: Principle and Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28254)
*Mirado Mortel,Luc Jaulin,Lionel Lapierre,Simon Rohou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出自然运动作为交换原理，通过被动动力学实现高效机器人运动，并构建自然运动流形（NLM）框架。该方法通过封闭/开放构造，在理想非完整约束系统（如Chaplygin雪橇/摆驱动车及其三体扩展）上验证了零平均推进-振荡器交换功率条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动效率可通过利用被动动力学、柔顺性和共振来实现，而非跟踪预设轨迹。传统方法难以捕捉这种自然运动的内在规律，需要一种统一原理来形式化环境约束介导的运动系统。

Method: 提出保守实现原理：环境约束不做外功，总机械能守恒，零平均POE功率作为内部交换。采用封闭/开放构造：先封闭推进通道揭示有效内部振荡器（标量情况用作用-角结构，多自由度用非线性模态扇区），再重新开放重建位姿，筛选满足内部循环和零平均POE功率的周期。

Result: 在Chaplygin雪橇/摆驱动车和三体扩展两个理想非完整无滑移系统上验证了原理。标量情形下POE闭包等价于内部循环缺失条件，给出定理支持的NLM族计算。多自由度情形下POE闭包仍必要，还需模态恒等、内部循环、动力学一致性、相同被动架构和非零位移等条件。

Conclusion: 自然运动被转化为设计问题：哪些被动架构支持零个、一个或多个认证的NLM族？该方法为被动动力学驱动的机器人运动设计提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Robotic locomotion can become efficient when mechanisms exploit passive dynamics, compliance, and resonance rather than track prescribed trajectories. This paper formulates natural locomotion as an exchange principle for systems whose motion is mediated by environmental constraints or interactions. A motion is natural when an internal oscillator returns periodically, the body pose drifts, and the mean Propulsion--Oscillator Exchange power (POE power) vanishes over one cycle. The selected family is a Natural Locomotion Manifold (NLM). We develop the conservative realization of this principle for continuous ideal environmental constraints: the constraints do no external work, total mechanical energy is conserved, and zero mean POE power is an internal exchange with the environment-mediated propulsive channel, not external energy input.
  The method is a closed/open construction. The propulsive channel is first closed to reveal an effective internal oscillator, organized by scalar action-angle structure in one effective degree of freedom or by nonlinear modal sectors in several degrees of freedom. The channel is then reopened, pose is reconstructed, and accepted cycles must preserve internal recurrence and zero mean POE power.
  We demonstrate the principle on two ideal nonholonomic no-slip systems: a Chaplygin-sleigh / pendulum-driven car and a three-body extension. In the scalar case, POE closure is equivalent to the missing internal return condition, giving a theorem-backed computation of the NLM family. In the multi-degree case, POE closure remains necessary but must be completed by modal identity, internal return, dynamics consistency, same fixed passive architecture, and nonzero displacement. Natural locomotion becomes a design question: which passive architectures support no, one, or several certified NLM families?

</details>


### [123] [IMU Propagation as Preintegration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28279)
*Jianzhu Huai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明IMU预积分与传播是同一底层计算的两种等价实现方式，并提出了一个与惯例无关的视角，使得可以用现有传播代码生成预积分测量、偏差雅可比和协方差，反之亦然。实验验证了RK4传播与GTSAM预积分模块在雅可比、协方差和转移矩阵上的高度一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子图框架中IMU预积分常被视为与常规IMU传播不同的专门实现，这种割裂导致了代码重复和理解困难。作者旨在揭示两者本质等价性，简化代码复用并支持不同误差状态定义间的转换。

Method: 提出一个惯例无关的视角，证明预积分测量、偏差雅可比和协方差可以通过对现有IMU传播例程进行封装得到，预积分模块也能反向恢复状态转移矩阵和传播协方差。采用RK4传播实现与GTSAM的切空间和流形预积分模块进行对比验证。

Result: 在随机IMU序列实验中，RK4传播实现与GTSAM的切空间和流形预积分模块在恢复的雅可比、协方差和转移矩阵上表现出高度一致性。

Conclusion: IMU预积分和传播等价，该视角简化了现有传播代码的复用，支持不同误差状态定义的翻译，并为预积分实现提供了一致性检查手段。

Abstract: IMU preintegration is widely used in factor-graph-based visual--inertial, lidar--inertial, and radar--inertial state estimation, yet it is often treated as a specialized implementation separate from conventional IMU propagation. This note shows that IMU preintegration and propagation are equivalent realizations of the same underlying computation. We present a convention-agnostic view in which the preintegrated measurement, bias Jacobians, and covariance can be obtained by wrapping an existing IMU propagation routine, while a preintegration module can conversely recover state-transition matrices and propagated covariances. This perspective simplifies the reuse of existing propagation code, supports translation across different error-state definitions, and provides practical consistency checks for preintegration implementations. Experiments with random IMU sequences demonstrate close agreement between an RK4-based propagation implementation and GTSAM's tangent and manifold preintegration modules in the recovered Jacobians, covariances, and transition matrices.

</details>


### [124] [EventShiftFlow: Towards Hardware-efficient FPGA-based Flow Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28312)
*Arianna Alonso Bizzi,Fernando Cladera,C. J. Taylor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向FPGA的流式事件相机速度估计方法，将异步事件离散化为固定时间片，构建1-bit空间占用网格，并行评估多个速度假设，仅使用整数逻辑（移位寄存器、计数器、比较器、LUT乘法器），无需除法器、DSP、浮点运算或迭代优化。在合成噪声数据上可恢复速度和方向，真实序列方向精度达99.5%，存储需求<2KB，并在Xilinx Artix-7上实现单轴原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机运动估计方法计算密集、难以映射到FPGA硬件，无法满足低延迟、小尺寸、低功耗平台的实时感知需求。

Method: 将异步事件离散化为固定时长的时间片，构建1-bit空间占用网格；并行评估多个离散速度假设；仅使用移位寄存器、计数器、比较器和LUT映射乘法等固定宽度整数逻辑，完全避免除法器、DSP块、浮点运算和迭代优化。

Result: 在合成噪声数据上可恢复速度和方向；真实事件相机序列中四个运动段的方向精度达99.5%；在10-40%占用密度范围内保持鲁棒；存储需求<2 kB；在低成本Artix-7 FPGA上实现单轴原型。

Conclusion: 该方法以稀疏量化速度估计替代稠密亚像素光流，适合SWaP受限平台上的低延迟任务（如反应式避障），并提供密度依赖行为及参数敏感性分析。

Abstract: Event-based vision sensors offer asynchronous, high-temporal-resolution measurements that are attractive for low-latency robotic perception, but many event-based motion estimation methods are computationally intensive and difficult to map to FPGA hardware. We present a streaming velocity estimator that discretizes asynchronous events into fixed-duration time bins, constructs a 1-bit spatial occupancy grid, and evaluates multiple velocity hypotheses in parallel using only fixed-width integer logic - shift registers, counters, comparators, and small LUT-mapped multiplies - with no dividers and no DSP blocks. It requires no frame reconstruction, no floating-point arithmetic, and no iterative optimization. The method deliberately trades dense sub-pixel optical flow for a sparse, quantized velocity estimate at each active pixel, suited to low-latency tasks such as reactive obstacle avoidance on size-, weight-, and power-constrained platforms. On noisy synthetic data with known ground-truth velocities, the method recovers both magnitude and direction, with magnitude estimates being most challenged when objects of different velocities intersect. On a real event-camera sequence, directional accuracy reaches 99.5% across all four evaluated motion segments, with performance remaining robust across occupancy densities in the 10-40% range. We characterize the algorithm's density-dependent behavior, present a parameter sensitivity analysis, show that the proposed datapath requires less than 2 kB of storage, and implement a single-axis prototype on a low-cost Xilinx Artix-7.

</details>


### [125] [Chance-Constrained MPPI under State and Dynamic Object Prediction Uncertainty and the Evaluation of Collision Risk Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28330)
*Benjamin Serfling,Konrad Doll,Kati Radkhah-Lens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DUCCT-MPPI框架，通过联合考虑定位不确定性和动态障碍物预测不确定性，并采用严格评分规则评估碰撞风险预测的统计有效性，在密集环境中导航成功率达28%提升，同时保持最低旅行时间和最小社会力干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率保证方法隐含假设上游不确定性（定位与感知）已良好校准，但实际中估计器常存在校准偏差，导致过度自信引发安全违规或欠自信导致保守冻结/概率稀释问题。

Method: 提出DUCCT-MPPI框架，联合集成定位不确定性（通过单管无迹变换UT近似）和动态障碍物预测不确定性（通过蒙特卡洛聚合），并采用严格评分规则评估碰撞风险预测的统计有效性。

Result: 在密集环境中，DUCCT-MPPI相比蒙特卡洛MPPI基线导航成功率提升近28%，同时实现最低旅行时间和最小社会力诱导。

Conclusion: 可靠的自主导航概率安全不仅需要具有表达力的风险模型，还需要整个自主栈中统计有效的估计不确定性。

Abstract: Chance-constrained Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control is increasingly adopted for navigation in dynamic environments to explicitly bound collision risk. However, these probabilistic guarantees implicitly assume that upstream uncertainties from localization and perception are well-calibrated. In practice, estimators are often miscalibrated, inducing characteristic closed-loop failure modes: overconfidence leads to systematic safety violations, while underconfidence triggers overly conservative freezing or probability dilution. To address this critical gap, our primary contribution is a rigorous evaluation methodology applying proper scoring rules to assess the statistical validity of predicted collision risks during closed-loop execution. Concurrently, Dual-Uncertainty Chance-Constrained Tube MPPI (DUCCT-MPPI) is proposed as a real-time, risk-aware planning architecture. DUCCT-MPPI jointly integrates localization uncertainty via a one-tube Unscented Transform (UT) approximation and dynamic obstacle prediction uncertainty via Monte Carlo aggregation. Through extensive physics-based simulations, the framework demonstrates robust failure-mitigation, seamlessly transitioning to safe, conservative maneuvering without succumbing to functional deadlocks in highly cluttered environments. In highly cluttered environments, DUCCT-MPPI achieves superior robustness, outperforming established Monte Carlo MPPI baselines by nearly 28\% in navigation success rate, while simultaneously recording the lowest travel times and minimizing induced social forces. Ultimately, these findings establish that reliable probabilistic safety in autonomous navigation dictates not only expressive risk models but statistically valid uncertainty estimates throughout the entire autonomy stack.

</details>


### [126] [Magnet-Based Soft Robotic Skin Using a 3D-Printed Multi-Lattice Structure and CNN-Based Tactile Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28352)
*Yunseong Bang,Joowon Park,Suan Sim,Youngjun Ryu,Sukho Park,Kyungseo Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于磁场的机器人皮肤，通过多层软晶格、分布式霍尔效应传感器阵列和触觉超分辨率模型，实现大面积、无盲区的触觉感知，并支持快速制造和实时接触力估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人触觉皮肤在实现大面积覆盖、高分辨率传感和适应复杂曲面方面存在挑战，尤其是在盲区抑制和感知精度之间难以平衡。

Method: 采用多层软晶格结构嵌入永磁体，将外部接触力转化为磁场变化；晶格将变化扩散到整个传感域，使每个传感器获得大且重叠的感受野；通过隐式建模工作流和SLS 3D打印实现快速制造；使用CNN对实验测量数据进行训练，实时估计接触位置和法向力。

Result: 实验验证了定位精度，并表明该方法可扩展到更大表面，具备应用于全身机器人皮肤和安全人机交互的潜力。

Conclusion: 该磁基机器人皮肤通过晶格参数可调实现了机械柔顺性与传感特性的联合调节，为大面积、无盲区触觉感知提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a magnet-based robotic skin that integrates a multilayer soft lattice with distributed Hall-effect sensor arrays and a tactile super-resolution model. External contact forces are converted to magnetic field changes by embedded permanent magnets, and the lattice spreads these changes across the sensing domain. This gives each sensor a large, overlapping receptive field and enables a large sensing area with minimal blind spots. Lattice parameters are tunable, enabling joint adjustment of mechanical compliance and transduction characteristics. An implicit modeling workflow and selective laser sintering (SLS) 3D printing support rapid fabrication of conformal, high-complexity structures. A convolutional neural network trained on experimental measurements estimates contact location and normal force in real time. Experiments validate localization accuracy and indicate scalability to larger surfaces, suggesting applicability to whole-body robotic skin and safe human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [127] [Safety-Critical Adaptive Impedance Control via Nonsmooth Control Barrier Functions under State and Input Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28367)
*Faisal Lawan,Xiaoran Han,Joaquin Carrasco,Barry Lennox,Xiaoxiao Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合二次规划安全滤波器与非光滑控制屏障函数（NCBF）的在线自适应阻抗控制框架，通过区间2型模糊系统补偿未知动力学，并利用扰动观测器增强安全性，在7自由度机械臂上验证了安全约束满足与鲁棒阻抗跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人与机器人交互及接触密集任务中，不确定性、外力及执行器限制会同时损害机械臂操作的安全性与性能，亟需一种能够在不确定动力学下实现合规交互并保障关节状态安全的方法。

Method: 采用基于二次规划的安全滤波器结合新型组合位置-速度非光滑控制屏障函数（NCBF），将关节位置与速度约束统一为相对度为一的屏障；利用区间2型模糊逻辑系统在线补偿未知动力学；通过软约束结合精确罚函数恢复可行解来处理执行器扭矩限制；引入扰动观测器增强对外部力的鲁棒性。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂上，面对严重的参数不确定性与外部交互力，仿真结果验证了该方法能够安全满足约束并实现鲁棒的阻抗跟踪。

Conclusion: 通过复合李雅普诺夫分析证明了安全集的前向不变性与阻抗跟踪误差的一致最终有界性，该框架为不确定性下的安全物理交互提供了有效保障。

Abstract: Safe physical interaction is critical for deploying robotic manipulators in human-robot interaction and contact-rich tasks, where uncertainty, external forces, and actuator limitations can compromise both performance and safety. We propose an online adaptive impedance control framework that enforces joint-state safety while achieving compliant interaction under uncertain dynamics. The approach combines a quadratic-program-based safety filter with a novel composed position-velocity non-smooth control barrier function (NCBF), enabling joint position and velocity constraints to be enforced through a unified relative-degree-one barrier. Unknown dynamics are compensated online using an interval type-2 fuzzy logic system, while actuator torque limits are handled through soft constraints with exact penalty recovery of feasible solutions. A disturbance-observer-enhanced safety mechanism improves robustness against modelling errors and external interaction forces. Using composite Lyapunov analysis, we prove forward invariance of the safe set and the uniform ultimately boundedness of the impedance-tracking error. Simulations on a 7-DOF manipulator with severe parametric uncertainty and external interaction wrenches demonstrate safe constraint satisfaction and robust impedance tracking.

</details>


### [128] [A Digital Twin Framework for Virtual Visuo-Haptic Teleoperation of Complex-Shaped Optical Microrobots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28448)
*Zongcai Tan,Lan Wei,Dandan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向复杂形状光学微机器人的数字孪生框架，用于虚拟视觉-触觉遥操作。该框架集成了数字孪生环境、图像位姿与深度估计、微机器人运动仿真和基于模型的触觉渲染，在多陷阱操控场景中，触觉反馈将接触力标准差降低53.2%、微机器人-陷阱中心距离标准差降低55.2%，任务成功率从30%提升至80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光镊技术可为精密生物医学任务提供皮牛级操控，视觉-触觉反馈可提升操作者对交互力与陷阱稳定性的感知。但针对复杂形状光学微机器人的视觉-触觉遥操作框架，尤其是在多陷阱操控场景下，尚不成熟。

Method: 构建数字孪生框架，集成数字孪生环境、基于图像的位姿与深度估计、微机器人运动仿真和基于模型触觉渲染；采用多球体分布式操控（MSDM）模型结合光学工具箱的光学力估计进行力建模；在ROS连接的双臂遥操作系统中实现。

Result: 框架能复现代表性微机器人运动趋势，触觉力渲染与拟合光学力模型数值一致；在模拟细胞递送任务中，触觉反馈使接触力标准差和微机器人-陷阱中心距离标准差分别降低53.2%和55.2%，任务成功率从30%提升至80%。

Conclusion: 该框架在评估复杂形状光学微机器人的视觉-触觉遥操作策略方面展现了有效性。

Abstract: Optical tweezers (OT) provide piconewton-scale manipulation for delicate biomedical tasks, where visuo-haptic feedback can improve operator awareness by conveying interaction-force cues and trap-stability information. However, visuo-haptic teleoperation frameworks for complex-shaped optical microrobots remain underdeveloped, particularly in multi-trap manipulation scenarios. This paper presents a digital twin framework for virtual visuo-haptic teleoperation of complex-shaped OT-driven microrobots. The framework integrates a digital twin environment, image-based pose and depth estimation, microrobot motion simulation, and model-based haptic rendering within a Robot Operating System (ROS)-connected bimanual teleoperation system. For force modeling, we combine a Multi-Sphere Distributed Manipulation (MSDM) model with optical-force estimation from the Optical Tweezers Toolbox, enabling simulator-driven visuo-haptic feedback. The framework reproduces representative microrobot motion trends and provides haptic force rendering that is numerically consistent with the fitted optical-force model. In simulated cell-delivery tasks, haptic feedback reduced the standard deviations of the contact-force metric and the microrobot-to-trap-center distance metric by 53.2% and 55.2%, respectively, and improved task success from 30% to 80%. These results demonstrate the framework's effectiveness for evaluating visuo-haptic teleoperation strategies for complex-shaped optical microrobots.

</details>


### [129] [Learning a Kinodynamic Trajectory Manifold for Impact-Aware Compliant Catching of Fast-Moving Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28462)
*Guorui Pei,Mengshi Zhang,Xi Chen,Jinsong Wu,Jiaming Qi,Peng Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合仿真强化学习与轨迹流形的方法，用于实现快速自由飞行物体抓取，无需在线非线性优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速抓取自由飞行物体面临反应时间短、冲击不确定性和运动学动力学约束等挑战，现有方法难以同时满足实时性与鲁棒性要求。

Method: 在仿真中利用强化学习收集成功的抓取轨迹，并学习一个低维的运动学动力学轨迹流形；在运行时，将估计的物体初始状态直接映射到参考抓取轨迹，无需在线非线性优化；在接近接触时采用柔顺控制进行轨迹跟踪，以改善冲击吸收和抓取稳定性。

Result: 实现了无需在线优化的快速自由飞行物体抓取，并在接近接触时具有良好的冲击吸收和抓取稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过离线学习轨迹流形和在线柔顺控制相结合，有效解决了快速抓取中的实时性与鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Fast catching of free-flying objects is difficult because of short reaction time, impact uncertainty, and kinodynamic constraints. We use reinforcement learning in simulation to collect successful catching trajectories and learn a low-dimensional kinodynamic trajectory manifold. At run time, the estimated object initial state is mapped directly to a reference catching trajectory without online nonlinear optimization. The trajectory is tracked with compliant control near contact for improved impact absorption and capture stability.

</details>


### [130] [EIT-Pneumatic Hybrid Robotic Skin for Practical and Accurate Force Map Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28468)
*Junhwi Cho,Sunggyu Bae,Junghyeon Ma,Hyosang Lee,Jung Kim,Kyungseo Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合电阻抗成像（EIT）与气动触觉传感的混合机器人皮肤，通过3D打印与喷涂工艺制造，利用Tikhonov正则化逆重建与逐垫气压校准实现大面积触觉感知。实验表明，与纯EIT相比，力重建敏感度变异系数从0.31降至0.14，并在人形机器人胸部集成中验证了多触点同时接触的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决EIT（电阻抗成像）触觉传感中力重建敏感度不均匀的长期局限性，提出一种混合方法提高大面积触觉传感的精度与一致性。

Method: 混合机器人皮肤：结合EIT与气动触觉传感；全3D打印与喷涂制造；Tikhonov正则化逆重建配合逐垫气压校准。

Result: 力重建敏感度变异系数从纯EIT的0.31降至0.14；多点同时接触时气动信号保持可靠；力重建在垫内各位置保持一致。

Conclusion: 提出了一种实现机器人全身高精度、可扩展触觉传感的实用路径。

Abstract: We present a hybrid robotic skin that combines electrical impedance tomography (EIT) with pneumatic tactile sensing to improve force reconstruction capability. The developed robotic skin is fabricated entirely by 3D printing and spray coating, making it affordable and easy to build. A Tikhonov-regularized inverse reconstruction, paired with per-pad pneumatic calibration, enables accurate large-area tactile sensing with a simple measurement scheme. For validation, we conducted load-cell indentation experiments; the results showed consistent force reconstruction across locations within a pad. Compared with an EIT-only baseline, sensitivity non-uniformity was also reduced, with the coefficient of variation decreasing from 0.31 to 0.14, indicating that the proposed approach addresses a longstanding limitation of EIT. We further demonstrated chest-mounted integration on a humanoid robot and found that the pneumatic signals remained reliable across diverse contact scenarios, including multiple simultaneous contacts on the same sensing pad. These results indicate a practical path toward accurate, scalable whole-body tactile sensing in real robotic systems.

</details>


### [131] [What Frozen VLAs Already Know About Success: A Probing Study of Value-Like Structure in Foundation Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28527)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junnan Nie,Junyi Lao,Wei Cheng,Chenghao Liu,Jiaxin Jiang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLAs在训练时仅通过模仿学习动作，但其冻结的表征中编码了任务成功信息，可通过轻量线性探针提取；使用探针进行测试时选择可提升部分任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究VLA策略在仅使用模仿学习目标训练后，其冻结表征是否隐含了与任务成功相关的信息，以及这些信息能否被提取并用于改善决策。

Method: 从LIBERO-Goal数据集的成功和失败轨迹中，用轻量线性探针在VLA冻结特征上预测蒙特卡洛结果标签；通过同任务、同时间步的匹配对比消除任务和时间捷径；将探针作为测试时选择器，对Pi0.5的采样动作前缀进行排序筛选。

Result: OpenVLA、Pi0.5、DINOv2和CLIP特征均能一致预测成功结果，基线方法（进度、剩余时间、任务身份、本体感知）效果显著更差；Pi0.5探针在同任务同时间步对比中仍达到约92%的配对排序准确率；用于动作选择后，push-plate任务成功率从26.7%提升至44.3%，wine-rack任务也有正向效果。

Conclusion: 冻结的VLA表征已经编码了模仿学习目标从未显式要求的学习信号（任务成功信息），这一发现可转化为实际的性能提升，但提升并非普遍且需额外推理计算。

Abstract: Vision--language--action (VLA) policies are trained to imitate actions; their loss never asks them to estimate reward, progress, or future success. Their frozen representations nevertheless carry such information, and it can be read out and used to guide action choice without retraining the policy. From mixed successful and failed manipulation trajectories on LIBERO-Goal, we recover Monte-Carlo outcome targets using lightweight linear probes on frozen features. The targets are consistently predictable from OpenVLA, Pi0.5, DINOv2, and CLIP features, and substantially less so from baselines built on progress, time-to-go, task identity, or proprioception. To rule out task and temporal shortcuts, we evaluate the probes under same-task, same-timestep matched comparisons: Pi0.5 probes still reach roughly 92% pairwise ordering accuracy, while label-shuffled controls stay at chance. Used as a test-time selector over sampled Pi0.5 action prefixes, the same probe turns this offline finding into behavior: on push-plate, success rises from 26.7% under greedy decoding to 44.3%, with a second positive case on wine-rack. The gains are not universal and require additional inference compute, but the underlying finding is clean: frozen VLAs already encode information about success that their imitation objective never explicitly demands.

</details>


### [132] [SPRINT: Efficient Spectral Priors for Humanoid Athletic Sprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28549)
*Yantong Wei,Kaihong Huang,Hainan Pan,Jiawei Luo,Jiawei Zhou,Ziyan Mai,Zhiwen Zeng,Yaonan Wang,Huimin Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SPRINT框架，利用频率自适应频谱先验，仅凭5个参考运动序列实现人形机器人高速冲刺（6 m/s），并完成零样本仿真到真实环境的迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏人形机器人可用的运动学参考数据，且无法在冲刺过程中维持稳定性，限制了人形机器人高速运动的发展。

Method: 在频域中利用5个离散运动序列的参考库表征人类运动的基本周期性，生成频率自适应频谱先验，进而产生跨越广泛速度范围的运动学可行关节轨迹，实现超出参考分布的速度外推；基于预训练先验的策略在Unitree G1平台上实现零样本仿真到真实环境的迁移。

Result: 在Unitree G1平台上的实地实验中达到6 m/s的峰值冲刺速度，展现出无缝的步态转换，同时保持仿生自然性。

Conclusion: 频率自适应频谱先验是一种高度数据高效的人形机器人运动基础，为人形机器人高速冲刺提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The pursuit of humanoid athletic sprints is hindered by a scarcity of humanoid-viable kinematic reference data and the inability of existing frameworks to maintain stability during sprints. To overcome these limitations, we introduce SPRINT, a novel framework driven by efficient, frequency-adaptive spectral priors. By characterizing the fundamental periodicity of human locomotion in the frequency domain using a reference library of five discrete motion sequences, these priors generate kinematically feasible joint trajectories across a broad velocity spectrum, successfully extrapolating to speeds that exceed the reference distribution. Guided by these pretrained priors, the SPRINT policy achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer in field experiments on the Unitree G1 platform, reaching a peak sprinting velocity of 6 m/s and demonstrating seamless gait transitions while preserving biomimetic naturalness. Ultimately, this work establishes frequency-adaptive spectral priors as a highly data-efficient foundation for humanoid athletic sprints. The project page is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/w/SPRINT-138A/.

</details>


### [133] [SARAD: LLM-Based Safety-Aware Hybrid Reinforcement Learning with Collision Prediction for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28583)
*Kangyu Wu,Peng Cui,Guoxi Chen,Ya Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SARAD混合框架，将大语言模型与深度强化学习结合，通过RAG增强的LLM引导决策替代DRL随机探索，利用注意力鉴别器整合先验知识，并设计碰撞预测器提升安全性，在Highway-Env模拟器中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DRL存在不安全随机探索和收敛慢的问题，而LLM在实时推理中存在固有延迟，亟需一种兼顾安全与效率的自动驾驶决策框架。

Method: 提出SARAD框架：1）用RAG增强的LLM引导决策替代DRL随机探索；2）注意力鉴别器将LLM先验知识融入DRL策略优化；3）碰撞预测器基于历史碰撞数据微调提升安全性。

Result: 在Highway-Env模拟器中，SARAD相比基线方法在安全性和决策效率方面取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: SARAD有效融合LLM的常识推理与DRL的策略学习能力，为自动驾驶提供了一种安全高效的混合决策方案。

Abstract: Ensuring both safety and efficiency in decision-making for autonomous driving systems remains a fundamental challenge. Traditional Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) suffers from unsafe random exploration and slow convergence, while Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate inherent latency in real-time inference operations. To address these limitations, this paper proposes SARAD, a novel safety-aware hybrid framework that synergizes LLMs and DRL for autonomous driving. SARAD substitutes the random exploration of DRL with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG)-enhanced, LLM-guided decisions sourced from a dynamic expert knowledge repository. An attention discriminator is proposed to integrate the prior knowledge of LLMs into DRL policy optimization. A collision predictor module, fine-tuned with historical collision data, is further designed to improve vehicle safety. Extensive experiments show that SARAD achieves significant performance improvements in the Highway-Env simulator, validating the effectiveness of the proposed model in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [134] [PrimitiveVLA: Learning Reusable Motion Primitives for Efficient and Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28634)
*Yutai Li,Shaohui Peng,Jiaming Guo,Di Huang,Zihao Zhang,Yuxuan Guo,Yunkai Gao,Siming Lan,Ling Li,Xing Hu,Yunji Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrimitiveVLA 提出了一种以原语为中心的拆解与组装范式，通过多模态规范表示将演示拆解为可复用的运动原语，并利用 VLM 规划器和 LLM 生成的切换模块实现鲁棒闭环执行，显著提升了 VLA 模型的数据效率和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型采用直接的指令到控制映射，迫使模型记忆整体轨迹而非可复用的运动模式，导致数据效率低和泛化能力差。作者认为这是性能瓶颈的根本原因。

Method: 提出 PrimitiveVLA 框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 微调阶段拆解——通过自动化流水线将演示拆解为可复用原语，由共享的多模态规范表示(MCR)支撑；(2) 推理阶段组装——使用 VLM 规划器和 LLM 生成的切换模块进行鲁棒闭环执行。

Result: 实验表明，PrimitiveVLA 提升了数据效率，并在未见任务和长时域任务上实现了优越的零样本泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过将任务拆解为可复用的原语，VLA 模型能够学习不变的运动模式而非任务特定的轨迹，从而突破数据效率和泛化瓶颈。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising paradigm for generalist robotic policies, yet their adaptation is hindered by data inefficiency and poor generalization. We argue that these bottlenecks stem from the prevailing Direct Instruction-to-Control Mapping, which forces models to memorize monolithic trajectories rather than reusable motion patterns, i.e., primitives. We propose PrimitiveVLA, a framework that shifts this paradigm toward a Primitive-Centric Disassemble & Assemble paradigm. Supported by a shared Multimodal Canonical Representation (MCR), PrimitiveVLA unifies two phases: (1) Fine-tuning-phase Disassembly, which uses an automated pipeline to disassemble demonstrations into reusable primitives; and (2) Inference-phase Assembly, which employs a VLM-based planner and an LLM-generated switch module for robust closed-loop execution. By disassembling tasks into reusable primitives, PrimitiveVLA enables VLA models to learn invariant motion patterns instead of task-specific trajectories. Extensive experiments show that our framework improves data efficiency and achieves superior zero-shot generalization across unseen and long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [135] [Integrated Exploration-Aware UAV Route Optimization and Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28654)
*Jimin Choi,Grant Stagg,Cameron K. Peterson,Max Z. Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向不确定和动态先验信息的无人机探索感知路径规划框架，将灾害监测问题建模为风险地图上的车辆路径问题，通过B样条轨迹优化和在线重规划，在访问已知报告区域与探索未知信息区域之间分配有限的飞行续航，实验表明在线重规划相比离线优化和直线路径分别提升KL散度降低15.9%和48.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在危险环境（如灾区、污染区、野火区等）中执行探索驱动监测时，先验信息通常不完整、空间不精确且在任务执行过程中可能变化（例如初始报告仅指出现险情可能存在的区域，实际险情可能偏移、部分可见或完全未被报告），现有方法难以在此类不确定且演变的先验信息下有效分配有限续航。

Method: 将环境表示为空间风险图（每个位置有危险条件的信念值）；将报告的险情建模为不确定的兴趣区域（ROI）而非确认目标；求解ROI上的车辆路径问题，用辅助伪节点增强路径以改善空间覆盖；将剩余飞行距离预算分配到路径各段；优化B样条轨迹实现局部探索；执行时通过无人机测量更新栅格信念图，当新信息和剩余预算支持时进行在线重规划。

Result: 在48种场景配置下，在线重规划相比离线优化规划器平均KL散度降低提升15.9%，相比直线遍历提升48.6%。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效应对不确定和动态变化的先验信息，通过在线自适应重规划显著提升信息获取效率，在灾害监测等任务中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly used for exploration-driven monitoring in hazardous environments such as disaster zones, contaminated sites, wildfire areas, and damaged infrastructure, where limited flight endurance must be allocated between visiting reported locations and gathering new information. In these settings, prior information regarding hazards is often incomplete, spatially imprecise, and subject to change during execution. For example, initial reports may identify a region where a hazard is likely to exist, but the actual hazard may be displaced, partially observed, or entirely unreported. We present an integrated exploration-aware UAV route optimization and path planning framework for hazard monitoring under uncertain and evolving prior information. The environment is represented as a spatial risk map, where each location has an associated belief of hazardous conditions. Reported hazards are modeled as uncertain regions of interest (ROIs) rather than confirmed target locations, requiring the UAV to inspect reported areas while also using its limited flight endurance to explore informative regions. The proposed method solves a vehicle routing problem over reported ROIs, augments the route with auxiliary pseudo-nodes to improve spatial coverage, allocates the remaining flight distance budget across route segments, and optimizes dynamically feasible B-spline trajectories for local exploration. During execution, UAV measurements update a grid-based belief map, and the remaining trajectory is replanned when new information and the remaining budget justify adaptation. Across 48 scenario configurations, online replanning improves average KL reduction by 15.9% over the offline optimized planner and 48.6% over straight-line traversal.

</details>


### [136] [How VLAs Fail Differently: Black-Box Action Monitoring Reveals Architecture-Specific Failure Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28726)
*Krishnam Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现VLA架构在电机指令层面存在根本性且可预测的失败模式。通过对比VQ-BeT、Diffusion Policy和ACT三种架构在PushT和ALOHA 14-DOF双臂操作任务上的表现（共450个测试回合），得出以下结论：(1)方向反转率是跨架构的通用失败预测指标（AUROC最高达0.93）；(2)急动度（jerk）监测仅对离散token架构有效；(3)速度违规单独无预测能力，但却是VLA部署中最常见的安全机制；(4)对连续族VLA，速度监测几乎无预测信号。研究表明离散与连续VLA架构需要不同的监测策略，并通过SafeContract工具实现无训练黑箱动作监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的安全监测方法缺乏系统性评估，特别是缺乏对架构差异导致的不同失败模式的量化理解。常见的速度检查机制广泛使用但未经验证，亟需系统性的跨架构失败预测能力评估。

Method: 对VQ-BeT（离散token架构）、Diffusion Policy（连续扩散架构）和ACT（连续动作架构）三种代表性VLA架构，采用相同的评估协议（n=450个回合），在PushT和ALOHA 14-DOF双臂操作两个任务上，测试方向反转率、急动度监测和速度违规三个指标的失败预测能力，并使用AUROC和p值进行量化评估。

Result: (1)方向反转率是通用失败预测指标，对VQ-BeT、Diffusion Policy、ACT的AUROC分别为0.93、0.79、0.91（p<0.001）；(2)急动度监测对离散token架构有效（VQ-BeT的AUROC=0.88），对连续架构效果递减（Diffusion Policy的AUROC=0.69，ACT的AUROC=0.41）；(3)速度违规在所有架构上均非预测性（AUROC 0.41-0.69）；(4)对连续族VLA，速度监测几乎无预测信号（ACT AUROC=0.52，Diffusion AUROC=0.41）。

Conclusion: 离散和连续VLA架构产生性质不同的失败特征，需要不同监测策略。不存在通用的单一监测器，必须根据架构匹配选择监测指标。该发现由SafeContract工具实现，这是一个无需训练、黑箱的动作监测工具包，具有保形校准功能。

Abstract: We discover that VLA architectures fail in fundamentally different, predictable ways at the motor-command level. Running VQ-BeT, Diffusion Policy, and ACT on identical evaluation protocols (n=450 episodes across PushT and ALOHA 14-DOF bimanual manipulation), we find: (1) direction reversal rate is a universal failure predictor across all three architectures (AUROC=0.93, 0.79, 0.91; p<0.001); (2) jerk monitoring is predictive only for discrete-token architectures, following a discrete-to-continuous gradient (0.88, 0.69, 0.41); (3) velocity violations alone are non-predictive everywhere (AUROC 0.41-0.69), yet velocity checking is the most common safety mechanism in VLA deployment code; and (4) for continuous-family VLAs, velocity monitoring provides effectively zero predictive signal (AUROC=0.52 on ACT, 0.41 on Diffusion), proving that architecture-matched monitor selection is essential. These results quantify a monitoring consequence of the well-known discrete/continuous VLA distinction: the two families produce qualitatively different failure signatures that require different monitors. No single monitor works universally; architecture-matched selection is required. This finding was enabled by SafeContract, a training-free, black-box action monitoring toolkit with conformal calibration. Code: https://github.com/krishnam94/vla-edge

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Binary: Sim-to-Real Dexterous Manipulation with Physics-Grounded Contact Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28812)
*Jiahe Pan,Stelian Coros,Jitendra Malik,Toru Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理原理的触觉表示——压力中心（CoP），通过可微动力学标定方案估计触觉方向，在零样本仿真到现实迁移中优于二值接触和原始触觉基线，并能使策略在控制过程中涌现出对物体质量等物理属性的编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作的主要瓶颈是真实数据难以收集。仿真到现实的强化学习虽可扩展，但仿真-现实差距阻碍了触觉等高信息密度模态的有效使用。现有方法通常将触觉数据简化为粗粒度的低维特征，牺牲了复杂操作所需的丰富信息。

Method: 提出压力中心（CoP）触觉表示，基于物理原理保留密集接触信息且鲁棒于仿真到现实迁移；设计基于可微动力学的传感器标定方案，无需真实力测量即可估计触觉方向；将CoP作为策略条件进行零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Result: 在盲操作的两项挑战性任务（插销入孔和球平衡）上，基于CoP的策略在多指手上实现了零样本仿真到现实迁移，并在两项任务中均优于二值接触基线和原始触觉基线。

Conclusion: CoP是一种有效的触觉表示，能在保留密集接触信息的同时具备仿真到现实迁移的鲁棒性；分析表明CoP条件化策略能涌现编码任务相关的物理属性（如物体质量），作为控制的副产品。

Abstract: A primary bottleneck in contact-rich manipulation is the difficulty of collecting real-world data. Sim-to-real reinforcement learning offers a scalable alternative, but the simulation-reality gap prevents information-dense modalities like touch from being effectively used. Existing sim-to-real methods often mitigate this gap by simplifying tactile data into coarse low-dimensional features -- sacrificing the richness required for complex manipulation. In this work, we introduce Center-of-Pressure (CoP), an effective tactile representation grounded in physical principles that preserves dense contact information while maintaining robustness for sim-to-real transfer. To support this representation, we propose a sensor calibration scheme based on differentiable dynamics, enabling the estimation of taxel orientations without requiring ground-truth force measurements. We evaluate CoP on two blind, challenging contact-rich manipulation tasks: peg-in-hole insertion and ball balancing. Across both tasks, policies conditioned on CoP achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on a multi-fingered hand, and outperform both coarse binary-contact and raw-taxel baselines. Analysis of learned policy states further suggests that CoP-conditioned policies encode task-relevant physical properties, such as object mass, as an emergent byproduct of control.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Speed-Weighted Adaptive Flocking for Sailing Swarms under Dynamic Environmental Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27422)
*Pranav Kedia,Aaron Gan,Hannah J. Williams,Andreagiovanni Reina,Heiko Hamann*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对自主航行帆船机器人群体的运动特性（受风速限制的机动性和航向约束），提出了一种考虑速度权重的社交交互规则（Speed-Weighted Interaction Rule），以解决群体中因风力变化导致的快慢协调问题。引入简化仿真器SailSwarmSwIM进行验证，结果表明增加慢速机器人的社交影响力能提升群体极化度并减少近距离接触，从而实现更稳定的编队飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统群体行为模型（如Couzin模型）假设机器人能直接执行期望速度和方向，但自主帆船机器人受风速、风向、无风区等约束，机动性与风速耦合，导致群体内出现瞬态的速度和机动性异质性，引发快慢协调问题。

Method: 1. 开发了SailSwarmSwIM简化仿真器，模拟风速依赖的速度和机动性、无风区、转向行为及阵风场。2. 基于Couzin模型提出速度加权的社交交互规则，使每个机器人的相互作用权重受其当前运动能力影响。

Result: 增加慢速机器人的社交影响力能显著提高群体极化度，同时减少近距离接触（碰撞风险）。群体行为在“吸引快邻以保持运动”和“围绕慢邻以维持凝聚”之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: SailSwarmSWIM仿真器和速度加权交互规则为研究受风持续影响的机器人舰队自适应群体行为提供了建模框架。

Abstract: Collective behavior models, such as aggregation and flocking, usually assume self-propelled robots that can directly execute their desired speed and direction of motion without fundamental constraints. However, autonomous sailing robots violate this assumption. Their motion is shaped by wind-dependent propulsion, restricted headings, and spatially varying wind conditions. In particular, maneuverability is coupled to wind speed: in weak wind, sailboats may turn only slowly or not at all, whereas stronger wind enables faster turns. This introduces transient heterogeneity in speed and maneuverability across the flock. We focus on this fast-slow coordination problem in sailing robot flocks. To study this problem, we introduce SailSwarmSwIM, a reduced-order simulator for autonomous sailing robot swarms that captures wind-dependent speed and maneuverability, no-go zones, tacking behavior, and steady or gusty wind fields. To design our novel flocking technique, we start from the Couzin model and introduce a speed-weighted social interaction rule that accounts for each robot's transient motion constraints. A key result is that increasing the social influence of slower robots improves polarization and reduces close encounters. This effect arises from a balance between attraction to fast neighbors, which helps maintain movement, and cohesion around slow neighbors, which prevents the flock from fragmenting. Together, our simulator, SailSwarmSwIM, and the speed-weighted interaction rule provide a modeling framework for studying adaptive collective behavior in robotic fleets whose motion capabilities are continuously shaped by wind.

</details>


### [139] [AgensFlow: A Coordination-Policy Substrate for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27466)
*Nicole Koenigstein*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: AgensFlow是一个将多智能体协作视为在线策略学习问题的开源框架，通过在部分可观测性下学习路由决策（如技能、角色、模型、拓扑等），优于固定管线基线，在协调密集型任务上取得更高质量结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统存在大量难以预先固定的协调选择（技能协议、角色分配、模型绑定、交互拓扑、检索/验证时机等），这些选择与任务场景和操作约束相互影响，静态管线和一次性模型对比无法充分探索设计空间。

Method: 提出AgensFlow框架，将多智能体协调建模为部分可观测下的在线策略学习问题，使协调决策（技能、角色、模型、拓扑、评估）可观测且可从重复轨迹中学习，而非固定为静态管线设计。

Result: 在分布式系统事件任务和安全咨询任务两个语料上评估，三个主要结果：1）学习路由在协调密集型任务上达到比固定管线基线更高质量的操作点；2）skip:X将拓扑压缩隔离为基板的有意义部分；3）热启动策略图可在保持平台质量的同时减少探索成本。

Conclusion: 结果表明，可学习、可审计的路由能够改善协调密集型多智能体工作流，优于静态接线方式。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built on large language models (LLMs) require many coordination choices that are difficult to fix a priori: which skill protocol to invoke, which agent role should perform a subtask, which model to bind to each role, how roles should interact, when to use retrieval or verification, and when to omit a step entirely. These choices interact with task regime and operational constraints, so static pipelines and one-off model comparisons provide only a limited view of the design space. This paper introduces AgensFlow, an open-source framework that treats multi-agent coordination as an online policy-learning problem under partial observability. The framework makes coordination decisions observable and learnable from repeated trajectories, rather than treating skill, role, model, topology, and evaluation choices as fixed pipeline design.
  AgensFlow is evaluated on two corpora: distributed-systems incident tasks and security-advisory tasks. The evaluation shows three main results: learned routing reaches a higher-quality operating point than a fixed pipeline baseline on coordination-heavy classes; skip:X isolates topology compression as a meaningful part of the substrate; and warm-started policy graphs can reduce exploration cost while preserving plateau quality. Overall, the results support that learned, auditable routing can improve coordination-heavy multi-agent workflows over static wiring.

</details>


### [140] [From Task Allocation to Risk Clearing: A Unifying Interface for Mixed Human-Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27547)
*Vassilis Vassiliades*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出风险感知选项清算（ROC）机制，通过将带风险摘要的选项作为协调单元，在中央清算层优化风险调整后的任务效用，为异构智能体（包括人类）提供可扩展、透明的协调基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协调框架要么假设刚性静态团队，要么学习难以解释和集成的黑盒联合策略，无法适应人类-机器人-软件智能体共享的安全关键环境。

Method: ROC机制：智能体暴露带风险摘要（预测结果分布）的选项（时间扩展技能），中央清算所在截止时间和安全约束下优化风险调整后的任务效用进行分配；支持从数据学习到完全分布预测的多种部署方式。

Result: 提出ROC作为统一协调机制家族，可处理不确定承诺，支持异构智能体集成，并勾勒出风险感知清算层的研究路线图。

Conclusion: ROC通过将风险感知选项作为基本协调单元，为未来混合人机社会提供了可扩展、透明的协调基础设施，但当前仅为框架性提案，需进一步实证验证。

Abstract: As humans, robots, and software agents increasingly share safety-critical environments, coordination must move from static task allocation to managing uncertain commitments. Existing frameworks fall short: they either assume rigid, static teams or learn opaque joint policies that are hard to adapt and difficult to integrate with human decision-makers. To overcome these limitations, we propose Risk-Aware Option Clearing (ROC), a unifying coordination mechanism in which agents expose options (temporally extended skills) paired with risk summaries that predict outcome distributions. A central clearinghouse then assigns tasks by optimizing risk-adjusted mission utility under deadlines and safety constraints. ROC is a family of mechanisms, ranging from deployments where the clearinghouse learns outcome models from data to ones that consume full distributional predictions from agents. By treating risk-aware options as the basic coordination unit, ROC sketches a scalable, transparent infrastructure for integrating heterogeneous agents into future mixed human--agent societies and outlines a research agenda for such risk-aware clearing layers.

</details>


### [141] [Long Live the Librarian! A Persistent Search Sub-Agent for Energy-Efficient Multi-Agent Software Engineering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27787)
*Seunghyuk Cho,Sunghyun Choi,Jaeseung Heo,Youngbin Choi,Saemi Moon,MoonJeong Park,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文发现多智能体系统的能源消耗主要集中在被忽视的输出token上（比输入token能耗高30-1000倍），并提出Librarian——一个持久化搜索子智能体，通过跟踪搜索历史和返回短引用而非完整文件摘录来减少冗余输出token，在SWE-Bench Verified上实现了最高25%的GPU能耗降低且不损害任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在自主软件工程中取得了显著进展，但其推理过程中的能源消耗日益增长，引发了可持续性担忧。现有工作忽视了一个关键点：跨智能体生成的冗余输出token是能源消耗的主要来源。

Method: 首先通过逐token能量归因揭示输出token与输入/缓存token之间的巨大能耗不对称性（30-1000倍），并实证发现多智能体系统中智能体会重复探索重叠的仓库区域导致输出膨胀。据此提出Librarian，一个持久化搜索子智能体，维护仓库搜索历史以抑制跨智能体的冗余探索，并通过返回文件区域的短引用替代完整文件摘录来减少输出token量。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Verified基准上，Librarian将现有基于多智能体的软件工程系统的每轮GPU能耗降低高达25%，同时保持任务性能不变。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统的能源效率可以通过针对性减少冗余输出token来显著提升，Librarian提供了一种轻量级、即插即用的解决方案，在不牺牲性能的前提下实现可持续的自主软件工程。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) have substantially advanced autonomous software engineering (SWE), but their growing inference energy demands raise sustainability concerns. In this paper, we demonstrate that this cost is concentrated in an overlooked source: redundant output tokens generated across agents. Two empirical findings ground this claim. First, our per-token energy attribution for MAS reveals a sharp asymmetry: an output token consumes 30 to 1,000 times more energy than an input or cached token. Second, MAS inflate per-episode output because agents repeatedly re-explore overlapping repository regions. To address this inefficiency, we propose Librarian, a persistent search sub-agent that tracks repository-search history and suppresses redundant exploration actions across agents. By returning short references to file regions instead of full file excerpts, Librarian further reduces output-token volume. On SWE-Bench Verified, Librarian reduces per-episode GPU energy consumption of existing multi-agent SWE systems by up to 25% while preserving task performance.

</details>


### [142] [Differentiable Model Predictive Safety for Heterogeneous Mobility at Urban Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27418)
*Wenzhe Song,Hao Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出可微分模型预测安全（DMPS）框架，将模型预测控制的预见性嵌入端到端强化学习架构中，通过学习潜在动力学模型和可微分安全评论家，利用反向传播计算未来安全相对于当前动作的梯度，实现精确的在线安全修正，在密集混合交通仿真中将碰撞率降至5.6%以下，达到最先进的安全水平且不牺牲能效和通行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自动驾驶车辆与移动机器人在未管制交叉口异构协同控制的复杂安全问题，应对未来智能交通系统中异构智能体动态差异大、缺乏集中式信号控制的挑战。

Method: 提出DMPS框架：1）学习潜在动力学模型预测未来轨迹；2）训练可微分的安全评论家评估轨迹风险；3）通过整个展开预测模型的反向传播计算未来安全关于当前动作的梯度，实现最小精确的在线安全修正；4）集成到多智能体训练方案中。

Result: 在高密度混合车辆-机器人交通仿真中，碰撞率降至5.6%以下，在安全性、能效和交通效率方面均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: DMPS通过将模型预测控制的预见性与强化学习的数据驱动能力结合，有效解决了异构智能体在无管制交叉口的协调安全问题，展示了安全性与效率可兼得的潜力。

Abstract: The imminent integration of autonomous vehicles and mobile robots in urban settings presents a critical safety challenge for future intelligent transportation systems. This paper addresses the complex problem of coordinating heterogeneous agents with disparate dynamics at unregulated intersections. We introduce a novel framework, differentiable model predictive safety (DMPS), which embeds the foresight of model-predictive control into a data-driven, end-to-end reinforcement learning architecture. DMPS agents learn a latent dynamics model to predict future trajectories contingent on their actions. A learned, differentiable safety critic then evaluates the risk of these trajectories. Crucially, by leveraging backpropagation through the entire unrolled predictive model, agents can efficiently compute the gradient of future safety with respect to their current action, enabling a minimal and precise online safety correction. Integrated into a multi-agent training scheme, DMPS virtually eliminates collisions to less than 5.6% in high-density, mixed vehicle-robot traffic simulations, demonstrating state-of-the-art safety without compromising energy and traffic efficiency.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [The Alignment Floor: When Persona Customization Is Safe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27382)
*Xing Zhang,Guanghui Wang,Yanwei Cui,Wei Qiu,Ziyuan Li,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了AI模型的对齐-定制化权衡，提出了"对齐地板"概念：强对齐模型（Claude Sonnet）上人格提示对谄媚行为几乎无影响（~15%），而弱对齐模型（Nova Lite）上同一组提示可将谄媚率从5%推升至50%。研究发现外向性和开放性比宜人性更危险，而"怀疑论者"人格能将谄媚降至5%。跨模型人格效应几乎为零相关（ρ=0.006），表明对齐测试必须针对每个模型单独进行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元AI的核心承诺是行为自适应——通过人格提示（如"要创新"或"要全面"）尊重用户多样化的价值观和沟通风格。然而，模型在多大程度上可以吸收这种定制化而不破坏其对齐（alignment）尚不清楚。作者旨在量化这一对齐-定制化权衡。

Method: 在两种不同对齐强度的模型（Claude Sonnet强对齐，Nova Lite弱对齐）上，对五项任务测试七种人格条件，共计1,800次运行。通过测量各条件下模型的谄媚（sycophancy）水平，评估人格提示对对齐的影响。

Result: 1) 强对齐模型上所有人格条件的谄媚率稳定在~15%，即存在"对齐地板"；2) 弱对齐模型上人格提示使谄媚率从5%变化到50%，无对齐地板；3) 外向性（+20pp）和开放性（+15pp）造成的退化比宜人性更严重；4) "怀疑论者"人格能将弱对齐模型的谄媚降至5%；5) 跨模型人格效应相关性近乎为零（ρ=0.006）。

Conclusion: 提出"对齐地板"作为设计原则：在部署人格定制化之前应测量模型的对齐地板，并在面向用户的人格层之下叠加面向安全的人格层，从而在不牺牲对齐的前提下实现个性化。

Abstract: A key promise of pluralistic AI is behavioral adaptation: persona prompts like "be creative" or "be thorough" let systems respect diverse user values and communication styles. But how much customization can a model absorb before its alignment breaks? We present the first controlled study of the alignment-customization tradeoff, testing seven persona conditions across five tasks on two models with different alignment strengths (1,800 runs). We discover the alignment floor: on a strongly-aligned model (Claude Sonnet), persona prompts have zero effect on sycophancy -- all conditions produce ~15%, a stable platform on which rich personalization is safe. On a weakly-aligned model (Nova Lite), the same personas shift sycophancy from 5% to 50% -- the floor is absent and customization becomes a safety liability. Surprisingly, Agreeableness is not the worst offender; Extraversion (+20pp) and Openness (+15pp) cause greater degradation. The constructive finding is the Skeptic defense: a critical-thinking persona reduces sycophancy to 5% even on the weak model -- the single largest effect in the study. Cross-model transfer of persona effects is near-zero ($ρ= 0.006$), meaning alignment testing must be per-model. We propose the alignment floor as a design principle: measure it before deploying persona customization, and layer safety-oriented personas underneath user-facing ones to enable personalization without compromising alignment.

</details>


### [144] [What Catches the Eye? A Conjoint Study of Infographic Design Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27554)
*Amit Kumar Das,Karanbir Pelia,Manav Nitesh Ukani,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过联合分析（N=65）研究信息图设计因素（比较类型、颜色、图形类型）对读者偏好的相对重要性，发现比较类型（58.5%）最重要，其次是图形类型（29.2%），颜色（12.3%）无实际影响。读者偏爱百分比刻度标记和基准比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究孤立地考察信息图设计因素，缺乏对读者如何在多个设计维度间权衡的量化理解。

Method: 采用基于选择的联合分析，65名参与者在一份模拟报纸页面上查看关于失业率的信息图配对，每个信息图在比较类型、颜色和图形类型三个属性上变化。

Result: 比较类型驱动了58.5%的偏好差异，图形类型占29.2%，颜色占12.3%；读者更偏爱百分比刻度标记和基准比较，颜色无实际效果。

Conclusion: 联合分析是研究可视化设计偏好的实用但未被充分利用的工具；研究存在主题单一和调色板狭窄等外部效度限制，比较类型这一维度混合了两种不同概念。

Abstract: Infographic designers balance many choices at once: chart type, color, and whether to add a benchmark or a scale. Past work studies these factors one at a time, so we know little about how readers weigh them against each other. We address this gap with a choice-based conjoint study (N = 65) in which participants viewed pairs of infographics on a mock newspaper page about unemployment. Each infographic varied across three attributes: comparison type (none, US average, percentage scale), color (red, blue), and graphic type (single icon, icon series, bar chart). Comparison type drove most of the preference variation (58.5%), followed by graphic type (29.2%) and color (12.3%). Readers favored percentage scale markers and benchmark comparisons; color had no practical effect. The percentage scale level adds axis information rather than a benchmark, so the comparison type result mixes two distinct ideas. A single topic and a narrow palette also limit external validity. We argue that conjoint analysis is a practical and underused tool for studying visualization preferences across many design dimensions.

</details>


### [145] [SmartIterator: Visual Analytics Workflows for Supervising Unsupervised Data Grouping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28219)
*Gennady Andrienko,Natalia Andrienko*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出SmartIterator (SI) 和 IteraScope (IS)，一种通过结构化六阶段工作流（质量概览→过渡稳定性→成员置信度→内容与上下文检查→循环原型验证→决策）对参数扫描下的无监督学习分组结果进行系统可视分析的方法，涵盖密度聚类、划分聚类和主题模型三类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督学习的分组结果缺乏人工监督，选择与评估这些分组不应是无监督的。现有方法缺乏对完整参数空间下分组结果序列的系统性探索工具，无法理解数据结构如何随配置变化而演变。

Method: 针对每类方法家族设计六阶段结构化工作流，通过IteraScope协调可视化展示：质量指标图、语义颜色编码、一维组嵌入+Sankey过渡流+小提琴图、二维组嵌入+HDBSCAN循环原型检测、领域特定关联视图。

Result: 在三个案例中验证：(1) VAST Challenge 2011模拟社交媒体消息（密度聚类，有真实标签验证），(2) EU约1500个NUTS-3区域人口统计数据（划分聚类），(3) 30年IEEE VIS论文（NMF主题模型）。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了可操作的、方法特定的参数空间导航指南，帮助理解数据结构如何随配置演变，并基于领域上下文建立分析理解，获得单一"最佳"结果无法提供的关于数据结构的深层知识。

Abstract: Unsupervised learning methods -- topic modeling, partition-based and density-based clustering -- produce data groupings without human guidance, yet choosing and evaluating those groupings should not itself be unsupervised. We present \emph{SmartIterator}~(SI), a visual analytics approach that treats the full sequence of grouping results across a parameter sweep as a first-class analytical object. For each method family, SI provides a structured six-phase workflow that guides the analyst through systematic exploration of grouping results -- from quality-metric overview through transition-stability assessment, membership-confidence evaluation, content and context inspection, and recurrent-archetype verification to an informed decision -- building cumulative understanding of data structure along the way. The workflows are operationalized through \emph{IteraScope}~(IS), a coordinated visual display combining quality-metric charts with semantic color encoding, a 1D group embedding with Sankey-style transition flows and violin plots of membership confidence, a 2D group embedding with HDBSCAN-detected recurrent archetypes that highlights iterations capturing all persistent patterns, and domain-specific linked views for contextualized interpretation. We demonstrate the three workflows on: (1)~simulated social-media messages from the VAST Challenge 2011 (density-based clustering, validated against ground truth), (2)~EU population statistics across ${\sim}1\,500$ NUTS-3 regions (partition-based clustering), and (3)~30 years of IEEE VIS papers (NMF topic modeling). The workflows constitute the main contribution: they provide actionable, method-specific guidance for navigating parameter spaces, studying how data structure evolves across configurations, and grounding analytical understanding in domain context -- yielding knowledge about the data that no single ``best'' result can provide.

</details>


### [146] [Not All Uncertainty Is Equal: How Uncertainty Granularity Shapes Human Verification in LLM-Assisted Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28571)
*Mauricio Villavicencio,Sitong Pan,Qianwen Wang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模用户实验（N=192）探究了LLM不确定性量化的粒度（输出级、关系级、词元级）对用户决策行为的影响，发现词元级不确定性增加了用户对AI的认同，而输出级和关系级降低了用户自信，且关系级减少了用户的外部验证行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的不确定性沟通研究将不确定性简化为单一数值或简单自然语言表达，忽略了LLM输出中多个主张和推理步骤具有不同不确定性水平这一关键特性。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究不确定性粒度对LLM辅助决策的影响。

Method: 开展大规模组间实验（N=192），让参与者使用以三种不同粒度（输出级、关系级、词元级）显示不确定性的LLM回答医学问题，比较不同粒度下用户行为差异。

Result: 词元级不确定性增加了用户对AI的认同；输出级和关系级不确定性未增加认同，但降低了用户对自己答案的信心；关系级不确定性还减少了用户的外部验证行为（如互联网搜索、检查URL）。

Conclusion: 不确定性粒度显著塑造了用户与LLM输出的互动和验证方式，为构建鼓励适当怀疑和验证行为的负责任LLM应用提供了具体设计指导。

Abstract: Despite warnings that LLMs can make mistakes, users often develop inappropriate trust and accept incorrect answers without critical evaluation. Uncertainty quantification (UQ), displaying LLMs' confidence, has emerged as a promising approach to calibrate user trust. However, prior empirical studies on uncertainty communication have treated uncertainty as a single numerical score or simple natural language expression. This simplification fails to capture a key property of LLM outputs: a single response often comprises multiple claims and reasoning steps, each with distinct levels of uncertainty. To address this gap, this study investigates uncertainty granularity (i.e., the extent to which uncertainty is expressed at different levels within an LLM response) and examines its impact on LLM-assisted decision-making. We conducted a large-scale, between-subjects study (N=192) in which participants answered medical questions using LLMs that displayed uncertainty at three different granularities: output-level (entire response), relation-level (individual reasoning steps), and token-level (specific words). Our findings reveal distinct behavioral effects as a function of uncertainty granularity. Token-level uncertainty increased users' agreement with the AI, whereas output- and relation-level uncertainty did not increase agreement but instead reduced users' confidence in their own answers. Notably, relation-level uncertainty also reduced external verification (i.e., internet searches, checking provided URLs), steering users away from independent fact-checking and toward reliance on the LLM and its accompanying uncertainty cues. Our findings demonstrate that uncertainty granularity significantly shapes how users interact with and verify LLM outputs, providing concrete design guidance for building responsible LLM applications that encourage appropriate skepticism and verification behaviors.

</details>


### [147] [Surprising Performances of Students with Autism in Classroom with NAO Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.12014)
*Qin Yang,Huan Lu,Dandan Liang,Shengrong Gong,Huanghao Feng*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了NAO机器人辅助特殊教育教师在集体课堂中对自闭症谱系障碍(ASD)学生的教学效果。实验表明，配备NAO机器人的课堂中，ASD学生表现出更好的专注力、课堂参与度，减少了刻板重复行为和无关小动作，有望提升教育表现和社交能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究表明社交机器人在ASD行为干预中有效，但将其融入集体课堂环境的研究仍然不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索NAO机器人在特殊教育学校集体课堂中的实际应用效果。

Method: 在特殊教育学校中设计并实施了一项由NAO机器人介导的集体课堂分组实验。特殊教育教师与NAO机器人协作开展课堂活动，通过教师-机器人-学生三方互动营造动态学习环境。

Result: 配备NAO机器人的课堂中，ASD学生表现明显优于普通课堂。NAO机器人的类人特征和肢体语言吸引了学生注意力，尤其在才艺展示和指令任务中，学生参与度提高，刻板重复行为和无关小动作减少。

Conclusion: 初步发现表明NAO机器人能显著提升ASD学生的专注力和课堂参与度，有望改善教育表现并促进更好的社交行为。

Abstract: Autism is a developmental disorder that manifests in early childhood and persists throughout life, profoundly affecting social behavior and hindering the acquisition of learning and social skills in those diagnosed. As technological advancements progress, an increasing array of technologies is being utilized to support the education of students with Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD), aiming to improve their educational outcomes and social capabilities. Numerous studies on autism intervention have highlighted the effectiveness of social robots in behavioral treatments. However, research on the integration of social robots into classroom settings for children with autism remains sparse. This paper describes the design and implementation of a group experiment in a collective classroom setting mediated by the NAO robot. The experiment involved special education teachers and the NAO robot collaboratively conducting classroom activities, aiming to foster a dynamic learning environment through interactions among teachers, the robot, and students. Conducted in a special education school, this experiment served as a foundational study in anticipation of extended robot-assisted classroom sessions. Data from the experiment suggest that ASD students in classrooms equipped with the NAO robot exhibited notably better performance compared to those in regular classrooms. The humanoid features and body language of the NAO robot captivated the students' attention, particularly during talent shows and command tasks, where students demonstrated heightened engagement and a decrease in stereotypical repetitive behaviors and irrelevant minor movements commonly observed in regular settings. Our preliminary findings indicate that the NAO robot significantly enhances focus and classroom engagement among students with ASD, potentially improving educational performance and fostering better social behaviors.

</details>


### [148] [Robo-Blocks: Generative Scaffolding in End-User Design and Programming of Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28154)
*Arissa J. Sato,Callie Y. Kim,Nathan Thomas White,Abhinav Maneesh,Yuqing Wang,Hui-Ru Ho,Bilge Mutlu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过研究型设计（RtD）流程，设计了Robo-Blocks——一种基于积木式编程环境，利用大语言模型（LLMs）为新手机器人程序员提供生成式支架（structured narratives），将高层想法转化为可执行的社交机器人行为。通过部署实验，发现新手用户的使用角色和模式，并展示了生成式支架如何塑造终端用户的设计与编程策略，最后提出有效整合生成式支架到社交机器人编程实践中的设计洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新手程序员在社交机器人编程中面临规划、交互设计和编程等多方面专业知识的挑战。虽然LLMs可通过自然语言生成代码，但会掩盖编程关键要素并取代设计者意图，导致过度依赖而非培养编程技能。

Method: 采用研究型设计（Research through Design, RtD）流程，设计并原型化了Robo-Blocks积木式编程环境，利用LLMs提供生成式支架（structured narratives），连接高层想法与可执行机器人行为。并通过部署实验收集新手用户的使用反馈。

Result: 发现了新手用户的不同角色（personas）和使用模式；展示了生成式支架如何影响终端用户的设计与编程策略。

Conclusion: 提出了在社交机器人编程中有效使用生成式支架的设计洞见，并为其整合到编程实践中提供了指导。

Abstract: Programming social robots is challenging for novice robot programmers due to required expertise in planning, interaction design, and programming. While large language models (LLMs) hold significant promise through code generation from natural-language descriptions, they can obscure critical elements of programming and supplant designer intent, eventually resulting in over-reliance instead of developing programming skills. In this paper, we explore how LLM-based social-robot-programming tools can support novice robot programmers through a Research through Design (RtD) process. We designed and prototyped Robo-Blocks, a block-based programming environment that leverages LLMs to offer novice robot programmers generative scaffolding through structured narratives that connect high-level ideas to executable robot behaviors. Through deployment with novices, we discovered emerging user personas and usage patterns for generative scaffolding and showed how this scaffolding shapes end-user design and programming strategies. We present design insights for the effective use of generative scaffolding and its integration into the practice of social-robot programming.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Grimlock: Guarding High-Agency Systems with eBPF and Attested Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27488)
*Qiancheng Wu,Wenhui Zhang,Gan Fang,Sheng Mao,Biao Gao,David Levitsky,Shawna Murphy Butterworth,Rob Cameron*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Grimlock 是一个 Agent Guard 系统，通过 eBPF 流量拦截和 TLS 1.3 信道绑定，在不修改用户编排代码的前提下，在沙箱层面实施身份验证、授权和最小权限委托，实现跨多云环境的安全 agent 通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 agent 系统将身份、授权、溯源和委托等安全机制推入应用层代码，导致安全策略难以一致执行且难以审计，缺乏关注点分离。

Method: 采用 eBPF 强制流量拦截确保沙箱通信经过守卫；结合 TLS 1.3 信道绑定的握手后认证；守卫授权后生成短生命周期的信道绑定 scope token 实现最小权限委托；接收端守卫重新验证身份、范围和信道绑定，策略检查通过后才释放明文；使用 kTLS 提供高效数据面。

Result: 实现了透明、可审计、范围绑定的 agent 间通信，无需修改用户层编排代码，基于通用 Linux 原语，支持异构多云环境。

Conclusion: Grimlock 为在异构多云环境中实现安全的 agent 通信提供了一条可行路径，通过在沙箱层面而非应用层面实施信任机制，恢复了关注点分离。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly run user-authored orchestration code that invokes tools, spawns subtasks, and delegates work across machines and clouds. Although this high agency is productive, it creates a security problem: identity, authorization, provenance, and delegation are often pushed into application code, where they become difficult to enforce consistently and difficult to audit.
  We present \emph{Grimlock}, an \emph{Agent Guard} that restores separation of concerns by moving trust enforcement into the sandbox substrate while leaving agent code unchanged. Grimlock uses \emph{eBPF-enforced traffic interception} to ensure that sandbox communication passes through a guard, and combines it with \emph{post-handshake attestation} bound to standard TLS~1.3 channel bindings. After a channel is established, the guard authorizes communication and mints short-lived, channel-bound \emph{scope tokens} that capture least-privilege delegation. At the receiving side, the destination guard re-validates identity, scope, and channel binding, terminates TLS, and releases plaintext to the destination sandbox only after policy checks succeed. kTLS provides an efficient dataplane for protected communication.
  As a result, Grimlock offers a path toward transparent, auditable, and scope-bound agent-to-agent communication across heterogeneous multi-cloud environments, using commodity Linux primitives and without requiring changes to user-layer orchestration code.

</details>


### [150] [HARP: Measuring Harm Amplification in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27489)
*Md Hafizur Rahman,Zafaryab Haider,Tanzim Mahfuz,Prabuddha Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出HARP方法，通过轨迹追踪研究多智能体LLM系统中的局部到全局危害放大效应，并在金融七智能体系统中验证了五种防御策略的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统的模块化架构虽提高了可解释性，但也引入了危害传播风险——单组件的有限扰动可被其他智能体复用并放大为系统性危害，而现有评估方法未关注这种传播效应。
